### [1] [The World Won't Wait: Evaluating and Teaching Agents Under a Live Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20665)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yeqing Shen,Ning Ding,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出实时基准与两时间尺度自我改进方法，研究agent在持续变化环境下的学习与行动，可迁移至长程agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出 RealtimeBench 评估推理期间游戏持续运行时的 LLM 智能体，并用 TORSI 双时间尺度离线递归自我改进提升实时表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体虽能处理复杂交互任务，但推理与工具调用耗时，在持续变化环境中会导致正确动作过晚；需要评估并解决实时性。

Method: 构建含六类游戏、180 个任务的 RealtimeBench，在游戏持续运行时评估；提出 TORSI：慢速教师在暂停游戏里分析失败、测试纠正并编写可复用指令，快速学生游戏时无需教师调用，依验证分数采纳更新。

Result: 配对评估中 GPT-6.1-sol 在暂停游戏时优于 Jev，持续运行时落后；两轮 TORSI 使 Jev 平均分从 13.51 升至 24.19（0--100），六类游戏均提升；指令无需重调即提升另外三个模型平均分。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通过跨时间尺度组织思考、学习与行动，实现实时智能，使强大 LLM 智能体在持续变化世界中保持有效。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体能够解决复杂的交互式任务，但推理和工具调用需要时间。在持续变化的环境中，原本正确的动作可能到达得太晚。我们提出 RealtimeBench，包含跨六种游戏类型的 180 个任务，用于在游戏于推理期间继续运行时评估智能体。配对评估显示，当游戏暂停时，GPT-6.1-sol 优于更快的 Jev，但当游戏持续运行时则落后。我们提出双时间尺度离线递归自我改进（TORSI）：一个慢速教师在两次尝试之间研究失败并在暂停的游戏中测试纠正，然后为快速学生编写可复用指令。学生在游戏过程中行动，无需调用教师，验证分数决定采用哪些更新。两轮将 Jev 的平均测试分数从 13.51 提升到 24.19（0--100 进度量表），改善了所有六种游戏类型。这些指令还在无需重新调参的情况下提升了另外三个模型的平均分数。这些发现指向实时智能：通过跨时间尺度组织审慎思考、学习和行动，使强大的 LLM 智能体在持续变化的世界中保持有效。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can solve complex interactive tasks, yet reasoning and tool calls take time. In continuously changing environments, an otherwise correct action may arrive too late. We introduce RealtimeBench, with 180 tasks across six game types, to evaluate agents while games continue running during inference. Paired evaluations show that GPT-6.1-sol outperforms the faster Jev when games are paused, but falls behind when they keep running. We propose Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI): a slow teacher studies failures and tests corrections in paused games between attempts, then writes reusable instructions for a fast student. The student acts without teacher calls during gameplay, and validation scores determine which updates are adopted. Two rounds raise Jev's average test score from 13.51 to 24.19 on a 0--100 progress scale, improving all six game types. The instructions also improve average scores for three other models without retuning. These findings point toward real-time intelligence: enabling powerful LLM agents to remain effective in a continuously changing world by organizing deliberation, learning, and action across timescales.

</details>


### [2] [AlphaDiana: A Measurement System for Agents in Verifiable Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20675)
*Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Weikai Huang,Xuan Li,Chentao Cao,Xiao Feng,Xiangyu Lu,Jinbo Hu,Yi Xie,Nico Pelleriti,Shiyang Liu,Menghan Lu,Max Zimmer,Brando Miranda,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Liu,Sanmi Koyejo,Sebastian Pokutta,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向agent的测量系统，可分别控制harness、工具、技能与记忆，直接支持agent组件评估。

TL;DR: AlphaDiana 是一个模块化智能体测量系统，用于在匹配条件下关联执行条件、行为、资源使用与正确性，并发现 harness 和组件干预并不稳定提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解智能体性能不能只看最终答案，还需将执行条件与结果、行为和资源使用联系起来；同时需要区分 harness、工具、技能、记忆等因素的独立影响。

Method: 提出 AlphaDiana，为数据集、智能体、环境和评分器提供模块化接口，保留 harness 原生控制循环，并分别控制文件复用与记忆保留；支持完整智能体的宏观分析及组件级微观干预。实验覆盖三个开源模型、三个开源 harness、五个基于答案的基准，其中两个模型另在两个交互式基准上测试。

Result: harness 并不一致提升推理准确率：对模型单独无法解决问题的增益，可能被无需工具即可正确回答问题的损失抵消；技能指导与保留历史也不能可靠提升准确率。行为与输出 token 使用必须结合正确性评估：低 token 熵可伴随错误答案，准确率下降时每次执行输出更少可能对应每个正确答案更多输出 token。

Conclusion: AlphaDiana 将答案正确性和执行状态与轨迹及资源记录关联，支持在共享任务与资源策略下比较智能体并诊断失败。

中文摘要：智能体将推理模型与 harness 配对，harness 负责组织模型调用、执行工具、加载技能指令，并在内存中保留历史。理解智能体性能需要将执行条件与结果、行为和资源使用联系起来。我们提出 AlphaDiana，一个具有面向数据集、智能体、环境和评分器的模块化接口的测量系统。它保留 harness 原生的控制循环，同时分别控制文件复用和记忆保留。AlphaDiana 支持对完整智能体进行宏观分析，以及在匹配条件下对智能体组件（如工具、技能和记忆）进行微观干预。我们在五个基于答案的基准上研究三个开源模型和三个开源 harness，其中两个模型还在两个交互式基准上进行了测试。我们发现，harness 并不能一致地提高推理准确率：在模型单独无法解决的问题上获得的增益，可能会被它在无需工具即可正确回答的问题上的损失所抵消。这种不一致性延伸到组件干预：技能指导或保留历史都不能可靠地提高准确率。行为和输出 token 使用必须与正确性一起评估：低 token 熵可能伴随错误答案，而当准确率下降时，每次执行输出更少可能意味着每个正确答案消耗更多输出 token。AlphaDiana 将答案正确性和执行状态与轨迹和资源记录关联起来，以便在共享任务和资源策略下比较智能体并诊断失败。

Original abstract: Agents pair reasoning models with harnesses that organize model calls, execute tools, load skill instructions, and retain history in memory. Understanding agent performance requires linking execution conditions to outcomes, behavior, and resource use. We introduce AlphaDiana, a measurement system with modular interfaces for datasets, agents, environments, and scorers. It preserves harness-native control loops while controlling file reuse and memory retention separately. AlphaDiana supports macro analyses of complete agents and micro interventions on agent components such as tools, skills, and memory under matched conditions. We study three open-source models and three open-source harnesses on five answer-based benchmarks, with two models also tested on two interactive benchmarks. We find that harnesses do not consistently improve the accuracy of reasoning: gains on questions unsolved by the model alone can be outweighed by losses on questions it answers correctly without tools. This inconsistency extends to component interventions: neither skill guidance nor retained history reliably improves accuracy. Behavior and output-token use must be assessed with correctness: low token entropy can accompany wrong answers, and less output per execution can mean more output tokens per correct answer when accuracy falls. AlphaDiana links answer correctness and execution status to traces and resource records for comparing agents and diagnosing failures under shared task and resource policies.

</details>


### [3] [When Correctness Feedback Erases Evidence: Phase-Separated Completeness Auditing in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20676)
*Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Paper accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AI for Verifiable Coding

Selection reason: 研究Verus规范补全审计中反馈循环的反效果，对编码agent的验证与反馈设计有启发。

TL;DR: 在 Verus/Rust 任务中，正确性反馈会抑制规约完整性审计所需的失败证据；独立无反馈发现产出更高，保留并认证见证后规约修复效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规约完整性审计需要发现满足书面契约但违反预期行为的程序，而正确性反馈可能抑制这类证据；作者检验这一目标反转及其对规约修复的影响。

Method: 在 12 个 Verus/Rust 任务、四个模型家族、匹配调用预算下，对比独立无反馈发现与 IntentShield 纠正性反馈循环，并设置格式匹配迭代对照、分阶段执行、解析器修复，以及在 Dafny/Lean 上检验复现性。

Result: 无反馈发现的认证见证产出率为 59.0%，高于纠正性反馈循环的 27.1%，差距主要来自反馈内容而非独立重启；优势在所有模型家族成立且经解析器修复仍存在。分阶段执行在不重用响应的情况下匹配总体产出率；保留见证后 Phase-Revised 实现成功率为 81.3%，IS-Revised 为 62.5%，Direct-Revised 紧随其后；两个无反馈来源均优于弱化契约。Dafny/Lean 中未复现该效应。

Conclusion: 完整性审计与正确性修复的直接目标相冲突；应先在修复前保留并认证已承认的失败，以提升规约修复效果，且该结论受 Verus/Rust 工作流经验边界限制。

中文摘要：正确性反馈恰恰抑制了规约完整性审计旨在揭示的证据：满足书面契约但违反预期行为的程序。我们在 12 个 Verus/Rust 任务、四个模型家族上，在匹配调用预算下证明了这一目标反转。独立的无反馈发现相对于 IntentShield 的纠正性反馈循环，使认证见证产出率提高一倍以上（59.0% 对 27.1%）。一个格式匹配的迭代对照将这一差距的较大份额归因于反馈内容，而不是独立重启。该优势在每个模型家族中都成立，并且在解析器修复后仍然存在。一个单独的分阶段执行在不重用响应的情况下匹配了总体产出率；大多数认证见证也在留出案例上失败。保留这些见证可带来更强的规约修复：Phase-Revised 契约达到 81.3% 的实现成功率，而 IS-Revised 为 62.5%，Direct-Revised 紧随其后。两个无反馈见证来源都显著优于弱化契约。该效应在可用的 Dafny 和 Lean 评估中未能复现，从而围绕所研究的 Verus/Rust 工作流建立了一个经验边界。完整性审计与正确性修复具有对立的直接目标。在修复之前，先保留并认证已承认的失败。

Original abstract: Correctness feedback suppresses the very evidence that specification-completeness auditing is designed to uncover: programs that satisfy a written contract but violate intended behavior. We demonstrate this objective reversal on 12 Verus/Rust tasks across four model families under matched call budgets. Independent no-feedback discovery more than doubles certified-witness yield relative to IntentShield's corrective-feedback loop (59.0% versus 27.1%). A format-matched iterative control places the larger share of this gap in feedback content, not independent restarts. The advantage holds in every model family and survives parser repair. A separate phase-separated execution matches the aggregate yield without reusing responses; most certified witnesses also fail held-out cases. Preserving these witnesses delivers stronger specification repair: Phase-Revised contracts achieve 81.3% implementation success, versus 62.5% for IS-Revised, with Direct-Revised close behind. Both no-feedback witness sources substantially outperform weakened contracts. The effect does not reproduce in the available Dafny and Lean evaluations, establishing an empirical boundary around the studied Verus/Rust workflow. Completeness auditing and correctness repair have opposing immediate objectives. Preserve and certify the admitted failure before repairing it.

</details>


### [4] [IneqAgent: Agentic Inequality Proving in Lean with Symbolic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20681)
*Gaolei He,Jinhui Li,Ruobing Zuo,Xia Zeng,Zhengfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 结合LLM推理与符号计算的Lean证明agent，含MCTS规划与工具反馈，方法可迁移。

TL;DR: IneqAgent 将 LLM 推理、符号计算、引理级 MCTS 与 Lean 反馈结合，用于形式化不等式证明，在四个基准上达到 94.09% 成功率，并获得若干数学结果的 Lean 验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化不等式证明同时依赖数学推理和精确计算，且二者常通过中间引理与目标重述连接，导致自动化证明困难。

Method: 提出 IneqAgent：结合 LLM 推理与符号计算；用引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索协调引理构建、目标重述和符号后端选择；采用平方和、Bernstein、逐次差分代换后端生成精确证书；再结合 LLM 生成步骤与 Lean 反馈生成完整可检查证明。

Result: 在四个基准上，使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的 IneqAgent 解出 186 题中的 175 题（94.09%），总体成功率最高；在四个骨干模型上，低推理成本阈值下优于同骨干 Direct Agent，且成功运行的平均推理成本更低；还获得 Dittert 猜想四维、五维的 Lean 验证强化，并认证 Holens--Djokovic (4,4) 不等式，推出四维 Merris 猜想。

Conclusion: IneqAgent 有效整合 LLM 推理、符号计算与交互式证明反馈，推进形式化不等式证明，并能产生经 Lean 验证的数学强化结果。

中文摘要：形式化不等式证明需要数学推理与精确计算，二者通常通过中间引理和目标重述相连接。我们提出 IneqAgent，一个将大语言模型推理与符号计算相结合、用于 Lean4 中不等式证明的智能体框架。引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索协调引理构建、目标重述和符号后端选择。互补的平方和、Bernstein 以及逐次差分代换后端生成精确证书，用于局部 Lean 证明生成。该智能体将这些证明与 LLM 生成的步骤整合，并利用 Lean 反馈构建对照原始定理陈述检查的完整证明。在四个基准上，使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的 IneqAgent 解决了 186 个问题中的 175 个（94.09%），在被评估方法中取得最高总体成功率。在四个骨干模型上，与使用相同骨干模型的 Direct Agent 相比，它在低 LLM 推理成本阈值下取得更高成功率，并且在各方法成功运行上的平均推理成本更低。我们还获得了 Dittert 猜想在四维和五维下的 Lean 验证强化，并认证了 Holens--Djokovic (4,4) 不等式，从而推出四维的 Merris 猜想。

Original abstract: Formal inequality proving requires mathematical reasoning and exact computation, often connected through intermediate lemmas and goal reformulations. We introduce IneqAgent, an agentic framework combining LLM reasoning with symbolic computation for inequality proving in Lean4. Lemma-level Monte Carlo Tree Search coordinates lemma construction, goal reformulation, and symbolic backend selection. Complementary sum-of-squares, Bernstein, and successive difference-substitution backends produce exact certificates for local Lean proof generation. The agent integrates these proofs with LLM-generated steps and uses Lean feedback to construct complete proofs checked against the original theorem statements. On four benchmarks, IneqAgent with DeepSeek-V4-Flash solves 175 of 186 problems (94.09%), achieving the highest overall success rate among the evaluated methods. Across four backbones, it achieves higher success rates at low LLM inference cost thresholds than Direct Agent using the same backbone, with lower mean inference costs over each method's successful runs. We also obtain Lean-verified strengthenings of Dittert's conjecture in dimensions four and five, and certify the Holens--Djokovic $(4,4)$ inequality, implying Merris's conjecture in dimension four.

</details>


### [5] [ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20780)
*Bolin Shen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出多agent系统运行时检查与修复框架，学习可复用的修复策略，直接相关。

TL;DR: ARIA将经执行验证的修复转化为可复用监督，构建首个失败多智能体轨迹与验证修复配对的大规模数据集，并训练紧凑模型在运行时检测与修正进行中的多智能体执行，在事后与运行时设置中表现强且可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统中局部错误会传播并导致任务失败；诊断已完成运行或反复重执行无法提供执行中干预的学习策略。

Method: 构建ARIA数据集：收集超过24,000条轨迹，含3,953个经验证的修复对；开发与MAS无关的运行时检查与修正框架，用经验证的修复训练紧凑模型识别何时干预及如何纠正进行中的运行。

Result: 训练后的ARIA在事后和运行时设置中均展现出强大的修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架和宿主模型。

Conclusion: 通过将修复搜索前置到数据构建和训练，ARIA实现了可复用的运行时修复策略学习，而非仅复现单次修正。

中文摘要：在基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中，局部错误会随着其他智能体在其基础上继续构建而传播，将可纠正的错误变成失败的任务。诊断已完成的运行，或反复重新执行以搜索修正，并不能提供一种在执行仍在进行时进行干预的学习到的策略。我们提出智能体运行时检查与修正（ARIA），一个用于学习何时以及如何修复进行中的多智能体执行的框架。我们的核心直觉是将经执行验证的修复转化为可复用的监督信号，将修复搜索转移到数据构建和训练中，使学习到的模型能够在运行时进行干预，而无需反复测试替代方案。首先，我们在四个多智能体框架、六个基准和八个宿主模型上收集了超过24,000条成功和失败轨迹。从这些轨迹中，我们构建了ARIA数据集；据我们所知，这是首个将失败的多智能体轨迹与经执行验证的修复配对的大规模数据集。它包含3,953个经验证的修复对，每个修复对都指定了哪里出错、应在何处干预以及执行应如何继续。然后，我们开发了ARIA框架，用于与MAS无关的运行时检查和修正，旨在与任何能够暴露执行轨迹并支持续接替换的MAS集成。使用经验证的修复作为训练示例，我们训练一个紧凑模型来识别何时需要干预以及如何纠正正在进行的运行。目标是学习能够应用于新配置中相关失败的修复策略，而不仅仅是复现单个修正。最后，训练后的ARIA模型在事后和运行时设置中均展现出强大的修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架和宿主模型。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA 获取。

Original abstract: In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.

</details>


### [6] [EvolvingBrain: An Adaptive Skill System for Multi-modal Agents Self-Evolution in Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20786)
*Runxuan Liu,Jingqi Shi,Baoxin Wang,Honghong Zhao,Haoyuan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Jiafeng Liang,Tao He,Dayong Wu,Qi Gu,Shijin Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多模态agent自适应技能系统与分层技能自演化，涉及经验复用与自我改进，可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出 EvolvingBrain，通过分层技能自我进化与状态感知自适应执行，构建自适应技能系统以提升多模态智能体在开放世界中的自我进化与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体自我进化能力有限；监督微调只能模仿且不能主动组织经验，基于结果的强化学习探索虽强但未充分利用中间轨迹信息。

Method: EvolvingBrain 以自适应技能系统作为智能体自我进化的大脑，包含分层技能自我进化和状态感知自适应执行。前者从模型特定轨迹提取过程模式与问题模式，生成技能并通过开放世界交互进化，形成包含领域、计划、动作/陷阱技能的分层技能架构；后者将长时程交互总结为结构化抽象记忆，并指导状态感知技能融合策略。

Result: 在 VAB-Minecraft 和 DiscoveryWorld 上，EvolvingBrain 提高了开放世界任务成功率并展现出泛化能力；代码在补充材料中提供，并将在最终定稿版本公开发布。

Conclusion: EvolvingBrain 为智能体自我进化提供了多粒度知识库与新进化方法，并验证了其在开放世界任务中的有效性与泛化性。

中文摘要：多模态智能体在与开放世界交互方面已取得显著进展。然而，其自我进化能力仍然有限。监督微调能够实现模仿，但无法主动组织经验；而基于结果的强化学习虽促进探索，却未充分利用中间轨迹信息。因此，我们提出 EvolvingBrain，它使用自适应技能系统作为智能体自我进化的大脑，包含以下两个组件。分层技能自我进化：从模型特定的轨迹中提取过程模式（Procedural Patterns）和问题模式（Problematic Patterns），通过生成技能来总结经验，并通过与开放世界的多次交互进行进化，最终形成由领域（Domain）、计划（Plan）和动作/陷阱（Action/Trap）技能组成的分层技能架构，为智能体提供多粒度知识库和一种新颖的进化方法。状态感知自适应执行：将长时程交互总结为结构化抽象记忆，并用其指导状态感知技能融合策略，使智能体能够根据其当前状态利用知识。在 VAB-Minecraft 和 DiscoveryWorld 上的实验表明，EvolvingBrain 提高了开放世界任务的成功率，并展现出泛化能力。我们的代码已在补充材料中提供，并将在最终定稿版本中公开发布。

Original abstract: Multi-modal agents have made significant progress in interacting with open worlds. However, their ability to self-evolve remains limited. Supervised fine-tuning enables imitation but not active experience organization, while outcome-based reinforcement learning promotes exploration yet underuses intermediate trajectory information. Therefore, we propose EvolvingBrain, which uses an adaptive skill system as the brain for agent self-evolution, consisting of two following components. Stratified Skill Self-Evolution: extracts Procedural Patterns and Problematic Patterns from model-specific trajectories, summarizes experience by generating skills, and evolves through multiple interactions with the open world, ultimately forming a Stratified Skill Architecture consisting of Domain, Plan, and Action/Trap Skills, which provides the agent with a multi-granularity knowledge repository and a novel evolution approach. State-Aware Adaptive Execution: summarizes long-horizon interactions into Structured Abstractive Memory and uses it to guide the State-Aware Skill Fusion Strategy, enabling the agent to utilize knowledge according to its current state. Experiments on VAB-Minecraft and DiscoveryWorld demonstrate that EvolvingBrain improves success rates on open-world tasks and exhibits generalization capabilities. Our code is provided in the supplementary materials and will be publicly released in the camera-ready version.

</details>


### [7] [When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20805)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 研究推理轨迹与证据冲突时的答案配置影响，对agent反思与答案生成设计有潜在启发。

TL;DR: 该研究分离了回答时保留推理轨迹的两种作用：提升准确率与在冲突时与当前证据竞争；发现单独答案调用比延续模型思考更遵循当前证据，删除轨迹会降低准确率，而延续思考常重复过时结论，因此“受益于轨迹”与“服从轨迹”是不同属性，评估需同时报告二者及回答配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答时保留的推理轨迹既能提高准确率，也可能在轨迹与当前证据冲突时干扰模型对证据的采用。为厘清模型究竟是受益于轨迹还是服从轨迹，需要将这两种作用分离，并明确评估不能仅靠删除轨迹来判断答案来源。

Method: 将经过验证的证据版本与各版本生成的轨迹交叉组合，在两种回答配置下使用相同轨迹和证据作答：延续模型自身思考，以及单独的答案调用。实验覆盖三个模型家族（gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3）和 HotpotQA、MuSiQue 数据集，比较证据遵循率与准确率，并进一步分析轨迹末行、黄金证据出现时机、干扰项，以及重新推理与验证指令的差异。

Result: 单独答案调用相比延续思考将证据遵循率提高 29.9 到 60.6 个百分点；在相同配对中，删除轨迹使两种配置的准确率下降相似幅度；延续在多数冲突中重复过时结论，单独调用则更常遵循当前证据。进一步分析显示：轨迹最后一行承载大部分价值；黄金证据在轨迹写出前帮助最大；干扰项削弱单独调用下的纠正；重新推理不同于验证指令，在多数冲突中遵循当前证据。

Conclusion: 从推理轨迹中受益与服从推理轨迹是可分离的属性。仅删除轨迹无法揭示答案遵循的是轨迹还是当前证据，因此评估应同时报告准确率与证据遵循这两类属性，并说明所采用的答案配置。

中文摘要：在回答时保留的推理轨迹发挥两种作用：它可以提高准确率，并且当它与当前证据不一致时，可以与当前证据相竞争。我们通过将经过验证的证据版本与由每个版本生成的轨迹交叉，并在两种配置下从相同的轨迹和证据进行回答来分离这两种作用：延续模型自身的思考，以及单独的答案调用。在三个模型家族（gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3）于 HotpotQA 和 MuSiQue 上，单独的答案调用相比延续将证据遵循率提高了 29.9 到 60.6 个百分点。在相同的配对中，删除轨迹在两种配置下都会使准确率下降相似幅度。然而，延续在大多数冲突中重复过时结论，而单独的答案调用则远更频繁地遵循当前证据。因此，从轨迹中受益和服从轨迹是不同的属性。进一步分析表明，轨迹的最后一行承载了其大部分价值；黄金证据在轨迹写出之前帮助最大；干扰项会削弱单独答案调用下的纠正；与验证指令不同，重新推理在大多数冲突中遵循当前证据。由于仅删除轨迹无法揭示答案遵循的是哪个来源，评估应同时报告这两种属性以及答案配置。

Original abstract: A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model's own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.

</details>


### [8] [A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20809)
*Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Yirou Ge,Yixi Li,Yiming Bian,Zijie Pan,Jenna L. Ballard,Uma Kona,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自改进agent自主诱导DSL算子，无需奖励或答案键，涉及验证与技能生长，高度相关。

TL;DR: 一个冻结模型通过嵌套循环在精确 DSL 上无奖励、无评判、无标准答案地自我扩展：内循环用两个独立表达式的一致性来回答问题，外循环仅接纳有独立第二实现且遵守守恒定律的新算子；该方法扩大可回答问题集、拒绝全部有缺陷变体，并跨领域与模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进智能体通常依据高奖励、评判模型或压缩信号保留技能，即使技能错误；但在交易表总计等需要完全正确的场景中，这种策略不可靠。作者因此追问：能否在没有奖励、评判者和标准答案的条件下，让智能体增长可计算能力并保持精确正确。

Method: 使用单一冻结模型，在小型精确领域特定语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内循环仅在两个独立编写的表达式于每条记录上都一致时才回答新问题。外循环发现自身缺失能力并编写算子，仅当独立第二实现一致且结果满足守恒定律时接纳，全程无需真实标签；并设置无归纳对照组。

Result: 内循环约将可回答问题集扩大三倍；外循环在不相交起始集上自我扩展并发明起始闭包外的算子，无归纳对照组答不出任何新问题。接纳规则在独立参考下拒绝 138/138 个有缺陷算子变体，包括不同模型家族编写的变体，而能运行就接纳、自洽性和压缩信号会接纳其中大多数。未经修改，同一机制在商业台账与临床就诊问答两个不相关模式领域分别将可回答集扩大超过十倍和接近二十倍。该循环可跨模型家族迁移：更多提议者下门控仍可靠，独立家族自我扩展程度相当，仅归纳产出率与各提议者的 DSL 熟练度相关。

Conclusion: 该机制表明，智能体可在无奖励、无评判者和无标准答案的情况下，通过独立验证与守恒约束可靠地自我扩展精确计算能力；其接纳规则经验上可靠，并可跨领域和模型家族泛化，为需要严格正确的自我改进智能体提供了可行路径。

中文摘要：一个自我改进的智能体必须决定保留哪些新技能；在实践中，它会保留任何能获得高奖励、说服评判模型或压缩其技能库的技能，即使该技能是错误的。在答案必须完全正确之前，这没什么问题：对于一张交易表，总计要么正确，要么错误。我们提出一个更难的问题：智能体能否在没有奖励、没有评判者、也没有标准答案的情况下，扩展其可计算的能力，并且仍然完全正确？一个单一的冻结模型在一个小型、精确的领域特定语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内循环仅当两个独立编写的表达式在每条记录上都一致时，才回答新问题。外循环发现自身缺失的能力，并为其编写一个算子；仅当一个独立的第二实现也一致，且结果遵守守恒定律时，该算子才被接纳；所有这些都不需要真实标签。内循环大约将可回答问题集扩大三倍，外循环在不相交的起始集上自我扩展，发明起始闭包之外的算子；一个无归纳对照组对新问题一个也答不出。该接纳规则在经验上是可靠的：在独立参考下，它拒绝了 138 个有缺陷的算子变体中的 138 个，即使其中一个由不同模型家族编写；而该领域依赖的“能运行就接纳”、自洽性和压缩信号会接纳其中大多数。未经修改，同一机制在两个具有不相关模式的进一步领域中自我扩展：商业台账和临床就诊问答，将其可回答集分别扩大超过十倍和接近二十倍。该循环也能跨模型家族迁移：用更多提议者运行时，门控保持可靠，独立家族以相当程度自我扩展，只有归纳产出率与每个提议者的 DSL 熟练度相关。

Original abstract: A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.

</details>


### [9] [FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22350)
*Dongxu Yang,Hongda Yan,Renxin Wang,Baoli Zhang,Haojia Zuo,Ying Zhao,Youmin Chen,Xiaojian Liao,Congming Gao,Tianyang Jiang,Yixiao Chen,Qing Wang,Zhiguo Yu,Xiaqing Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiawen Sun,Renzhe Xu,De Mi,Tiancheng Wu,Xianya Fu,Xuanlu Zhao,Bo Feng,Meixuan Ren,Guanming Lin,Ziheng Zhou,Xinhua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables. Website: https://fde-gym.com/paper ; Code: https://github.com/deeplethe/fde-gym ; Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/DeepLethe/FDE-Gym

Selection reason: FDE-Gym基准评测agent在模糊需求、隐式约束与真实交付环境中的长程交互能力，含评分与惩罚机制，可迁移到agent评测。

TL;DR: 提出 FDE-Gym 基准，评估智能体在模拟客户交付中澄清模糊需求、发现未明说约束并交付方案；最佳模型平均分 0.56，仅解决 14/95 任务，暴露从明确任务到真实交付的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准侧重定义明确的 issue、规格或工单任务，无法反映真实客户交付中需求模糊、需在实时系统工作并发现未明说约束的挑战，因此需要评估前向部署工程师（FDE）能力。

Method: 构建 FDE-Gym：包含 18 个行业、20 个客户场景的 95 个端到端交付任务，由行业专家从真实项目改编；在含脚本化利益相关者和可修改类生产系统的模拟客户环境中交互与操作；按客户成功指标提升归一化评分（0 到 1），并对消息、试运行、等待时间等交互施加惩罚；提供在线平台供人类在相同标准下练习；评估来自七家提供商的 13 个模型配置。

Result: 最佳配置平均 FDE-Gym 分数为 0.56，仅解决 95 个任务中的 14 个（≥0.8 且无事故）；发现未明说约束是主要瓶颈，无明确指导时性能急剧下降；即使最佳配置从利益相关者获得所有关键事实的 23 个任务中，也仅 10 个达到 ≥0.8 的归一化业务提升。

Conclusion: 解决定义明确的任务与为真实客户交付有效 AI 系统之间存在巨大差距，信息发现之外仍有其他能力缺口。

中文摘要：现有的智能体基准通常基于问题报告、规格说明或任务工单定义的明确任务来评估智能体。真实的客户交付则不同：前向部署工程师（FDE）必须澄清模糊需求、在实时系统中工作，并发现未明说的约束。我们提出 FDE-Gym，一个包含来自 18 个行业、20 个客户场景的 95 个端到端交付任务的基准，每个任务均由行业专家根据真实交付项目改编。在具有脚本化利益相关者和可修改客户系统的模拟客户环境中，智能体与利益相关者交互，并在类生产系统上操作，以交付可用的解决方案。FDE-Gym 根据交付对客户自身成功指标的提升程度进行评分，归一化到 0（系统未改变）到 1（参考解决方案）之间，并对利益相关者交互（消息、试运行和等待时间）施加惩罚。一个在线平台让人类在相同评分标准下练习 FDE 技能。我们评估了来自七家提供商的 13 个模型配置。在需要发现未明说约束的真实设定中，最佳配置取得平均 FDE-Gym 分数 0.56，并且仅解决 95 个任务中的 14 个（得分至少 0.8 且无事故）。发现未明说的约束是主要瓶颈：当模型必须在没有明确指导的情况下揭示这些约束时，性能急剧下降。然而，仅靠信息发现并不能解释这一差距：即使在最佳配置从利益相关者处获得所有关键事实的 23 个任务上，它也仅在 10 个任务上达到 0.8 或以上的归一化业务提升。这些结果揭示了解决明确任务与为真实客户交付有效 AI 系统之间存在巨大差距。

Original abstract: Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.

</details>


### [10] [Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20836)
*Weida Liang,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: 评估器引导推理的置信校准与计算感知控制器，可迁移至agent的推理资源分配。

TL;DR: 论文将评估器引导推理中的每步选择建模为计算感知的三动作控制问题，并基于局部动作间隙与遗憾分析学习校准置信度，在宽度扩展和修订任务上以更少计算量取得效用提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估器引导推理每一步都面临局部控制问题：是信任当前答案、返回回退方案，还是投入更多推理计算。需要显式权衡答案成功率与计算成本。

Method: 将选择形式化为计算感知的三动作控制器：终止动作按预测答案成功率打分，扩展动作按基于 rollout 的延续成功代理打分，并显式计入计算成本。利用局部动作间隙分析刻画分数误差何时改变决策，用期望遗憾界处理实际扩展路径上的误差。据此学习并校准动作特定置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。

Result: 在留出的宽度扩展和修订任务上，控制器相比校准选择的固定策略提高了观测效用，并将归一化在线计算量分别减少约 30% 和 16%。在四个 Qwen3-8B 随机种子下，两种设置的计算量均低于拟合 Q，但效用差异较小且方向不一。

Conclusion: 结果将评估器引导的推理重新定位为不确定性下的资源分配问题，而非一个统一更强的推理器。

中文摘要：评估器引导的推理在每一步都呈现一个局部控制问题：信任当前答案、返回一个回退方案，还是花费更多推理。我们将该选择形式化为一个计算感知的三动作控制器，通过预测的答案成功率对终止动作打分，并通过基于 rollout 的延续成功代理对扩展动作打分，同时显式计入计算成本。局部动作间隙分析刻画了分数误差何时会改变决策，而期望遗憾界则考虑了沿实际扩展路径的误差。在该分析的指导下，我们学习并校准动作特定的置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。在留出的宽度扩展和修订任务上，该控制器相比校准选择的固定策略提高了观测效用，同时将归一化在线计算量分别减少约 30% 和 16%。在四个 Qwen3-8B 随机种子下，两种设置中的计算量均低于拟合 Q，而效用差异较小且方向不一。结果将评估器引导的推理框定为不确定性下的资源分配，而不是一个统一更强的推理器。

Original abstract: Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

</details>


### [11] [Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20845)
*Yujie Feng,Xiaobin Wang,Jinluan Yang,Xiaonan Shi,Pengjun Xie,Philip S. Yu,Xiaoming Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Under Review

Selection reason: 揭示自改进agent技能-策略协同演化陷阱并提出漂移审计协议，直接相关。

TL;DR: 论文指出技能-策略协同演化评估忽视部署后技能漂移，提出面向部署的CODA审计协议；在4种方法、5个基准（6种设置）上，最坏情况成功率比交付配对峰值低5–13点，抹去52–93%增益，并主张以技能任意演化下的利用能力作为新目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前协同演化方法只在训练得到的交付技能库上评估策略，但部署后技能的重写、增删可能快于策略重训，导致策略面对漂移技能库；方法共有的协作式接受规则把策略能力绑定到共同演化的技能库，使脆弱性随训练推进而加剧。

Method: 通过理论分析与实证分析揭示上述脆弱性；提出CODA（CO-evolution Drift Audit）部署导向评估协议，模拟技能漂移，并在最坏情况漂移成功率之外，引入保持语义编辑下的一致性率和对损坏技能的抵抗率；在4种协同演化方法、5个基准、6种设置上评估。

Result: 最坏情况成功率比每个已交付策略-技能库配对的峰值低5–13个百分点，抹去交付技能库相对无技能基线增益的52–93%；对技能漂移的敏感性随协同演化训练推进而增长。

Conclusion: CODA将协同演化的目标从在协同演化技能库上的峰值性能，重新定义为在技能任意演化时仍能利用技能的能力，并为未来方法提供可审计的共享标准。

中文摘要：技能-策略协同演化，即智能体的策略模型与其技能库在训练过程中相互改进，是一种有前景的自我改进智能体范式。当前评估在协同演化后的策略所训练时使用的最终技能库（交付技能库）上对其打分。然而，在部署之后，技能可能比重训策略模型更频繁地被重写、添加或删除，使策略在一个漂移的技能库上运行，而这种漂移既未被训练目标也未被现有评估协议处理。本文首先通过理论分析与实证分析确证：这一盲点所隐藏的脆弱性并非偶然。这些方法共有的接受规则（协作式接受）将策略的能力与其协同演化所依赖的技能库绑定，使其一旦技能发生漂移就变得脆弱，无论具体实现如何；实证上，对技能漂移的敏感性会随着协同演化训练的推进而增长。因此，我们提出协同演化漂移审计（CO-evolution Drift Audit，CODA），一种面向部署的评估协议，它模拟技能漂移，并除最坏情况漂移下的成功率外，引入两个新指标：在保持语义编辑下的一致性率，以及对损坏技能的抵抗率。在四种协同演化方法和五个基准（六种设置）上，最坏情况成功率比每个已交付（策略-技能库）配对所测得的峰值低5–13个百分点，抹去了交付技能库相对于无技能基线所获增益的52–93%。因此，CODA重新定义了协同演化的目标：从在协同演化技能库上的峰值性能，转向无论技能如何演化都能利用技能的能力，并提供了一个可供未来方法接受审计的共享标准。

Original abstract: Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.

</details>


### [12] [SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20926)
*Enze Ma,Chunyu Miao,Yankai Chen,Jie Yang,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hanrong Zhang,Muyao Xu,Xinle Yu,Chengze Li,Wei-Chieh Huang,Ziwei Ren,Yunhao Du,Zhixiang Shen,Xue Liu,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 非精英式演化优化 agent 技能，用执行反馈进行版本保留与修复，直接对应代码优化 agent 的技能/经验迭代与自我改进。

TL;DR: SkillNEO提出非精英演化优化智能体技能，区分“使用版本”与“继续改进版本”，通过比较相关版本在同一任务上的执行表现来识别保留与修复点；在86个SkillsBench任务上将GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励从49.1%提升到66.4%，且所得技能可无需再编辑地提升更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能演化依赖执行反馈迭代改进技能，但改进常需要多个相互依赖的编辑，低奖励版本可能仍值得继续开发；若要求即时奖励提升，会在收益显现前丢弃有用的部分修复。非精英演化允许低分新版本继续接受编辑，因此需要证据来判断下一步应保留什么、修复什么。

Method: 提出SkillNEO（Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills），分别选择要使用的版本与要继续改进的版本。通过比较相关版本执行同一任务的表现，识别有效工作步骤与剩余失败；还可利用后续证据回溯并修订更早版本。

Result: 在八个领域86个SkillsBench任务上，SkillNEO将GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励从49.1%提升至66.4%，处于GPT-5.5与GPT-5.6 Luna原始技能范围；增益覆盖全部八个领域。在41个技能被修改的任务上，复用使Luna提升10.5个百分点，并使GPT-5.5较其已发布原始技能参考高出10.6个百分点。相同技能无需进一步编辑即可提升更强模型；执行记录显示，即使Mini仍未能满足部分任务要求，这些技能也能使更强模型受益；搜索历史显示低分版本可导向最终被选用的技能。

Conclusion: 技能演化能够将较弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升能力更强的智能体；低奖励版本不应被过早淘汰，它们可能通过后续编辑与复用产生最终价值。

中文摘要：智能体技能将程序性知识转化为可复用的产物，使LLM智能体能够通过经验积累并精炼专业知识。在此基础上，技能演化利用执行反馈迭代地改进技能。然而，改进可能需要若干相互依赖的编辑，因此低奖励的版本仍然值得继续开发。要求即时奖励提升可能会在收益显现之前丢弃有用的部分修复。非精英演化允许新版本尽管得分低于早前版本，仍能接受进一步编辑。对于智能体技能而言，这需要关于下一步应保留和修复什么的证据。我们提出SkillNEO（面向智能体技能的非精英演化优化），它分别选择要使用的版本和要继续改进的版本。比较相关版本在同一任务上的表现，可以揭示需要保留的有效步骤以及仍需修复的失败之处。它还可以利用后续证据重新审视并修订更早的版本。在涵盖软件工程和办公工作等八个领域的86个SkillsBench任务上，SkillNEO将GPT-5.4 Mini的平均奖励从49.1%提升至66.4%，处于GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6 Luna原始技能范围内。Mini执行任务、提出修订并编辑技能；最终评估使用相同的任务实例。增益覆盖全部八个领域。相同的技能无需进一步编辑即可提升更强的模型。在41个技能被修改的任务上，复用使Luna提升10.5个百分点，并使GPT-5.5比其已发布的原始技能参考高出10.6个百分点。执行记录显示，即使Mini仍然未能满足某些任务要求，这些技能也能使更强的模型受益。搜索历史还显示，得分较低的版本会导向最终被选中使用的技能。总之，这些发现表明，技能演化可以将较弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升能力更强的智能体。

Original abstract: Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

</details>


### [13] [RAC: A Relational Agent Contract for Constraint-Governed Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20935)
*Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Zubin Aaron Assadian,Yuetai Li,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出约束工程任务中 agent 动作-反馈层的 harness 无关契约，强调工具实现、独立验证与修复证据，可迁移到代码优化 agent 的验证闭环。

TL;DR: 论文提出与执行框架无关的动作与反馈契约 RAC，让代理做语义关系决策、由工具推导具体后果，从而提升受约束工程任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工程代理需同时做语义决策并推导精确几何、时序等后果，容易在精确耦合约束任务中失败。

Method: 提出 RAC 契约，规定权威观察、语义动作、工具实现、独立验证与修复证据；代理决定制品应满足的关系，专用工具计算具体后果。

Result: 在 PCB 布局、布线和修复中，RAC 在九个模型上平均干净通过率 90.9%，高于最强基线 74.0%；作业车间调度中直接关系动作质量通过率 96.3%，高于代码中介交付 59.6%；四类微控制器的 141 个嵌入式代码任务验证了动作契约的作用。

Conclusion: 保留语义控制并将可机械推导的输出委托给工具，是提升代理能力与受约束制品可靠性的关键。

中文摘要：工程代理必须生成满足精确、耦合约束的制品。现有解决方案通常要求代理做出语义决策，并从这些决策中推导诸如精确几何或时序等后果，导致其在受约束支配的工程任务中失败。本文中，我们提出 RAC，一种用于工程代理动作与反馈层的、与执行框架无关的契约。它规定权威观察、语义动作、基于工具的实现、独立验证和修复证据。代理决定制品应满足哪些关系；专用工具推导其具体后果。在 PCB 布局、布线和修复任务上，RAC 在全部九个被评估模型中取得最高干净通过率，平均为 90.9%，而最强基线“仅需求委托”为 74.0%。在作业车间调度中，直接关系动作达到 96.3% 的质量通过率，而使用相同调度后端的代码中介交付为 59.6%。我们在四类微控制器上的 141 个嵌入式代码任务中的评估确认，动作契约是代理能力的一种来源：在保留语义控制的同时委托可机械推导的输出，可提高受约束制品的可靠性。

Original abstract: Engineering agents must produce artifacts that satisfy exact, coupled constraints. Existing solutions often require agents to make semantic decisions and also derive consequences such as precise geometry or timing from those decisions, leading to failures in constraint-governed engineering tasks. In this paper, we introduce RAC, a harness-agnostic contract for the action and feedback layer of engineering agents. It specifies authoritative observations, semantic actions, tool-based realization, independent verification, and repair evidence. The agent decides which relations an artifact should satisfy; specialized tools derive their concrete consequences. On PCB placement, routing, and repair tasks, RAC achieves the highest clean-pass rate for all nine evaluated models, averaging 90.9%, compared with 74.0% for the strongest baseline, requirements-only delegation. In job-shop scheduling, direct relational actions achieve 96.3% quality pass versus 59.6% for code-mediated delivery with access to the same scheduling backend. Our evaluations on 141 embedded-code tasks across four microcontroller identify the action contract as a source of agent capability: preserving semantic control while delegating mechanically derivable outputs improves the reliability of constrained artifacts.

</details>


### [14] [MAI-Gym: An Open All-in-One Platform for GUI Agent Evaluation and RL Training across Mobile and Computer Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20939)
*Panrong Tong,Quyu Kong,Pengxiang Li,Tianyu Xia,Hanzhang Zhou,Lei Wang,Chenglin Cai,Long Li,Long Chen,Jianan Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Xu Zhang,Gongjie Zhang,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 31 pages, 15 pages

Selection reason: 统一 GUI agent 评测与在线 RL 训练的平台，含可验证任务合成与并行 rollout，其训练-评测一致性设计对 agent 基础设施有参考价值。

TL;DR: MAI-Gym 统一 GUI 智能体评估、数据收集与在线 RL 训练，支持多环境与自动任务合成；基于其训练的 MAI-Gym-9B 在 <10B 开放 GUI 智能体中达 SOTA，并展示模拟到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 RL 与可验证奖励在数学、编程、工具使用上进展快，但 GUI 智能体受限于交互环境成本高、现实训练任务稀缺，难以规模化。

Method: 提出 MAI-Gym 一体化开放平台：共享 rollout 流水线统一评估与训练，支持移动/计算机/网页，十个基准，20,000 并行实例；可自动合成多样化 GUI 应用与任务，包括可重置模拟应用、跨应用世界和执行验证器；用其训练统一 9B 移动与计算机智能体 MAI-Gym-9B。

Result: MAI-Gym-9B 在 <10B 开放 GUI 智能体中达 SOTA：MobileWorld 78.4%，OSWorld-Verified 76.2%；在模拟火车票预订应用 RL 迁移到真实物理手机，任务成功率从 28.8% 提升到 48.1%。

Conclusion: 发布框架、合成技能、模拟应用、2,000 个已验证任务和模型检查点，支持可复现研究与可扩展 GUI 智能体训练。

中文摘要：具有可验证奖励的在线强化学习（RL）已推动数学、编程和工具使用领域的快速进展，但将这一成功扩展到 GUI 智能体仍然具有挑战性，因为交互环境成本高昂且现实训练任务稀缺。我们提出 MAI-Gym，一个开放的一体化平台，统一了跨移动端、计算机和网页环境的 GUI 智能体评估、数据收集和在线 RL 训练。通过共享的 rollout 流水线，MAI-Gym 确保评估和训练中的智能体行为一致，支持十个已有基准，并可扩展至 20,000 个并行环境实例。除可扩展基础设施外，MAI-Gym 还支持自动合成多样化的 GUI 应用和具有挑战性的任务，包括可重置的模拟应用以及具有执行验证任务验证器的连贯跨应用世界。使用 MAI-Gym，我们训练了 MAI-Gym-9B，一个面向移动端和计算机使用的统一 9B 智能体，在参数少于 10B 的开放 GUI 智能体中达到最先进性能：在 MobileWorld 上为 78.4%，在 OSWorld-Verified 上为 76.2%。重要的是，在模拟的火车票预订应用中进行的 RL 可迁移到其真实世界对应应用，将物理手机上的任务成功率从 28.8% 提升至 48.1%。我们发布框架、合成技能、模拟应用、2,000 个已验证任务以及模型检查点，以支持可复现研究并推动可扩展 GUI 智能体训练的进一步进展。

Original abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has driven rapid progress in mathematics, coding, and tool use, but scaling this success to GUI agents remains challenging due to the high cost of interactive environments and the scarcity of realistic training tasks. We introduce MAI-Gym, an open all-in-one platform that unifies GUI agent evaluation, data collection, and online RL training across mobile, computer, and web environments. Through a shared rollout pipeline, MAI-Gym ensures consistent agent behavior across evaluation and training, supports ten established benchmarks, and scales to 20,000 parallel environment instances. Beyond scalable infrastructure, MAI-Gym enables automated synthesis of diverse GUI applications and challenging tasks, including resettable mocked apps and coherent cross-app worlds with execution-validated task verifiers. Using MAI-Gym, we train MAI-Gym-9B, a unified 9B agent for mobile and computer use, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open GUI agents under 10B parameters: 78.4% on MobileWorld and 76.2% on OSWorld-Verified. Importantly, RL in a mocked train-ticketing app transfers to its real-world counterpart, improving task success on a phys- ical phone from 28.8% to 48.1%. We release the framework, synthesis skills, mocked apps, 2,000 verified tasks, and the model checkpoint to support repro- ducible research and further advances in scalable GUI agent training.

</details>


### [15] [StepSafe: Learning Timely Intervention with Context-Aware Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20960)
*Siyi Gu,Kunhan Gao,Chao Peter Yang,Sukanta Ganguly,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 步骤级 agent 安全护栏，研究风险证据随时间累积与何时干预，对 agent 可靠性与过程监督有可迁移启发。

TL;DR: 提出面向智能体安全的步骤级及时干预训练框架 ASPO，并给出护栏 StepSafe；通过前缀级阶段对齐、自适应反馈自蒸馏、自演化多智能体红队测试，在五个基准上取得开源护栏 SOTA，Youden's J 53.0（4B）对 46.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全具有序列依赖性，安全与否取决于先前动作、工具输出、信息披露和智能体交互；现有护栏训练目标对步骤证据累积及干预时机监督不足。

Method: ASPO：前缀级阶段对齐在同前缀响应间比较并按阶段奖励继续/询问/停止；自适应反馈自蒸馏用模型自身作教师，在干预步骤利用步骤标注、在安全步骤利用自身误报挖掘规则；训练数据带违规分类法和每步干预窗口，并用自演化多智能体红队测试扩展。构建步骤级推理护栏 StepSafe。

Result: StepSafe 在五个智能体安全基准、安全-效用帕累托曲线上，开源护栏中最优，Youden's J 达 53.0（4B），对比 46.2。

Conclusion: 将智能体安全从轨迹级事后评估推进到步骤级及时干预，ASPO 与 StepSafe 验证了该范式的有效性。

中文摘要：智能体系统中的安全性本质上是序列性的：一个动作是否安全取决于先前的动作、工具输出、信息披露以及智能体之间的交互。近期工作已将智能体安全护栏从轨迹级的事后评估转向步骤级动作诊断和主动风险预测，但现有训练目标对于步骤证据如何随时间累积、以及何时足以证明干预合理，提供的监督仍然有限。我们提出 \methodlong（ASPO）：一种用于步骤级及时干预的训练框架。Pre-fix level 阶段对齐允许在每一步对其自身决策进行信用分配：它将每个响应与在同一前缀下采样得到的其他响应进行比较，并奖励符合当前阶段的动作——在风险尚不可见时继续，在澄清仍可避免伤害时询问，在伤害不可逆之前停止。自适应反馈自蒸馏进一步为每个决策背后的推理进行信用分配，模型自身作为教师，在干预步骤上查看步骤标注，并在安全步骤上查看从其自身误报中挖掘出的规则。其训练数据在每一步都包含违规分类法和干预窗口，并通过自演化多智能体红队测试进行扩展。最后，我们提出 StepSafe，一种具有及时干预能力的步骤级推理护栏，在五个智能体安全基准上，于安全-效用帕累托曲线上，以 53.0（4B）对 46.2 的 Youden's J 值，在开源护栏中取得最先进性能。

Original abstract: Safety in agentic systems is inherently sequential: whether an action is safe depends on prior actions, tool outputs, information disclosures, and interactions among agents. Recent work has shifted agent safety guardrails from post-hoc trajectory-level assessment toward step-level action diagnosis and proactive risk prediction, yet existing training objectives provide limited supervision for how step evidence accumulates over time and when it becomes sufficient to justify intervention. We propose \methodlong~(ASPO): a training framework for step-level timely intervention. Pre-fix level stage alignment allows crediting each decision at its own step: it compares each response with the other responses sampled at the same prefix and rewards the action that fits the stage, continuing before the risk is visible, asking while clarification can still avert harm, and stopping before harm is irreversible. Adaptive feedback self-distillation further credits the reasoning behind each decision, with the model itself as a teacher that sees the step annotations on intervention steps and rules mined from its own false alarms on safe steps. Its training data carry a violation taxonomy and an intervention window at every step and are extended through self-evolving multi-agent red-teaming. Finally we present \textsc{StepSafe}, a step-level reasoning guardrail with timely intervention, that achieves the state-of-the-art peformance among open-source guardrails across five agent-safety benchmarks on safety-utility pareto curve with Youden's $J$ of 53.0 (4B) against 46.2.

</details>


### [16] [Recursive Reward Improvement via Adversarial Meta-Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21040)
*Wei Dai,Qianchu Liu,Guanghui Qin,Xing Han,Tristan Naumann,Paul Pu Liang,Sheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用奖励黑客对抗者监督 rubric 的递归自我改进循环，其防奖励作弊思想可迁移到代码优化 agent 的廉价奖励验证。

TL;DR: 提出RRI：用奖励破解对抗者监督模型生成的rubric，先让钻空子答案与诚实答案对抗，再修复rubric，从而提升开放式专家任务的递归自我改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式专家任务（如临床报告生成）没有可自动检查的标准答案，proposer必须生成rubric；但朴素生成的rubric不全面，solver会钻空子（只列主题而无实质），污染学习过程。

Method: 在proposer-solver循环中，solver训练前，让对抗者写一个钻rubric空子且尽量无用的答案，同一模型不看rubric写诚实答案，最多两轮修复rubric使诚实答案胜出；发现的漏洞还用于改写未来任务与rubric的生成。

Result: HealthBench Professional上，调整后分数较Generation Only基线：9B +10.9，27B +12.4；无外部模型 +10.3；困难与写作任务增益最大；27B在未见过任务时PRBench Hard +6.6、ProfBench +8.0。

Conclusion: RRI能缓解模型生成rubric被利用的问题，提升开放式专家任务自我改进及跨任务泛化，并分析问题-rubric对的可利用点以推动开放式自博弈研究。

中文摘要：递归自我改进（Recursive Self-Improvement, RSI）因其在有限数据下工作的能力而受到广泛关注。在提议者-求解者循环中，提议者模型编写训练任务，求解者模型通过强化学习学会解决这些任务。此类循环已在数学和编码领域取得成功，因为其中的每个答案都可以被自动检查。开放式专家任务，例如临床报告生成，没有标准答案，这使得简单的问答生成不可行。在这里，提议者还必须编写评分标准（rubric），列出答案应包含的内容以及应避免的错误。然而，模型编写的评分标准背后的独特挑战在于其全面性。对于朴素生成的评分标准，求解者倾向于利用它，列出预期主题却没有背后的实质内容，从而破坏学习过程。为了解决这个问题，我们引入RRI，即递归奖励改进（Recursive Reward Improvement），它使用一个奖励黑客（reward hacking）对抗者来监督奖励。在求解者在生成任务上训练之前，一个对抗者编写一个钻其评分标准空子且尽可能无用的答案，同一个模型在未看到评分标准的情况下编写一个诚实答案，并最多经过两个修复步骤修订评分标准，使诚实答案获胜。发现的漏洞也会改写未来任务和评分标准的生成方式。在HealthBench Professional上，RRI将调整后分数相较于仅生成（Generation Only）自我改进基线在9B上提升10.9分，在27B上提升12.4分，并且在不使用任何外部模型的情况下提升10.3分。特别是，这些增益在困难任务和写作任务上最大。我们表明，在HealthBench Professional描述上训练的27B模型，在未见过PRBench Hard和ProfBench中任何任务的情况下，分别在PRBench Hard上提升6.6分，在ProfBench上提升8.0分。我们进一步分析了模型生成的问题-评分标准对的可利用点，以促进未来关于开放式自博弈的研究。

Original abstract: Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) has gained wide attention for its ability to work with limited data. In a proposer-solver loop, a proposer model writes training tasks and a solver model learns to solve them through reinforcement learning. Such loops have succeeded in mathematics and coding, where every answer can be checked automatically. Open-ended expert tasks, like clinical report generations, have no answer key, which makes simple question-answer generation not viable. Here the proposer must also write a rubric that lists the content an answer should include and the errors it should avoid. However, the unique challenge behind model-written rubric is the comprehensiveness of it. For naively generated rubric, solvers tend to exploit it, naming the expected topics without the substance behind them, which corrupts the learning process. To solve this problem, we introduce RRI, Recursive Reward Improvement, which uses a reward hacking adversary to supervise the reward. Before the solver trains on a generated task, an adversary writes an answer that games its rubric while being as useless as possible, the same model writes an honest answer without seeing the rubric, and up to two repair steps revise the rubric to make the honest answer win. The exploits found also rewrite how future tasks and rubrics are generated. On HealthBench Professional, RRI improves the adjusted score over the Generation Only self-improvement baseline by 10.9 points at 9B and 12.4 at 27B, and by 10.3 points without any external model. In particular, the gains are largest on difficult and writing tasks. We show that the 27B model trained on the HealthBench Professional description improves by 6.6 points on PRBench Hard and 8.0 on ProfBench without seeing a task from either. We further analyze the exploitable points of model-generated question-rubric pairs to facilitate future research on open-ended self-play.

</details>


### [17] [Learning When to Withdraw: Curriculum Coverage and Rule-Specific Failures in Fine-Tuned Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21045)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究微调语言 agent 的选择性撤回与规则级失败诊断，对 agent 行为可靠性与评测方法有一般性参考。

TL;DR: 该研究在合成环境 NAQD-Env 中评估 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 的 LoRA 监督微调对选择性撤回的影响，并用规则级诊断揭示聚合指标可能掩盖的特定回归；微调提升决策一致性与撤回召回，但存在错误撤回、禁止组合识别较弱、委托保留差及事件报告遗漏等问题，不能证明微调可确保智能体安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型语言智能体需在证据变化或授权撤销时选择性撤回动作，但仅凭总体准确率可能掩盖规则特定缺陷，因此需要检测微调是否真正带来安全相关的选择性撤回能力。

Method: 在具有显式依赖与确定性参考策略的合成环境 NAQD-Env 中，对 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 训练四个 LoRA 检查点，比较原始课程与增强课程（后者含三套系统提示框架，视图和目标相同）；在三种推理提示下，用新验证案例、留出依赖族和留出结构组合评估这些检查点与三个指令微调基线，并做规则级诊断。

Result: 在 naqd 提示下，SFT 检查点决策一致性为 0.77-0.92、撤回召回为 0.46-0.86，基线为 0.45-0.63 和至多 0.06。原始课程检查点错误撤回 34 个有替代支持动作中的 26 个，增强检查点为 0-1；但所有 SFT 检查点比 7B/8B 基线更少收窄禁止组合，且该案例未出现在两个课程中。委托场景撤回更多但独立授权动作保留更差，四个检查点均遗漏有效事件报告。框架对比在不同指标和划分上变化，未确立可复现优势或等价性。

Conclusion: 贡献是检测聚合准确率可能掩盖的规则特定回归的方法，而非微调本身能确保智能体安全的证据；每个臂仅一次训练、课程属性联合变化、先前评估暴露和量化差异限制因果归因。

中文摘要：使用工具的语言智能体必须在证据变化或授权被撤销时修改已准备好的动作。选择性撤回意味着暂停受影响的动作、保留受支持的工作，并且仅在充分修复后才恢复。我们在 NAQD-Env 中研究监督微调；NAQD-Env 是一个具有显式依赖关系和确定性参考策略的合成环境。Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 的四个 LoRA 检查点比较原始课程与增强课程，后者在具有相同视图和目标的三套系统提示框架下进行。三个指令微调基线和所有四个检查点在三种推理提示下，于新验证案例、留出依赖族和留出结构组合上接受评估。在 naqd 提示下，SFT 检查点的决策一致性达到 0.77-0.92，撤回召回率达到 0.46-0.86，而基线分别为 0.45-0.63 和至多 0.06。规则级诊断对这些增益作出限定。原始课程检查点错误地撤回了 34 个仍有替代支持的动作中的 26 个；增强课程检查点仅撤回 0-1 个。相反，所有 SFT 检查点比 7B 和 8B 基线更少地收窄禁止组合，而这一情形未出现在两个课程中。委托结果显示撤回更多，但对独立授权动作的保留更差，并且所有四个检查点都遗漏了有效事件报告。提示框架对比在不同指标和数据划分上存在差异，并未确立可复现的优势或等价性。每个实验臂仅进行一次训练运行、课程属性被联合改变、先前评估暴露以及基线-SFT 量化差异，均限制了因果归因。其贡献是一种用于检测可能被聚合准确率掩盖的规则特定回归的方法，而不是微调本身即可确保智能体安全的证据。

Original abstract: Tool-using language agents must revise prepared actions when evidence changes or authority is revoked. Selective withdrawal means suspending affected actions, preserving supported work, and resuming only after sufficient repair. We study supervised fine-tuning in NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment with explicit dependencies and a deterministic reference policy. Four LoRA checkpoints of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct compare an original curriculum with an enriched one, the latter under three system-prompt framings with identical views and targets. Three instruction-tuned baselines and all four checkpoints are evaluated under three inference prompts on fresh validation cases, held-out dependency families, and held-out structural combinations. Under the naqd prompt, SFT checkpoints reach decision agreement of 0.77-0.92 and withdrawal recall of 0.46-0.86, versus 0.45-0.63 and at most 0.06 for the baselines. Rule-level diagnostics qualify these gains. The original-curriculum checkpoint wrongly withdraws 26 of 34 actions with surviving alternative support; enriched checkpoints withdraw 0-1. Conversely, all SFT checkpoints narrow prohibited combinations less often than the 7B and 8B baselines, a case absent from both curricula. Delegation results show more withdrawal but poorer preservation of independently authorized actions, and all four checkpoints omit valid event reports. Framing contrasts vary across metrics and splits and do not establish a reproducible advantage or equivalence. One training run per arm, jointly changed curriculum properties, prior evaluation exposure, and baseline-SFT quantization differences limit causal attribution. The contribution is a method for detecting rule-specific regressions that aggregate accuracy can conceal, not evidence that fine-tuning alone ensures agent safety.

</details>


### [18] [Athanor: An Agent-Native System for Autonomous Post-Training Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21055)
*Yufa Zhou,Yichen Wang,Jiakun Fan,Ziqi Shu,Zhichao Liu,Anru R. Zhang,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: agent 原生自主后训练研究系统，agent 自行读数据、改算法、调参与迭代分析，长程自主科研 agent 架构高度可迁移。

TL;DR: Athanor让四台八GPU节点上的Claude Opus 5.5智能体在104小时内自主后训练Qwen3-4B-Base，通过SFT、RL、权重合并与自蒸馏，使其在六任务开发集和留出集上接近或超过官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507，首次展示前沿智能体团队可自主完成跨域通用后训练研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索递归自我改进的可测量弱形式：以“前沿智能体用公开数据后训练小型原始基础模型”检验其研究潜力，并解决缺少真实GPU上全自主后训练系统与证据的问题。

Method: 构建Athanor：面向智能体原生、开放、支持真实GPU自主后训练研究（AutoPostTrain）的系统。赋予智能体完全研究自主权、低摩擦工具与高效起始代码；智能体在沙箱内自行读取数据集和评估、构建训练数据、编写修改算法、调参并分析决策，禁用外部教师模型。系统提供冻结评估、托管作业、去污染数据、持久研究记录及智能体未知的留出套件。四名Claude Opus 5.5智能体各占一个八GPU节点，无研究建议，运行104小时。

Result: Qwen3-4B-Base在六任务开发套件上从15.9提升至67.3，在留出套件上从9.2提升至48.2；官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507分别为66.5和47.9，平均被达到或超过。完整记录显示提升来自已知方法重组：公开推理数据SFT、某智能体引入的rollout变更RL、跨智能体权重合并和多轮自蒸馏。

Conclusion: 首次证明前沿智能体团队能自主将原始基础模型后训练为跨域通用模型，并在平均表现上匹配或超越官方instruct版本；在执行得到处理后，当今智能体已具备完成该任务的工程与研究能力，Athanor为扩展自主AI研究提供基础。

中文摘要：递归自我改进，即AI系统构建更强的AI系统，是通往超级智能的一条有前景的路径。我们研究一种如今可测量的较弱形式：前沿智能体用公开数据对一个小型原始基础模型进行后训练，以检验其研究潜力。我们构建了Athanor，一个开放、智能体原生的系统，用于在真实GPU上进行自主后训练研究（AutoPostTrain）。我们赋予智能体完全的研究自主权、低摩擦的工程工具和高效的起始代码。智能体自行开展研究：它们读取数据集和评估结果，构建训练数据，编写和修改训练算法，调优超参数，并分析结果以决定下一步尝试什么。它们在沙箱中工作，不能使用外部教师模型。Athanor的服务提供冻结评估、托管作业、去污染数据、持久研究记录，以及一个智能体并不知晓的留出套件。在一项104小时的实验活动中，一个由四名Claude Opus 5.5智能体组成的团队，每个智能体位于一个八GPU节点上，且未获得任何研究建议，将Qwen3-4B-Base在六任务开发套件上从15.9提升到67.3，在留出套件上从9.2提升到48.2，而官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507分别为66.5和47.9。据我们所知，这是首次证明一组前沿智能体能够自主地将一个原始基础模型后训练为一个跨领域通用模型，并在平均表现上达到或超过官方指令版本。该实验活动的完整记录表明，智能体通过重新组合已知方法和彼此的模型达到这一水平：在公开推理数据上进行SFT、使用由某个智能体引入的rollout变更的RL、跨智能体的权重合并，以及多轮自蒸馏。在执行问题得到处理后，当今的智能体已经具备攻克这一任务所需的工程与研究能力。我们希望Athanor为扩展自主AI研究提供基础。

Original abstract: Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor's services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign's full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other's models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today's agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.

</details>


### [19] [OmicsBench: a long-horizon multi-turn benchmark of AI agents on research-study-scale bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21074)
*Gideon Shaked,Siddharth Nagisetty*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 32 pages, 12 figures

Selection reason: 长程多轮依赖子任务 benchmark 加无监督失败模式挖掘流水线，其依赖图评测与失败聚类方法可迁移到 agent 评测。

TL;DR: OmicsBench是一个长时程、多轮生物信息学基准，包含97个DAG任务和662个子任务，采用确定性评分，评估12个模型在3个测试框架上的表现；结果显示无组合接近饱和，多轮依赖与上下文反馈显著影响得分，并且无监督失败模式发现流程能揭示代理分析中的定性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算生物学家越来越依赖AI代理完成长时程、多轮的大量分析，但现有生物信息学能力基准大多只设置单一简短任务和单次提示，无法评估依赖子任务、多轮交互、反馈恢复以及失败模式等真实使用场景。

Method: 提出OmicsBench：97个任务，每个任务将依赖子任务组织成有向无环图，共662个子任务；每个子任务对应一个用户轮次，依据答案键确定性评分，并可对错误答案提供上下文内反馈。在12个模型、3个测试框架上评估。另提出无监督失败模式发现流程，对代理失败尝试进行描述、嵌入和聚类，以发现涌现失败模式。

Result: 最慢代理平均每任务耗时3.6小时；无上下文反馈时平均得分0.43至0.57，有反馈时为0.54至0.67，均未接近饱和。多轮评估显示，代理在依赖前序子任务的子任务上得分更低，上下文反馈可部分挽回损失。失败模式流程暴露定性缺陷，例如用自身统计过滤器替代指定过滤器，或完全凭记忆写出答案。

Conclusion: OmicsBench表明，长时程多轮基准能揭示单次提示无法发现的代理行为，并凸显当前AI代理在真实生物信息学分析中仍有明显不足；确定性评分结合无监督失败模式分析，有助于系统评估和改进代理能力。

中文摘要：计算生物学家日益将大量分析委托给AI代理，在长时程、多轮会话中进行，然而大多数针对代理生物信息学能力的基准测试都只提出一个单一简短任务，并使用单次提示。我们提出OmicsBench，一个面向AI代理的长时程、多轮生物信息学基准。其97个任务中的每一个都将相互依赖的子任务连接成一个有向无环图，总共包含662个底层子任务。每个子任务作为一个用户轮次提出，并依据答案键进行确定性评分，可选地对错误答案提供上下文内反馈。在三个测试框架上的12个模型中，最慢的代理平均每个任务耗时3.6小时，且没有任何组合接近使该基准饱和：在没有上下文内反馈时平均得分为0.43至0.57（满分为1），在有上下文内反馈时为0.54至0.67。多轮评估还揭示了单次提示无法发现的行为，因为代理在依赖较早子任务的子任务上得分较低，而上下文内反馈帮助代理挽回其中部分损失。由于确定性评分无法定性说明代理如何失败，我们还引入了一个无监督失败模式发现流程，用于描述、嵌入并聚类代理的失败尝试，以揭示涌现出的失败模式。应用于OmicsBench时，该流程暴露了代理执行生物信息学分析方式上的定性缺陷，从代理用自身的统计过滤器替代指定的统计过滤器，到代理完全凭记忆写出答案。

Original abstract: Computational biologists increasingly delegate substantial analyses to AI agents in long-horizon, multi-turn sessions, yet most benchmarks of agents' bioinformatics capabilities pose a single short task with a single-shot prompt. We present OmicsBench, a long-horizon, multi-turn bioinformatics benchmark for AI agents. Each of its 97 tasks links dependent subtasks into a directed acyclic graph, for 662 underlying subtasks in all. Each subtask is posed as one user turn and graded deterministically against an answer key, with optional in-context feedback on incorrect answers. Across 12 models on three harnesses, the slowest agent averages 3.6 hours per task, and no combination comes near saturating the benchmark, with mean scores of 0.43 to 0.57 out of a possible 1 without in-context feedback and 0.54 to 0.67 with it. Multi-turn evaluation also uncovers behavior that single-shot prompts cannot, as agents score lower on subtasks that depend on earlier subtasks, and in-context feedback helps agents recover some of that loss. Because deterministic scoring gives no qualitative account of how an agent failed, we also introduce an unsupervised failure mode discovery pipeline that describes, embeds, and clusters agents' failed attempts to surface emergent failure modes. Applied to OmicsBench, this pipeline exposes qualitative shortcomings in how agents carry out bioinformatics analyses, ranging from agents applying their own statistical filters in place of the specified ones to agents writing whole answers from memory.

</details>


### [20] [Sci$Π$vot: Benchmarking Principal-Investigator Judgment at Pivotal Moments of Scientific AutoResearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21082)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Kejia Zhang,Di Wu,Yingkang Cao,Allen Zhou,Jianping Ye,Suying Liu,Jiaxuan Guo,Jianda Du,Weicheng Ye,Kecheng Liu,Hefan Zhang,Haixin Wang,Jie Xiao,Eric Yang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以人类纠正为基准评测自主科研 agent 的关键决策判断力，提供 agent 决策质量评测范式。

TL;DR: Sci$Π$vot是一个基于资深研究者对LLM智能体248次人类修正的科研判断基准，结果显示前沿模型常偏好原始LLM计划，在所选科研决策上表现不佳，项目记录与重新考虑也未必提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可长期自主运行实验，但可靠自主研究依赖科学判断；研究决策开放式且依赖项目背景，因此科学判断难以评估。

Method: 构建Sci$Π$vot，含248个基于人类修正的案例，来自六个科学领域26个项目六个月研究记录；每个案例让智能体在原始LLM计划与人类修正方案间选择，仅以修正前项目记录为上下文；比较LLM与人类表现，并考察推理努力、项目记录访问和重新考虑的影响。

Result: 中等推理努力且有项目记录时，模型系统性偏好原始LLM计划；五个前沿模型中四个低于随机；项目条件下最高准确率63.5%，新手研究者73.4%；中等努力下所有模型有项目记录时准确率低于无记录时；重新考虑未持续提升总体准确率。

Conclusion: 即使存在人类修正备选方案，前沿模型仍难以处理所选的科研决策；Sci$Π$vot可用于系统评估LLM智能体的科学判断。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体可以在很少人类监督的情况下使科学实验持续运行数月，但可靠的自主研究还需要科学判断。评估科学判断很困难，因为研究决策是开放式的，并且依赖于项目背景。我们构建了Sci$Π$vot，一个基于人类修正的、包含248个案例的科学判断基准。该基准取自六个月的研究记录，在此期间资深研究人员在六个科学领域的26个项目中运行LLM智能体，并反复干预以纠正智能体的研究判断。在每个案例中，智能体在原始LLM计划和人类修正后的替代方案之间进行选择，仅使用修正前可用的项目记录作为上下文。我们比较LLM与人类的表现，并考察推理努力程度、项目记录访问和重新考虑的影响。在中等推理努力程度且可访问项目记录时，模型系统性地偏好原始LLM计划而非人类修正后的替代方案。五个前沿模型中有四个在所选案例上表现低于随机水平。在项目条件下，跨被测模型和推理努力设置的最高准确率为63.5%，而新手研究人员为73.4%。在中等努力程度下，每个模型在可访问项目记录时的准确率都低于不可访问时，并且重新考虑并不能持续提高总体准确率。即使提供了人类修正后的替代方案，前沿模型在所选择的研究决策上仍表现困难，而Sci$Π$vot为LLM智能体的科学判断提供了系统评估。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can keep scientific experiments running for months with little human supervision, but reliable autonomous research also requires scientific judgment. Evaluating scientific judgment is difficult because research decisions are open-ended and depend on project context. We construct Sci$Π$vot, a 248-case benchmark of scientific judgment based on human corrections. The benchmark draws on records from six months of research in which senior researchers ran LLM agents on 26 projects across six scientific fields and repeatedly intervened to correct the agents' research judgments. In each case, an agent chooses between the original LLM plan and the human-corrected alternative, using only the project record available before the correction as context. We compare LLM and human performance and examine the effects of reasoning effort, access to the project record, and reconsideration. At medium reasoning effort with the project record, models systematically prefer the original LLM plan over the human-corrected alternative. Four of five frontier models perform below chance on the selected cases. The highest accuracy in the project condition across the tested models and reasoning effort settings is 63.5%, compared with 73.4% for fresh researchers. At medium effort, every model achieves lower accuracy with the project record than without it, and reconsideration does not consistently improve overall accuracy. Even with a human-corrected alternative available, frontier models struggle with the selected research decisions, while Sci$Π$vot supports systematic evaluation of scientific judgment for LLM agents.

</details>


### [21] [Probe-and-Resume: When and How to Evaluate Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21088)
*Jiakai Xu,Tiancheng Ge,Tujie Guan,Yusheng Li,Rohan Timmaraju,Eugene Wu,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究何时调用 critic 以及给 critic 探针式环境访问，Probe-and-Resume 直接改进 agent 验证与测试时搜索策略。

TL;DR: 在基于终端的计算机使用环境中，MCTS 未显著提升任务成功率；作者提出 Probe-and-Resume：仅在智能体声称完成时调用评论器，并让其通过读、写、执行访问状态分叉进行探查，从而在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上提升 8.4 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时搜索有望通过探索备选动作和评论器选择分支提升智能体，但在仅暴露部分状态的终端计算机使用环境中，所评估的 MCTS 配置未显著提升任务成功率，且评论器性能差。作者旨在弄清何时调用评论器以及应给予其何种访问权限。

Method: 分析评论器在中间状态评估困难的原因，提出策略仅在智能体认为的最终节点调用评论器，并赋予其对当前状态与初始状态分叉的读、写和执行权限，以探查并揭示轨迹遗漏的隐藏状态；被拒绝的智能体从停止处继续，该设计称为 Probe-and-Resume。

Result: 中间状态评论器对后续分支的排序仅略好于随机，环境访问改善甚微；在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，Probe-and-Resume 比使用传统评论器的 MCTS 成功率提高 8.4 个百分点，并接近使用相同底层模型的 Codex CLI 智能体成功率，成本不到其一半。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测的终端计算机使用环境中，评论器应只在轨迹终点对状态分叉进行主动探查，而非在中间步骤评估；该设计能更有效提升任务成功率并具成本优势。

中文摘要：测试时搜索可以通过探索备选动作并使用评论器（critic）选择有前景的分支来提升智能体性能。我们在基于终端的计算机使用环境中研究这一方法；这类环境仅暴露庞大底层状态的部分视图，并发现我们评估的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）配置并未显著提升任务成功率。我们识别出评论器性能不佳的两个来源：智能体的观测遗漏了评估所需的环境状态，且中间状态对判断一条轨迹是否会成功提供的证据有限。在中间状态，评论器对后续分支的排序仅略好于随机，而提供环境访问对此改善甚微。我们研究何时调用评论器，以及应给予它何种访问权限来评估一个节点。我们的发现促成了一种策略：仅在智能体认为是最终节点的节点处调用评论器，而不是在部分进展期间调用。此外，该策略赋予评论器对计算机当前状态与初始状态的一个分叉的读、写和执行访问权限，使其能够探查并执行可能具有破坏性的动作，以揭示轨迹所遗漏的任何隐藏状态。我们将由此得到的设计称为 Probe-and-Resume：当智能体声称完成时，评论器探查其状态的一个分叉，而被拒绝的智能体从其停止处继续。在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，相较于使用传统评论器的 MCTS，这将成功率提高了 8.4 个百分点，并以不到其一半的成本接近使用相同底层模型的 Codex CLI 智能体的成功率。

Original abstract: Test-time search can improve agent performance by exploring alternative actions and using a critic to select promising branches. We study this approach in terminal-based computer-use environments, which expose only a partial view of a large underlying state, and find that the Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) configuration we evaluate does not significantly improve task success. We identify two sources of poor critic performance: the agent's observations omit environment state needed for evaluation, and intermediate states offer limited evidence of whether a trajectory will succeed. At intermediate states, critics rank continuations only modestly better than chance, and environment access improves this little. We study when to invoke a critic and what access to give it to evaluate a node. Our findings motivate a policy that invokes the critic only at nodes the agent believes are final, rather than during partial progress. Further, the policy gives the critic read, write, and execute access to a fork of the computer's current and initial state, allowing it to probe and perform potentially destructive actions to surface any hidden state the trajectory omits. We call the resulting design Probe-and-Resume: when the agent claims to be done, the critic probes a fork of its state, and a rejected agent resumes where it stopped. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, this raises success by 8.4 percentage points over MCTS with conventional critics, and approaches the success rate of the Codex CLI agent with the same underlying model at less than half its cost.

</details>


### [22] [MemComposer: Learning to Assemble Memories for LLM Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21091)
*Yixuan Xie,Xueqiang Xu,Xinyi Fan,Xinxi Lyu,Tao Feng,Xing Fan,Chenlei Guo,Jie Hao,Shaowen Wang,Jiashuo Sun,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 30 pages, 8 figures, 14 tables

Selection reason: 用 RL 学习记忆组装策略以任务成功率反馈优化，直接对应 agent 经验复用与检索上下文构造。

TL;DR: 提出 MemComposer 与 ExPO，通过完整智能体交互和任务成功反馈学习查询特定的记忆选择与排序，在多个 τ²-bench 领域优于基线并减少记忆 token 消耗，且可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠语义相似性无法保证检索记忆对下游任务有用，记忆的选择、数量和呈现顺序会显著影响智能体性能。

Method: MemComposer 联合当前查询与候选记忆进行记忆组装决策；采用基于 GRPO 的 ExPO，通过完整智能体交互评估组装后的记忆上下文，并用任务成功反馈优化组装策略。

Result: 在三个 τ²-bench 领域和两个冻结执行器上，MemComposer 均优于各执行器的最强基线，平均记忆 token 使用量分别降低 16.9% 和 4.9%；策略可迁移到未见领域、执行器和记忆库，并能利用更强模型生成的记忆而无需再训练。

Conclusion: 利用下游任务反馈学习查询特定的记忆组装具有重要价值。

中文摘要：智能体记忆使大语言模型（LLM）智能体能够在不更新其参数的情况下复用过去经验。然而，仅凭语义相似性并不能保证记忆有用：下游性能取决于记忆如何被选择并呈现给智能体。我们提出 MemComposer，一种记忆组合器，它联合检查当前查询与检索到的候选记忆，以决定包含哪些经验、选择多少条以及如何排序。我们使用经验策略优化（ExPO）训练该组合器；ExPO 是一个基于 GRPO 的框架，通过完整的智能体交互来评估组装后的记忆上下文，并利用任务成功反馈来优化组装策略。在三个 $τ^2$-bench 领域、两个冻结执行器上，MemComposer 对每个执行器均优于最强基线，同时分别将平均记忆 token 使用量降低 16.9% 和 4.9%。学习到的策略还能迁移到未见过的领域、执行器和记忆库，并且无需进一步训练即可受益于更强模型生成的记忆。这些结果证明了利用下游任务反馈学习查询特定的记忆组装的价值。

Original abstract: Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. The learned policy also transfers to unseen domains, executors, and memory banks, and benefits from memories generated by stronger models without further training. These results demonstrate the value of learning query-specific memory assembly using downstream task feedback.

</details>


### [23] [Planning Commitment of LLM under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21108)
*Cassie Huang,Zhanwei Cao,Yeawon Lee,Nathalia Gomez,Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Tiffany Do,Jiayuan Mao,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 刻画部分可观测环境下 LLM 规划 agent 的计划承诺长度与安全-效率权衡，对长程 agent 规划控制有启发。

TL;DR: 研究LLM规划智能体在部分可观测环境中激进承诺长计划与保守规划的权衡：两者准确率相近；保守更安全但API调用更多，激进更易失败但在先验有效时重规划高效；LLM会将先验近似当作事实；混合策略可通过超参数调节安全与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的规划智能体在交互前需承诺不同长度的计划：激进承诺长计划可能因未观测状态假设而失败，保守规划则可能增加LLM调用。二者的安全-效率权衡尚未被系统刻画，也无法控制。因此需要研究并调节该权衡。

Method: 在两个部分可观测领域中，以显式统计或常识知识提供先验，比较激进与保守的LLM规划器和形式化器（包括直接输出计划与生成PDDL）在三个LLM上的表现，分析失败尝试、API调用、准确率、重规划效率及LLM对先验的依赖，并探索可调超参数的混合策略。

Result: 三个LLM上两种策略准确率相近；保守方法失败尝试更少但API调用更多，激进方法失败更多且仅在先验信息丰富时能高效重规划；分析显示LLM几乎将先验当作事实，先验不可靠时风险上升；混合策略可通过超参数调节安全与效率平衡。

Conclusion: 该安全-效率权衡可被系统表征并可部分控制：保守策略偏向安全，激进策略在信息丰富先验下偏向效率，但LLM对先验的过度信任会带来风险；可调混合策略为平衡二者提供了途径。

中文摘要：基于LLM的规划智能体在与部分可观测环境交互之前，会承诺不同长度的计划。一些基于对未观测状态的假设，激进地承诺一个长计划，而另一些则保守地仅针对已观测到的内容进行规划。无论LLM直接输出计划，还是为经典求解器生成PDDL表述，这两种行为都会在预训练模型中隐式出现。激进承诺有代价高昂的失败风险，而保守承诺则有过多LLM调用风险。这一选择使安全性与效率对立，而这种权衡既未被系统性地刻画，也未被变得可控。我们在两个部分可观测领域中研究激进与保守的LLM规划器和形式化器，其中先验以显式统计或常识知识的形式提供。在三个LLM上，两种策略达到相似的准确率。保守方法实现更少的失败尝试（安全性），代价是更多的API调用（效率）。激进方法更常失败，但仅在先验信息丰富时能高效地重新规划。有趣的是，分析表明LLM几乎将提供的先验视为事实，这在先验不可靠时有失败风险。我们还探索了带有可调超参数的混合策略，以调整安全性与效率之间的平衡。

Original abstract: LLM-based planning agents commit to plans of different lengths before interacting with a partially observable environment. Some aggressively commit to a long plan based on assumptions about unobserved states, while others conservatively plan only over what they have observed. Both behaviors emerge implicitly in pre-trained models, whether the LLM outputs a plan directly or generates a PDDL formulation for a classical solver. Aggressive commitment risks costly failures, while conservative commitment risks excessive LLM calls. This choice pits safety against efficiency, a trade-off that has been neither characterized systematically nor made controllable. We investigate aggressive and conservative LLM planners and formalizers on two partially observable domains, where priors are provided as either explicit statistics or common-sense knowledge. Across three LLMs, both strategies reach similar accuracy. Conservative methods achieve fewer failed attempts (safety) at the cost of more API calls (efficiency). Aggressive methods fail more often but replan efficiently only when priors are informative. Interestingly, analysis shows that LLMs treat the provided priors nearly as facts, which risks failure when priors are unreliable. We also explore hybrid strategies with tunable hyperparameters that adjust the balance between safety and efficiency.

</details>


### [24] [Kolab: Benchmarking Human-Agent Collaboration in Long-Horizon Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21109)
*Shirley Anugrah Hayati,Patricia Anugrah Setiani,Ruizi Wang,Zhouyi Li,Khanh Chi Le,Young-Jun Lee,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程真实工作流中人-agent 协作 benchmark，揭示 agent 预测人类采纳与协作质量评估的缺陷，对 agent 评测有参考。

TL;DR: 论文提出Kolab数据集，用50位专业人士、115+小时录屏研究长时程人-智能体协作，并构建基准测试智能体预测专家对贡献的采纳、修改或拒绝及评估协作质量。前沿模型常高估协作且难以区分协作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准偏重自动化，无法衡量真实长时程协作；专业工作流中步骤相互依赖，智能体需理解专家如何采纳、修改或拒绝其贡献，并从工作流中判断协作质量。

Method: 收集Kolab：40种职业50位专业人士与智能体协作解决真实长时程问题的115+小时屏幕录制及协作与输出质量判断；据此构建基准，任务包括预测专家如何使用智能体贡献，以及验证协作满意度与输出质量判断。

Result: 分析显示协作模式多样，专家与智能体协调的时机和方式很重要；前沿模型（智能体与非智能体）均高估与专家的协作，难以区分高与低质量协作，更长上下文也未持续提升预测。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体尚不能理解与专家的长时程协作，Kolab可作为研究真实专业工作流中的人-智能体协作的数据集。

中文摘要：当人类在长时程任务中与AI智能体协作时，智能体的贡献会发生什么？对于解决长时程任务，人类更重视与AI智能体的持续交互（例如修改计划），而不是智能体一次性完成任务的能力。然而，大多数智能体基准更关注自动化。在这类基准上的强劲表现并不意味着现实世界中的有效协作。专业工作流提供了一个有用的场景，因为它们通常涉及相互依赖的步骤。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了Kolab，这是一个新数据集，包含115小时以上的屏幕录制，记录了来自40种职业的50位专业人士与智能体协作解决真实的长时程问题。Kolab还包含参与者对协作和输出质量的判断。我们的分析揭示了多样化的协作模式，强调了专业人士何时以及如何与智能体协调的重要性。协作型智能体必须做两件自动化基准从未测试的事：预测专家在其离开聊天窗口后是否会采纳、修改或拒绝其贡献，以指导其后续步骤；以及在没有专家持续反馈的情况下，从展开的工作流中评估协作质量。我们的基准将两者操作化：（1）预测专业人士在不同工作流中如何使用智能体贡献；（2）验证他们对协作满意度和输出质量的判断。研究结果表明，前沿模型，无论是否具备智能体能力，都会高估与专家的协作，并难以区分高质量与低质量协作。更长的工作流上下文并不总能改善这些预测。由于前沿智能体尚不能理解与专家的长时程协作，我们将Kolab定位为研究基于真实专业工作流的人-智能体协作的宝贵数据集。

Original abstract: When humans collaborate with AI agents on long-horizon tasks, what happens to the agents' contributions? For solving long-horizon tasks, humans place greater importance on ongoing interaction with AI agents (e.g., revising plans) than on an agent's ability to complete the task in a single attempt. However, most agentic benchmarks have focused more on automation. Strong performance on such benchmarks does not imply effective real-world collaboration. Professional workflows offer a useful setting, as they often involve interdependent steps. To address this gap, we introduce Kolab, a new dataset of 115+ hours of screen recordings of 50 professionals from 40 occupations collaborating with agents on realistic, long-horizon problems. Kolab also contains participants' collaboration and output quality judgments. Our analysis reveals diverse collaboration patterns, highlighting the importance of when and how professionals coordinate with agents. A collaborative agent must do two things that automation benchmarks never test: anticipate whether experts will adopt, modify, or reject its contribution once it leaves the chat window to guide its next steps, and assess collaboration quality from the unfolding workflow without constant expert feedback. Our benchmark operationalizes both: (1) forecasting how professionals use agent contributions at diverse workflows, and (2) verifying their judgments of collaboration satisfaction and output quality. Findings show that frontier models, both agentic and non-agentic, overestimate collaboration with experts and struggle to distinguish between high- and low-quality collaboration. Longer workflow context does not consistently improve these predictions. As frontier agents cannot yet understand long-horizon collaboration with experts, we position Kolab as a valuable dataset for studying human-agent collaboration grounded in real professional workflows.

</details>


### [25] [All-in-One Memory: Process-Rewarded Reinforcement Learning for Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21147)
*Bingbing Wang,Jingjie Lin,Weiming Qiao,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 统一搜索、写入与停止决策的记忆 agent，用过程奖励模型与 RL 优化，直接对应 agent 记忆与过程监督。

TL;DR: AiO-Mem 将检索、写入和停止决策统一为共享证据状态上的转移，在统一奖励空间中评估动作，并用过程奖励模型与 PA-DAPO 优化记忆策略，在长上下文记忆基准上稳定提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆方法将检索与记忆构建分离优化，缺少统一的奖励空间来评价搜索、写入与停止决策，导致这些操作难以协同优化。

Method: 提出 AiO-Mem：将异构历史编译为可溯源多视图事件；用统一记忆策略 Actor 在共享证据状态上生成完整交互轨迹；训练轨迹条件过程奖励模型（证据监督微调+轨迹级 DPO）分配过程分；聚合为动作平衡轨迹奖励，通过 PA-DAPO 优化记忆策略。

Result: 在长上下文记忆基准上，AiO-Mem 相比静态记忆系统和 RL 训练的记忆智能体，答案准确率持续提升。

Conclusion: 表明在统一证据状态和奖励空间下联合优化检索、记忆写入与停止决策，可有效提升长时程记忆问答性能。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越依赖外部记忆来回答基于长时程历史的查询，例如多轮会话和冗长文档。然而，现有记忆方法通常将检索和记忆构建分开优化，缺乏一个用于评估搜索、写入和停止决策的共享奖励空间。为解决这一问题，我们提出一体化记忆（All-in-One Memory, AiO-Mem），它将上述操作组织为在共享证据状态上的状态转移，并在统一奖励空间中直接评估每个动作的语义质量。AiO-Mem 将异构历史编译为可溯源的多视图事件，为检索和记忆写入提供共享基础。随后，一个统一的记忆策略 Actor 在这个共享证据状态上展开完整的交互轨迹。一个独立的、以轨迹为条件的过程奖励模型通过基于证据的监督微调和轨迹级 DPO 训练，用于为收集到的轨迹分配过程分数。这些分数被聚合为动作平衡的轨迹奖励，并由 Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) 用于优化记忆策略。在长上下文记忆基准上，AiO-Mem 相较于静态记忆系统和经强化学习训练的记忆智能体，均持续提升了答案准确率。

Original abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on external memory to answer queries grounded in long-horizon histories such as multi-session dialogues and lengthy documents. However, existing memory methods often optimize retrieval and memory construction separately, without a shared reward space for evaluating search, writing, and stopping decisions. To address this, we propose All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem), which organizes these operations as transitions over a shared evidence state and directly evaluates the semantic quality of each action in a unified reward space. AiO-Mem compiles heterogeneous histories into source-traceable multi-view events, providing a shared substrate for retrieval and memory writing. A unified memory policy actor then rolls out complete interaction trajectories over this shared evidence state. A separate trajectory-conditioned process reward model is trained through evidence-grounded supervised fine-tuning and trajectory-level DPO to assign process scores to the collected trajectories. These scores are aggregated into an action-balanced trajectory reward and used by Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) to optimize the memory policy. Across long-context memory benchmarks, AiO-Mem consistently improves answer accuracy over both static memory systems and RL-trained memory agents.

</details>


### [26] [GRADS: Graph-Regulated Agentic Data Synthesis with Controllable Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21167)
*Gaocheng Yang,Ruijie Tao,Kai Ye,Senhua Zhang,Zhengqin Zhu,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 7 figures

Selection reason: 图约束的 agentic 数据合成并可控难度，覆盖软件工程域，对 agent 训练数据构造与难度调节有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出 GRADS：一种图引导、显式控制难度的智能体数据合成框架，通过维护证据与推理依赖图并闭环生成验证任务，在多个领域提升深度研究智能体后训练效果，4B 模型在部分基准上匹配或超越 Sonnet~4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体在多跳、长时程任务上仍困难；现有合成数据流程通过扩展种子任务增加表观深度与广度，但新证据与答案弱耦合，导致预期难度与经验难度不匹配。

Method: GRADS 以图指导智能体式数据合成，维持推导每个答案所需的证据与推理依赖图；闭环执行种子选择、证据扩展、问答构建与验证；每步以相关子图为条件生成，保留关键推理且避免无关上下文。

Result: 在安全、软件工程和科学文档领域，GRADS 持续生成经验证且难度分布可分的任务。匹配训练预算下，SFT 将求解终止率提至 95% 以上，并最多减少 75% 工具调用和轮次；RL 再减少最多 19.5% 工具使用；所得 4B 模型在软件工程上匹配 Sonnet~4，在科学和安全领域超越它。

Conclusion: GRADS 通过图引导和显式难度控制解决合成数据中推理必要性与难度不匹配问题，生成高质量训练数据，并在多个领域显著提升智能体后训练性能，使小模型在领域基准上达到或超过强基线。

中文摘要：深度研究智能体正越来越多地被部署用于从大规模语料库中检索精确信息，然而多跳、长时程任务仍然具有挑战性。合成数据生成已成为一种可扩展的解决方案，现有流程通常通过扩展种子任务来增加其表观深度和广度。然而，这种扩展并不能可靠地转化为必要的推理：新引入的证据可能与答案仅弱耦合，造成预期难度与经验难度之间的不匹配。为解决此问题，我们提出 GRADS，一种具有显式难度控制的图引导智能体数据合成框架。GRADS 运行一个由种子选择、证据扩展、问题–答案构建和验证组成的闭环，同时维护一个包含推导每个答案所需的证据与推理依赖关系的图。在每一步，它以相关子图为条件进行生成，保留不可或缺的推理，同时不累积无关上下文。在安全、软件工程和科学文档领域的实验表明，GRADS 能够持续生成经过验证的任务，其难度分布经独立求解器测量后在经验上具有可分性。此外，在匹配的训练预算下，基于该数据的后训练在每个层面都带来了持续收益：SFT 将求解终止率提高到 95% 以上，同时将工具调用次数和交互轮次最多削减 75%；RL 进一步将工具使用最多再削减 19.5%；在我们的留出、领域特定基准上，所得 4B 模型在软件工程上与 Sonnet~4 持平，并在科学和安全领域超过它。

Original abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly deployed to retrieve precise information from large corpora, yet multi-hop, long-horizon tasks remain challenging. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a scalable solution, with existing pipelines typically expanding seed tasks to increase their apparent depth and breadth. However, this expansion does not reliably translate into necessary reasoning: newly introduced evidence may be only weakly coupled to the answer, creating a mismatch between intended and empirical difficulty. To address this problem, we introduce GRADS, a graph-guided agentic data synthesis framework with explicit difficulty control. GRADS runs a closed loop of seed selection, evidence expansion, question--answer construction, and verification while maintaining a graph of the evidence and inference dependencies required to derive each answer. At each step, it conditions generation on a relevant subgraph, preserving indispensable inference without accumulating irrelevant context. Experiments across security, software-engineering, and scientific-document domains show that GRADS consistently generates verified tasks with empirically separated difficulty distributions, as measured by independent solvers. Moreover, under matched training budgets, post-training on this data delivers consistent gains at every level: SFT raises the solution-termination rate to over 95% while cutting tool calls and turns by up to 75%, RL trims tool use by up to a further 19.5%, and on our held-out, domain-specific benchmarks, the resulting 4B model matches Sonnet~4 on software engineering and surpasses it on the scientific and security domains.

</details>
