### [27] [MemScope: Evolving Agent Memory Architectures through Localized Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21170)
*Miao Su,Yucan Guo,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用工具型 Patch Agent 依据原始执行轨迹与运行时状态对 agent 记忆架构代码做定位式补丁修复与原地演化，直接对应代码级自动优化与轨迹反馈复用。

TL;DR: MemScope利用原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态和架构代码进行有界原地修复，实现智能体记忆架构的局部化演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆架构部署后固定，且自演化方法多依赖评估分数或压缩摘要等粗粒度反馈，难以精确定位长时程多组件系统中的失败，导致修改目标不明确、更新范围失控和大规模重写。

Method: 提出MemScope，其使用工具的Patch Agent联合检查原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态和当前架构代码，在提供者身份与记忆兼容性约束下识别修复目标并进行有界原地编辑。

Result: GAIA上轨迹与记忆检查变体准确率86.67%，优于仅轨迹70.00%和仅摘要63.33%；xBench上DeepSeek V3.2从49提升至55，Kimi K2.7 Code从50提升至53；ALFWorld未见任务成功率从50.75%升至71.64%；归档架构快照转换的代码变更比例中位数为4.4%。

Conclusion: 细粒度执行证据可作为局部化、原地记忆架构演化的基础。

中文摘要：智能体记忆架构定义了LLM智能体如何跨任务存储和复用经验，但它们通常在部署后即固定，限制了其跨任务类型的适应性。近期的自演化方法转而利用执行反馈来适配这些记忆架构，这类反馈通常表示为评估分数或压缩摘要。然而，在长时程任务执行涉及多个交互组件的当前智能体系统中，这种粗粒度反馈不足以进行精确的故障定位。没有精确的故障位置，现有方法缺乏明确的代码级修改目标，导致更新范围约束较弱，并常常对原本性能良好的记忆架构进行大规模重写。我们提出MemScope，一个用于对现有记忆架构进行基于证据的局部化演化的框架。其使用工具的Patch Agent联合检查原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态和当前架构代码，以识别修复目标，并在提供者身份和记忆兼容性约束下应用有界的原地编辑。在GAIA上，轨迹与记忆检查变体达到86.67%的准确率，而仅轨迹反馈为70.00%，仅摘要反馈为63.33%。在xBench任务上，演化后的记忆架构相比原始版本，在使用DeepSeek V3.2时正确回答从49提升到55，在使用Kimi K2.7 Code时从50提升到53。在不相交的ALFWorld未见任务上，成功率从50.75%上升到71.64%。在归档的架构快照转换中，代码变更比例中位数为4.4%。这些结果支持将细粒度执行证据作为局部化、原地记忆架构演化的基础。

Original abstract: Agent memory architectures define how LLM agents store and reuse experience across tasks, but they are typically fixed after deployment, limiting their adaptability across task types. Recent self-evolving approaches instead adapt these memory architectures using execution feedback, typically represented as evaluation scores or compressed summaries. However, such coarse-grained feedback is insufficient for precise failure localization in current agent systems, where long-horizon task execution involves multiple interacting components. Without a precise failure location, existing approaches lack a clear code-level target for modification, leaving the update scope weakly constrained and often leading to large-scale rewrites of an otherwise capable memory architecture. We propose MemScope, a framework for evidence-grounded, localized evolution of an existing memory architecture. Its tool-using Patch Agent jointly inspects raw execution traces, runtime memory state, and current architecture code to identify repair targets and apply bounded, in-place edits under provider-identity and memory-compatibility constraints. On GAIA, the trace-and-memory inspection variant achieves 86.67% accuracy, compared with 70.00% for trace-only and 63.33% for summary-only feedback. Under xBench tasks, the evolved memory architecture improves over its original version from 49 to 55 correct answers with DeepSeek V3.2 and from 50 to 53 with Kimi K2.7 Code. On disjoint ALFWorld unseen tasks, success rises from 50.75% to 71.64%. Across archived architecture-snapshot transitions, the median code-change ratio is 4.4%. These results support fine-grained execution evidence as a basis for localized, in-place memory-architecture evolution.

</details>


### [28] [AgentEval: Evidence-Bounded Assessment of Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21177)
*Ruiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 提出有状态 agent 的契约约束证据化评估记录，将行为、证据与结论可核查地分离，对 agent 验证与可靠性评测有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: AgentEval 通过受契约约束的评估记录，将智能体行为、证据与结论分离，并在多项研究中验证义务判定、证据有效性和报告复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务完成不等于义务满足，已记录判定也不保证支持证据持续有效；需要可追溯、可检查的评估机制。

Method: 提出 AgentEval，贯穿工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告资格认定和案例维护；用 Scenario Studio 连接审查案例与报告。

Result: 32次工具世界管理中独立检查在30个完整回合一致，区分满足/违反/未解决义务，保留2次中断；48个证据视图干预暴露36个哈希检查遗漏的绑定故障；12份两轮报告含24个独立核查答案；192次留出执行揭示10个已完成返回子目标并分离缺失证据影响。

Conclusion: 可执行评估记录使范围、证据支持与报告复用在整个智能体评估工作流中可检查。

中文摘要：仅凭任务完成并不能判定一个有状态智能体是否履行了其义务，而一条记录下来的判定也不能保证其支持证据仍然有效。我们提出 AgentEval，一个系统，它使受契约约束的评估记录贯穿工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告资格认定以及案例维护。它将智能体做了什么、证据确立了什​​么，以及结论在当前使用中是否得到支持区分开来。在32次工具世界管理实例中，独立检查在所有30个完整回合上一致，区分已满足、已违反和未解决的义务；两次被中断的管理实例被保留。对48个证据视图的受控干预暴露了36个仅依赖哈希的检查所遗漏的绑定故障，且不改变智能体行为。Scenario Studio 将经过审查的案例连接到12份两轮报告，其中包含24个独立核查的答案。一项独立的公共基准研究在192次留出执行中揭示了更广泛任务失败中的十个已完成返回子目标，并分离出缺失证据的影响。这些研究表明，可执行评估记录如何使范围、证据支持和报告复用在整个智能体评估工作流中可检查。

Original abstract: Task completion alone does not determine whether a stateful agent satisfied its obligations, and a recorded judgment does not guarantee that its supporting evidence remains valid. We present AgentEval, a system that carries a contract-bound assessment record through tool execution, state and event capture, report qualification, and case maintenance. It separates what the agent did, what the evidence establishes, and whether the conclusion is supported for present use. On 32 tool-world administrations, independent checks agree on all 30 complete episodes, distinguishing satisfied, violated, and unresolved obligations; two interrupted administrations are retained. Controlled interventions on 48 evidence views expose 36 binding faults missed by hash-only checks without changing agent behavior. Scenario Studio connects reviewed cases to 12 two-turn reports with 24 independently checked answers. A separate public-benchmark study on 192 held-out executions reveals ten completed return subgoals within broader task failures and isolates the effect of missing evidence. These studies show how an executable assessment record makes scope, evidential support, and report reuse inspectable across an agent evaluation workflow.

</details>


### [29] [Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21203)
*Lan Wei,Bingyu Shen,Jiaqi Tang,Qing Lu,Kuan Huang,Meng Xu,Miles Q. Li,Bin Hu,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages, 7 figures, 13 tables. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/standing27/

Selection reason: 研究多主体对话中承诺的授权释放与越权执行，并给出自我维护 commitment ledger 的可靠控制手段，对 agent 权限与状态一致性设计有启发。

TL;DR: 研究助手承诺的取消资格（standing），区分未经授权解除与执行已解除承诺（override），发现模型普遍违背债权人，并测试 oracle、承诺账本等干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 助手在团队中作出承诺时，取消权限常被忽视；需要明确谁有权解除承诺，并测量模型在规则、身份和政策线索下的两类失败。

Method: 在受控聊天环境中操纵取消者，区分 unauthorized release 与 override；测试六个模型，并比较规则、债权人、承诺是否仍欠等 oracle、思考模式、承诺账本和一段式解除政策。

Result: 较小开源模型 override 债权人（Qwen3.5-9B：69%）；较大模型因同伴直接发话而放弃承诺（Qwen3.8-27B：89%，gpt-6.1-sol：83%）；几乎都服从旁观者。规则/债权人 oracle 降低 unauthorized release 但不降 override，债权人 oracle 在 3/4 模型反升 override；只有“是否仍欠承诺” oracle 降 override。启用思考类似扩大规模。承诺账本在 4/5 模型阻止 override；加一段政策达到或超过最佳 oracle 存储，gpt-6-luna 严格准确率 49%→83%。

Conclusion: 仅靠规则或身份提示不足以解决承诺解除问题；关于承诺是否仍有效的状态信息、承诺账本与释放政策更有效；规模与思考有帮助但不一致；发布代码、提示、输出和标签以支持可复现研究。

中文摘要：当为团队服务的助手向 Alice 作出承诺时，谁可以取消它？我们称此为 standing（资格）：无论级别如何，只有债权人（被承诺方）或债权人/运营方政策授权的人可以解除承诺。在受控聊天环境中，我们变化由谁取消，并区分两类错误：未经授权解除（服从没有 standing 的人）和 override（执行一个已被解除的承诺）。在六个模型中，较小的开源模型会越过债权人（Qwen3.5-9B：69%）；较大的模型会因同伴的直接发话而放弃承诺（Qwen3.8-27B：89%；gpt-6.1-sol：83%）；几乎所有模型都会按旁观者“还是继续吧”行事。Oracles（神谕信息）分离了这两类错误：说明规则或说明债权人能抑制未经授权解除，但不能抑制 override；其中，债权人 oracle 甚至在四分之三的模型中提高了 override；而在这些模型中，只有知道承诺是否仍然欠着才能降低 override。启用思考会以类似规模扩大（scale）的方式推动较小模型。自我保存的承诺账本在五分之四的模型中阻止 override；配合一段话的解除政策，它达到或超过最佳 oracle 存储，将 gpt-6-luna 的严格准确率从 49%（仅政策）提升到 83%。代码、提示、输出和标签均已发布。

Original abstract: When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.

</details>


### [30] [AutoOnboarder: Autonomously Onboarding LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21218)
*Tanay Arora,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: Onboarder coding agent 自动迭代调整基础模型、提示与 harness 源码以提升性能并降本，属于典型的 agent harness 自动优化与自我改进。

TL;DR: AutoOnboarder 通过一个 Onboarder 编码智能体迭代调整基础模型、提示词与 harness 源码，在基因组测序预授权表单提交任务上以更低成本达到或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，但性能波动且仍不完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式大型语言模型放入 harness 后常出现推理能力下降；需要一种能像新员工入职一样自动适配任务并兼顾性能与成本的方案。

Method: 提出 AutoOnboarder：由智能 Onboarder 编码智能体监督，仅用数十个示例，迭代优化基础模型、提示词和 harness 源代码；评估数据隔离在独立虚拟机以防泄漏。

Result: 在基因组测序预授权表单提交任务上，自主入职智能体可匹配或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，成本最多降低 29 倍；但智能体仍不完美，最终性能波动大。

Conclusion: 未来 Onboarding 系统需为 Onboarder 提供稳健环境，包括结构化思维接口、受控互联网访问和广泛的模型搜索空间。

中文摘要：已有记录表明，智能体式大型语言模型在置于 harness（即中介其观察和行动的软件）内部时会丧失推理能力。为弥补这些损失，我们提出 AutoOnboarder，一个自主系统，它像培训师让新员工入职一样使智能体适应任务。它将智能体置于一个智能“Onboarder”编码智能体的监督之下，该编码智能体迭代地调整其基础模型、提示词和 harness 源代码，以同时提升性能并降低成本。与真实入职流程类似，Onboarder 仅获得数十个示例，并且所有评估数据都隔离在单独的虚拟机上以防泄漏。我们报告在基因组测序预先授权表格提交任务上构建和测试 AutoOnboarder 所得到的经验教训。自主入职的智能体能够以最多降低 29 倍的成本，匹配或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线的性能。然而，这些智能体仍不完美，且 Onboarding 过程在最终性能方面差异很大。我们得出结论：未来的 Onboarding 系统需要为 Onboarder 提供稳健的环境，包括结构化思维接口、（受控的）互联网访问以及广泛的模型搜索空间。

Original abstract: Agentic large language models have been documented to lose reasoning capability when placed inside a harness, the software that mediates their observations and actions. To reconcile these losses, we introduce AutoOnboarder, an autonomous system that adapts an agent to a task the way a trainer onboards a new employee. It places the agent under the supervision of an intelligent ``Onboarder'' coding agent, which iteratively tailors its foundation model, prompt, and harness source code to simultaneously improve performance and decrease cost. Like a real onboarding process, the Onboarder is only given tens of examples, and all evaluation data is isolated on a separate virtual machine to prevent leakage. We report the lessons learned from building and testing AutoOnboarder on genomic-sequencing prior authorization form submission. Autonomously onboarded agents are able to match or exceed the performance of a Gemini 3.5 Flash baseline at up to 29$\times$ lower costs. However, these agents remain imperfect, and the Onboarding process varies greatly in terms of final performance. We conclude that future Onboarding systems require a robust environment for the Onboarder, including a structured thought interface, (controlled) internet access, and a wide model search space.

</details>


### [31] [Diviner: Agentic RCA with Human-Governed Adaptation for Large-Scale Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21281)
*Pradipta Ghosh,Gaurav Mitra,Shripad Nadgowda,Akshil Shah,Junyu Liu,Canturk Isci,Syamla Bandla,Xinyao Hu,Onkar Deshpande,Anik Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 大规模基础设施 agentic 根因分析系统，含技能编码、上下文隔离 worker、证据链假设、轨迹回放审查与人工把关的适应闭环，架构可迁移。

TL;DR: Diviner 是 Meta 的代理式根因分析系统，通过 Encode/Execute/Evaluate/Adapt 四阶段可靠性生命周期，将事故知识、证据链与人工评审结合，提升大规模分布式系统 RCA 的可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统 RCA 困难：单一症状跨服务、数据、配置、计算与组织边界，证据不完整，专家分散；通用语言模型代理仅靠工具访问会因上下文过时或时机错误、将查询失败误判为症状缺失、报告无依据状态以及过载可观测性服务而失效。

Method: 提出 Diviner，围绕四阶段可靠性生命周期：Encode 结合已评审事故语料与服务所有者专长，生成有范围、版本化的技能、工作流、类型化工具和事故知识图谱；Execute 用自适应 Lead 与上下文隔离 worker，将知识应用于时点证据并维护证据关联假设；Evaluate 将保存调查轨迹与响应者评审结果对比，衡量 exact cause、useful direction 和 Groundedness；Adapt 用反复差距与 on-call 反馈生成聚焦运营知识更新，并需重放、质量检查和所有者批准。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验指标；系统通过 Evaluate 以保存轨迹和响应者评审结果衡量 exact cause、useful direction 与 Groundedness，并通过 Adapt 在重放、质量检查和所有者批准后接纳知识更新。

Conclusion: Diviner 表明，面向大规模分布式系统 RCA 的代理系统需要领域知识编码、证据关联假设、人工评审闭环和受控知识更新，而非仅依赖工具访问的通用语言模型代理。

中文摘要：大规模分布式系统中的根因分析（RCA）具有挑战性，因为单个症状可能跨越服务、数据、配置、计算和组织边界。响应人员必须汇集不完整的证据，并协调掌握因果路径不同部分的专家。通用语言模型代理仅靠工具访问并不能解决这个问题：它们可能使用过时或时机不当的上下文，将失败的查询解释为不存在症状，报告无支持的系统状态，并使为人类节奏使用而设计的可观测性服务过载。
我们提出 Diviner（Domain-Informed Verifiable Incident Navigation through Evidence and Review，基于证据与评审的领域知情可验证事故导航），一个在 Meta 运行、围绕四阶段可靠性生命周期组织的代理式 RCA 系统。Encode 将大量已评审事故语料库与服务所有者专业知识相结合，生成有范围的、版本化的技能、工作流、类型化工具和事故知识图谱。Execute 使用自适应 Lead 和上下文隔离的 worker，将相关知识应用于时间点证据，并维护与证据关联的假设。Evaluate 将保存的调查轨迹与响应者评审的结果进行比较，以衡量确切原因、有用方向和 Groundedness。Adapt 利用反复出现的差距和 on-call 反馈来推导聚焦的运营知识更新，仅在重放、质量检查和所有者批准后才接纳它们。

Original abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) in large-scale distributed systems is challenging because a single symptom can cross service, data, configuration, compute, and organizational boundaries. Responders must assemble incomplete evidence and coordinate experts who hold different parts of the causal path. Generic language-model agents do not solve this problem through tool access alone: they can use stale or mistimed context, interpret failed queries as absent symptoms, report unsupported system state, and overload observability services designed for human-paced use.
  We present Diviner (Domain-Informed Verifiable Incident Navigation through Evidence and Review), an agentic RCA system operating at Meta and organized around a four-stage reliability lifecycle. Encode combines a large corpus of reviewed incidents with service-owner expertise to produce scoped, versioned skills, workflows, typed tools, and an incident knowledge graph. Execute uses an adaptive Lead and context-isolated workers to apply relevant knowledge to point-in-time evidence and maintain evidence-linked hypotheses. Evaluate compares saved investigation trajectories with responder-reviewed outcomes to measure exact cause, useful direction, and Groundedness. Adapt uses recurring gaps and on-call feedback to derive focused operational- knowledge updates, admitting them only after replay, quality checks, and owner approval.

</details>


### [32] [Mallea: Building, Steering, and Memorizing Worlds with Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21283)
*Xuanyi Liu,Ruiqi Li,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: coding agent 从文本构建可执行世界模型并规划对象结构与规则，程序推进状态、文本编辑保持未受影响状态，涉及代码化记忆与状态保留。

TL;DR: 提出 Mallea，一个面向代码世界模型的文本驱动系统，通过编码智能体联合规划对象结构、属性与规则，并借助对象纹理记忆与生成式渲染器，实现世界的构建、用户引导、文本编辑和状态一致的可视化记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代码世界模型中，如何协调对象表示、行为修订与视觉记忆，以支持一致且可编辑的世界构建，是待研究问题。

Method: Mallea 从提示推断对象交互需求，联合规划对象结构、属性和规则；世界按规则演化，用户操作可引导，文本请求可编辑属性或扩展能力并保留未受影响状态；对象纹理记忆仅对白盒对象保存可复用外观并随对象变化调整；程序推进状态，生成式渲染器结合当前结构与记忆外观。

Result: 系统支持从文本构建、引导和记忆世界；可保留未受影响状态，支持属性编辑与能力扩展，并针对同一对象使后续观察以最新状态与兼容外观为条件。

Conclusion: 围绕同一对象组织构建、干预和记忆，有助于维持代码世界模型的状态与外观一致性，并支持可编辑、可复用的世界表示。

中文摘要：代码世界模型通过显式状态和可执行规则来表示世界动态。我们研究如何在这种表述中协调对象表示、行为修订和视觉记忆。我们提出 Mallea，一个从文本构建、引导和记忆世界的系统。一个编码智能体从提示中推断对象交互需求，并联合规划对象结构、属性和规则，偏好支持这些需求的紧凑表示。世界在规划出的规则下演化；可选的用户操作引导它，而文本请求可以编辑属性或扩展能力，同时保留未受影响的状态。对象纹理记忆仅对白盒定义的对象保留可重用外观，并随着这些对象变化而调整该外观。程序推进世界状态，而生成式渲染器将当前结构与记忆中的外观结合起来。通过围绕相同的对象组织构建、干预和记忆，Mallea 使后续观察以最新状态和兼容外观为条件。项目页面：https://lxycopper.github.io/Mallea.

Original abstract: Code world models represent world dynamics through explicit state and executable rules. We investigate how to coordinate object representations, behavioral revisions, and visual memory within this formulation. We present Mallea, a system for building, steering, and memorizing worlds from text. A coding agent infers object interaction requirements from the prompt and jointly plans object structure, properties, and rules, favoring compact representations that support those requirements. The world evolves under the planned rules; optional user actions steer it, while text requests can edit properties or extend capabilities with unaffected state preserved. Object texture memory retains reusable appearance only for whitebox-defined objects and adapts it as those objects change. The program advances world state, while a generative renderer combines current structure with remembered appearance. By organizing construction, intervention, and memory around the same objects, Mallea conditions subsequent observations on the latest state and compatible appearance. Project page: https://lxycopper.github.io/Mallea.

</details>


### [33] [Bias Control and Injection for Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21290)
*Sungjae Lee,Soheil Abbasloo,Jabin Koo,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多 agent 聚合中的偏见控制与注入式可靠性信号，无需验证集即可在测试时选择答案，可迁移到多 agent 协作与仲裁机制。

TL;DR: 该工作分析多智能体LLM推理中的非语义偏差，提出同时利用偏差控制与偏差注入的测试时聚合框架，无需验证数据或模型强度先验，在数学推理基准上提升准确率与 token 效率，并可增强多智能体辩论和 Mixture-of-Agents。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM在意见分歧时难以判断应信任谁；多数压力和响应风格偏好等非语义偏差会干扰决策，掩盖推理质量。

Method: 分析偏差可缓解也可利用：偏差控制改善初始答案选择，偏差注入产生决策不稳定性作为可靠性信号。据此提出测试时聚合框架：对每个不同答案呈现一条风格归一化推理轨迹以控制偏差；将各聚合智能体初始选择答案置于少数派以注入偏差。无需验证数据和模型相对强度先验。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准、四种异构LLM组合上，平均准确率高于竞争性非 oracle 聚合方法，token 效率更好；应用于多智能体辩论和 Mixture-of-Agents 时，在不改变其交互或合成过程的情况下提升最终答案准确率。

Conclusion: 非语义偏差既可被缓解也可被利用；所提测试时聚合框架无需验证数据或模型强度先验，在多个推理基准和异构模型组合上有效，并能无缝增强现有多智能体协作方法。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLMs）的多智能体推理为集体决策提出了一个核心问题：当智能体意见不一致时，应当信任谁的决策？解决此类分歧并不容易，因为智能体的决策容易受到非语义偏差的影响，例如多数压力与响应风格偏好，这些偏差会掩盖其底层推理的质量。我们的分析表明，偏差既可以被缓解，也可以被利用：偏差控制可改进初始答案选择，而偏差注入会引发决策不稳定性，这种不稳定性可作为可靠性信号。基于这些发现，我们提出一种测试时聚合框架，该框架既不需要验证数据，也不需要关于模型相对强度的先验知识。该框架通过为每个不同答案呈现一条风格归一化的推理轨迹来实现偏差控制，并通过将每个聚合智能体最初选择的答案置于少数派来实现偏差注入。总体而言，在三个数学推理基准和四种异构LLM组合上，我们的框架相比竞争性的非 oracle 聚合方法取得了更高的平均准确率和更好的 token 效率。当应用于多智能体辩论和 Mixture-of-Agents 时，它还能在不修改其交互或合成过程的情况下提升最终答案准确率。

Original abstract: Multi-agent reasoning with large language models (LLMs) raises a central question for collective decision-making: when agents disagree, whose decision should be trusted? Resolving such disagreements is difficult because agents' decisions are susceptible to non-semantic biases such as majority pressure and response-style preferences, which can obscure the quality of the underlying reasoning. Our analysis shows how bias can be both mitigated and exploited: bias control improves initial answer selection, while bias injection elicits decision instability that serves as a reliability signal. Building on these findings, we introduce a test-time aggregation framework that requires neither validation data nor prior knowledge of relative model strength. The framework implements bias control by presenting one style-normalized reasoning trace per distinct answer, and bias injection by placing each aggregating agent's initially selected answer in the minority. Overall, our framework achieves higher average accuracy and better token efficiency than competing non-oracle aggregation methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four heterogeneous LLM combinations. It also improves final-answer accuracy when applied to multi-agent debate and Mixture-of-Agents, without modifying their interaction or synthesis processes.

</details>


### [34] [DocPilot: Interactive Agent Orchestration and Training for Long Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21327)
*Zhuowen Liang,Xiaotian Lin,Yuyu Luo,Haixun Wang,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 13 figures, 9 tables

Selection reason: 文档理解 agent 框架支持页级导航、细粒度证据定位、代码数值推理与可执行交互轨迹的课程式 agentic 训练，编排与训练思路可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出DocPilot，一种支持页面导航和细粒度证据定位的智能体文档框架，结合课程式智能体训练，使小模型也能进行多模态长文档推理，在多个基准上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档问答中，模型需跨页面和模态寻找稀疏证据并推理，但现有VLM单次处理易忽略证据、跨模态推理弱，现有文档智能体又受限于预定义流程或粗粒度动作，缺乏灵活性和细粒度。因此需要更灵活、可学习的智能体框架，并使小模型也能掌握复杂文档推理。

Method: DocPilot框架通过页面级导航和细粒度证据定位来寻找证据，集成本地视觉检查、代码数值推理和跨模态推理。为迁移能力到小模型，提出课程式智能体训练，从单一能力学习逐步到自适应编排，利用数据驱动的推理轨迹和可执行交互轨迹进行监督。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc、DocBench、FinRAGBench-V、MADQA上，DocPilot结合前沿VLM骨干取得显著提升；DocPilot-4B与更大模型相比性能有竞争力，展现出强有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: DocPilot通过表达性编排和课程式智能体训练，使小语言模型也能有效解决多模态长文档推理任务，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

中文摘要：多模态长文档问答要求模型跨页面和模态寻找稀疏证据，理解异构文档内容，并对收集到的证据进行推理。即便是先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）也可能忽略稀疏证据，并在单次处理中难以进行跨模态推理。现有的文档智能体进一步引入了多步交互，但其中一些仍依赖于预定义的工作流或粗粒度的动作，限制了文档推理的灵活性和粒度。我们提出了DocPilot，一个智能体文档框架，支持表达性编排，并使复杂文档求解可被小语言模型（SLM）学习。DocPilot通过页面级导航和细粒度证据定位来编排证据寻找，并集成本地视觉检查、基于代码的数值推理和跨模态推理以实现多模态理解。为了将这些能力迁移到小模型，我们提出了基于课程的智能体训练，通过数据驱动的推理轨迹和可执行交互轨迹的监督，从单一能力学习逐步过渡到自适应编排。在MMLongBench-Doc、DocBench、FinRAGBench-V和MADQA上，DocPilot在前沿VLM骨干模型上取得了显著提升，而DocPilot-4B在性能上与规模大得多的模型相比具有竞争力，展示了在多样长文档推理设置中的强有效性和泛化能力。代码可在https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot获取。

Original abstract: Multimodal long-document question answering requires models to seek sparse evidence across pages and modalities, understand heterogeneous document content, and reason over the collected evidence. Even advanced vision-language models (VLMs) can overlook sparse evidence and struggle with cross-modal reasoning in a single pass. Existing document agents further introduce multi-step interaction, but some still rely on predefined workflows or coarse-grained actions, limiting the flexibility and granularity of document reasoning. We introduce DocPilot, an agentic document framework that supports expressive orchestration and makes complex document solving learnable by small language models (SLMs). DocPilot orchestrates evidence seeking through page-level navigation and fine-grained evidence localization, and integrates local visual inspection, code-based numerical reasoning, and cross-modal reasoning for multimodal understanding. To transfer these capabilities to small models, we propose curriculum-based agentic training that progresses from single-ability learning to adaptive orchestration through supervision from data-grounded reasoning traces and executable interaction trajectories. Across MMLongBench-Doc, DocBench, FinRAGBench-V, and MADQA, DocPilot achieves strong gains with frontier VLM backbones, while DocPilot-4B delivers competitive performance against substantially larger models, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization across diverse long-document reasoning settings. The code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot.

</details>


### [35] [ARGUS: An Agenda-Driven Framework for Reliable User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21346)
*Hang Zheng,Nan Jiang,Zichen Zhu,Zixu Yang,Zhiyang Tang,Shuai Fan,Lu Chen,Xiaobao Wu,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 神经符号议程栈维持用户目标状态并配合评测引导 SFT 与在线 RL 蒸馏，为 agent 训练与交互评测提供可靠模拟器思路。

TL;DR: 提出神经符号用户模拟器ARGUS，用动态议程栈维护用户目标状态，并将状态跟踪与自然语言生成解耦；通过评估器引导SFT和在线RL蒸馏为紧凑模型，并引入CBS指标，在τ^3-Bench和MultiWOZ上验证了架构优越性与训练流水线有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真用户模拟器对交互环境中的智能体评估与训练至关重要，但现有基于LLM的模拟器存在目标遗漏、策略不合规等可靠性问题。

Method: 提出神经符号框架ARGUS，利用动态议程栈和符号操作维护用户目标状态，严格解耦状态跟踪与自然语言生成；采用评估器引导SFT和带密集步骤级奖励的在线RL统一流水线蒸馏紧凑模型；引入基于评分标准的复合行为得分CBS评估行为保真度。

Result: 在τ^3-Bench和MultiWOZ上的评估表明，ARGUS架构具有显著优越性，且训练流水线有效。

Conclusion: ARGUS与所提出的训练流水线能够提升用户模拟器的行为保真度与可靠性，代码和数据将很快发布。

中文摘要：高保真用户模拟器对于在交互环境中评估和训练智能体至关重要。然而，当前基于LLM的模拟器面临可靠性问题，例如目标遗漏和策略不合规。我们提出ARGUS（Agenda-dRiven Goal-oriented User Simulator，议程驱动的目标导向用户模拟器），一个神经符号框架，使用通过符号操作操纵的动态议程栈来维护用户的目标状态。通过严格地将状态跟踪与自然语言生成解耦，ARGUS促进了严格的目标推进和约束对齐的交互。为了实现成本高效的部署，我们使用统一流水线将ARGUS蒸馏为紧凑模型：评估器引导的SFT，随后是带有密集步骤级奖励的在线RL。为了严格评估行为保真度，我们引入了基于评分标准的指标——复合行为得分（CBS）。在τ^3-Bench和MultiWOZ上的评估表明ARGUS架构具有显著优越性，并且我们的训练流水线有效。代码和数据将很快发布。

Original abstract: High-fidelity user simulators are essential for evaluating and training agents in interactive environments. However, current LLM-based simulators struggle with reliability issues, such as goal omission and policy non-compliance. We propose ARGUS (Agenda-dRiven Goal-oriented User Simulator), a neuro-symbolic framework that maintains the user's goal state using a dynamic agenda stack manipulated via symbolic operations. By rigorously decoupling state tracking from natural language generation, ARGUS facilitates strict goal progression and constraint-aligned interactions. For cost-efficient deployment, we distill ARGUS into compact models using a unified pipeline: evaluator-guided SFT followed by online RL with dense step-level rewards. To rigorously evaluate behavioral fidelity, we introduce a rubric-based metric Composite Behavioral Score (CBS). Evaluations on $τ^3$-Bench and MultiWOZ demonstrate the significant superiority of the ARGUS architecture and the efficacy of our training pipeline. Code and data will be released soon.

</details>


### [36] [FedSkillGit: Federated Skill Evolution via Version Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21348)
*Boxun Lan,Jiamei Li,Weijian Li,Chengyu Yang,Zhuang Qi,Chengcheng Wan,Xiaohong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以补丁提交、去重合并、本地验证集评分门槛发布的联邦技能演化框架，与执行经验驱动的 agent 技能/代码版本化自进化高度契合。

TL;DR: 提出 FedSkillGit，以补丁提交代替全文档更新，结合两阶段隐私过滤、服务器去重与验证集评分发布，实现联邦技能演化；在三个数据集和三个语言模型家族上最高提升 22%，并接近集中式演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中，执行经验分散在无法共享原始数据的数据所有者处，需要联邦技能演化；但直接让客户端演化并上传完整技能文档会带来客户端特定信息残留、通信浪费以及单客户端贡献难以归因的问题。

Method: FedSkillGit 让每个参与客户端提交对命名规则的有界补丁集合及概括本轮所学内容的本地经验，并在离开客户端前经过两阶段隐私过滤器。服务器对补丁与本地经验去重，更新仅由优化器读取的全局经验文档，并将存活补丁合并为候选技能。候选技能由客户端在本地验证集评分，仅在分数优于当前版本时发布。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个语言模型家族上，FedSkillGit 优于最先进基线最高 22%，并在不共享原始客户端数据的情况下接近集中式演化。

Conclusion: FedSkillGit 通过提交式联邦技能演化，缓解完整文档更新在隐私、通信和归因上的缺陷，在保护原始客户端数据的同时取得接近集中式演化的技能提升。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体可以通过从执行经验中演化可复用的自然语言技能来提升其能力。在真实世界部署中，这些经验分布在无法汇聚原始数据的数据所有者之间，这推动了联邦技能演化。自然的初步设计让每个客户端演化整个技能文档，并上传结果以供服务器端聚合。该设计在三个方面与技能演化并不匹配。演化后的文档仍可能携带从私有轨迹中提炼出的客户端特定内容；反复传输几乎未改变的文档会把通信开销花在群体已经掌握的知识上；单个客户端的贡献被埋没在服务器无法有意义地归因的文档中。我们提出 FedSkillGit，一种联邦技能演化框架，用提交（commits）替代全文档更新。每个参与的客户端提交对命名规则的有界补丁集合及其本地经验，本地经验概括本轮所学内容，且该二元组在离开客户端前通过两阶段隐私过滤器。服务器对所有参与者提交的补丁和本地经验进行去重，更新仅由优化器读取的全局经验文档，并将存活的补丁合并为候选技能。候选技能分发给客户端，客户端在其自身验证集上评分并报告结果，只有当报告的分数优于当前版本时才发布。在三个数据集和三个语言模型家族上的实验表明，FedSkillGit 相比最先进基线最高提升 22%，并在不共享原始客户端数据的情况下接近集中式演化。

Original abstract: Large language model agents can improve their capabilities by evolving reusable natural-language skills from execution experience. In real-world deployments, this experience is distributed across data owners who cannot pool their raw data, which motivates federated skill evolution. The natural first design lets each client evolve the whole skill document and upload the result for server-side aggregation. That design is poorly matched to skill evolution for three reasons. An evolved document can still carry client-specific content distilled from private traces, repeated transmission of a nearly unchanged document spends communication on knowledge that the population already holds and the contribution of a single client is buried in a document that the server cannot meaningfully attribute. We propose FedSkillGit, a federated skill evolution framework that replaces full-document updates with commits. Each participating client submits a bounded set of patches over named rules together with its local experience, which summarizes what the round taught it, and the pair passes a two-stage privacy filter before it leaves the client. The server deduplicates the submitted patches and the local experience of all participants, updates a global experience document that only the optimizers read, and merges the surviving patches into a candidate skill. The candidate is distributed to the clients, which score it on their own validation sets and report the results, and it is published only when the reported scores improve on the current version. Experiments on three datasets and three language model families show that FedSkillGit outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 22% and approaches centralized evolution without sharing raw client data.

</details>


### [37] [Self-Guided LLM Reasoning for Error Diagnosis in Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21393)
*Ni Zhang,Jianan Zhou,Yue-Jiao Gong,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 让LLM自主生成代码辅助VRP错误诊断，体现代码作为结构化验证工具的可迁移思路。

TL;DR: 本文系统评估LLM检测与纠正VRP实例错误的能力，发现复杂实例或高推理负担错误等局限，并提出LLM自主生成代码提供结构化见解的自引导推理框架，实验取得接近完美的错误检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VRP求解方法多假设输入实例无错误，忽视真实数据中常见故障；需研究LLM错误检测能力并克服其推理局限。

Method: 系统研究LLM检测和纠正VRP实例错误的能力；提出自引导推理框架，让LLM自主生成代码提供结构化见解以辅助错误诊断。

Result: 跨多种问题设置和LLM的实验表明，该框架显著提升错误检测性能，达到接近完美的准确率。

Conclusion: 自引导代码生成可缓解LLM在复杂错误上的推理负担，有效提升VRP实例错误检测表现，具有现实应用潜力。

中文摘要：车辆路径问题（VRPs）是许多现实世界应用的基础。近年来，大语言模型（LLMs）的进展推动了求解这些NP难问题的进展。然而，大多数现有方法假设输入实例是无错误的，忽视了现实世界数据中常见的故障。在这项工作中，我们系统地研究了LLMs检测（并纠正）VRP实例中错误的能力，并识别出关键局限，特别是在处理复杂实例或会显著增加LLM推理负担的错误时。为了克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种自引导推理框架，其中LLMs自主生成代码，提供结构化见解以辅助错误诊断。跨多种问题设置和LLMs的实验表明，我们的框架显著提高了错误检测性能，达到接近完美的准确率。

Original abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental to many real-world applications. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred progress in solving these NP-hard problems. However, most existing approaches assume that input instances are error-free, neglecting the presence of faults commonly encountered in real-world data. In this work, we systematically investigate the capability of LLMs to detect (and correct) errors in VRP instances, and identify key limitations, particularly in handling complex instances or errors that substantially increase the LLM's reasoning burden. To overcome these challenges, we propose a self-guided reasoning framework in which LLMs autonomously generate code that provides structured insights to aid in error diagnosis. Experiments across diverse problem settings and LLMs demonstrate that our framework significantly improves error detection performance, achieving near-perfect accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [MemCut: Every Secret Leaves a Trail in the Long-Term Memory of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21405)
*Cheng Zhang,Xuanning Cao,Zhicheng Zheng,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures, 13 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: 面向LLM agent长期记忆的遗忘/删除评测，涉及记忆可靠性与隐私安全，可迁移到agent记忆设计。

TL;DR: 提出MemCut基准与原生/残余恢复指标，评估LLM智能体长期记忆的用户请求遗忘能力，发现移除披露可显著降低原生恢复，但残余恢复仍高，候选覆盖是参考基线瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆对LLM智能体至关重要，但可靠系统须能按用户请求遗忘敏感数据；现有研究多关注检索，缺少对用户请求遗忘能力的系统评估。

Method: 提出MemCut基准，包含长程对话中456个手工设计秘密及完整证据链；定义原生恢复与残余恢复指标；建立三个参考删除基线，在五种记忆设计和四种LLM上测试检索器与写入模型；并进行子集扫描。

Result: 移除披露信息对原生恢复的降低远大于残余恢复；LLM引导删除额外十轮后残余恢复仍为39–76%；子集扫描显示扩大候选池比删除所有原始候选更能降低残余恢复。

Conclusion: 参考基线中候选覆盖仍是瓶颈，表明遗忘评估需关注候选覆盖等问题。

中文摘要：长期记忆已成为大语言模型（LLM）智能体的关键组成部分，而大多数研究侧重于准确高效地检索相关信息。然而，一个可靠的记忆系统还必须应请求遗忘敏感用户数据。为评估这一能力，我们提出了MemCut，一个用于用户请求遗忘的系统性基准。其长程对话中的456个人工设计的秘密具有完整标注的证据链，用于测试删除后的恢复，包括从从未直接陈述这些秘密的消息中恢复。我们提出了两个恢复指标。原生恢复通过系统检索衡量读取器成功，而残余恢复衡量同一读取器在直接给定可追溯至标注披露或证据的留存对象时的成功。我们建立了三个参考删除基线，以测试现有检索器和写入模型在五种记忆设计和四种LLM上对遗忘的支持程度。移除披露信息使原生恢复的下降远大于残余恢复。由LLM引导从二十个检索候选中删除额外的十个轮次后，仍留下39–76%的残余恢复。子集扫描（144个事实、三个系统、两个模型）表明，在固定预算下扩大候选池比删除所有原始候选更能降低残余恢复。这些扫描将候选覆盖识别为参考基线中的瓶颈。

Original abstract: Long-term memory has become a crucial component of large language model (LLM) agents, with most research focusing on retrieving relevant information accurately and efficiently. However, a dependable memory system must also forget sensitive user data upon request. To evaluate this capability, we introduce MemCut, a systematic benchmark for user-requested forgetting. Its 456 hand-designed secrets in long-horizon conversations have fully annotated evidence trails to test recovery after deletion, including from messages that never state the secrets directly. We propose two recovery metrics. Native recovery measures reader success through the system's retrieval, whereas residual recovery measures the same reader's success when directly given surviving objects traced to annotated disclosures or evidence. We establish three reference deletion baselines to test how far existing retrievers and write models support forgetting across five memory designs and four LLMs. Disclosure removal reduces native recovery far more than residual recovery. LLM-guided deletion of ten additional turns from twenty retrieval candidates leaves 39-76% residual recovery. Subset sweeps (144 facts, three systems, two models) show that widening the pool at a fixed budget reduces residual recovery more than deleting all original candidates. These sweeps identify candidate coverage as a bottleneck in the reference baselines.

</details>


### [39] [Jev-LiteMAS: Separating Decisions from Generation in Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21411)
*Kaixiang Wang,Zhaijiacheng Zhou,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent工作流中用轻量决策模型替代部分生成调用，降低token并保持效果，对agent路由与harness设计有启发。

TL;DR: Jev-LiteMAS通过用轻量级决策模型Jev替代多智能体系统中高开销的路由与审查操作，同时保留生成式执行与反馈，弥合规划与审查两个接口缺口，在多个基准上减少生成式LLM令牌并保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，路由和审查等协调操作仅需有限决策，却消耗大量生成式LLM令牌（31.7-43.5%）。但直接用低成本决策模型替换会带来规划缺口（缺少执行计划和交接指令）和审查缺口（缺少可操作修复反馈），因此需要一种既能降低成本又能弥补这些缺口的框架。

Method: 提出Jev-LiteMAS：结合轻量级决策模型Jev与生成式执行和反馈。规划缺口方面，Jev根据任务和先前专家输出动态选择专家，利用累积上下文指导后续执行，无需预生成轨迹；审查缺口方面，Jev按任务标准筛查中间产物，通过者直接继续，被标记者交由LLM重新审查以决定接受或给出纠正反馈。

Result: 在四个基准和两个骨干模型上，Jev-LiteMAS相比LAS减少生成式LLM令牌12.7-54.6%，准确率差距在1.34个百分点以内，并在八种设置中的七种降低延迟。将路由建立在实质性专家输出上可防止高达18.0个百分点的准确率损失。

Conclusion: Jev-LiteMAS表明，用轻量级决策模型替代多智能体系统中的有限决策操作，并辅以生成式执行与反馈来弥合接口缺口，可在保持准确率的同时显著降低令牌消耗和延迟。

中文摘要：多智能体LLM系统依赖生成模型进行专家执行、工作流规划和产物审查。然而，许多协调操作仅需要有限决策，例如选择下一个专家或判断产物质量。通过分析三个代表性的MAS框架，我们发现路由和审查占生成式LLM令牌的31.7-43.5%。然而，用低成本决策模型替代这些操作会引入两个接口缺口：生成式规划器产生执行计划和交接指令（规划缺口），而审查者提供可操作的反馈以修复有缺陷的产物（审查缺口），这是仅靠有限决策无法提供的。我们提出Jev-LiteMAS，一个多智能体框架，通过将轻量级决策模型（Jev）与生成式执行和反馈相结合，弥合这两个缺口。为了解决规划缺口，Jev根据任务和先前专家输出动态选择专家，利用累积上下文为后续执行提供依据，而无需预先生成轨迹。为了解决审查缺口，Jev根据任务标准筛选中间产物：通过的产物直接继续，而被标记的产物则经过LLM重新审查以决定接受或提供纠正反馈。在四个基准和两个骨干模型上，Jev-LiteMAS相比LAS将生成式LLM令牌减少了12.7-54.6%，准确率保持在1.34个百分点以内，并在八种设置中的七种降低了延迟。将路由建立在实质性专家输出上可防止高达18.0个百分点的准确率损失。

Original abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems rely on generative models for expert execution, workflow planning, and artifact review. Yet many coordination operations require only bounded decisions, such as selecting the next expert or judging artifact quality. Profiling three representative MAS frameworks, we find that routing and review account for 31.7-43.5% of generative-LLM tokens. However, replacing these operations with low-cost decision models introduces two interface gaps: generative planners produce execution plans and handoff instructions (planning gap), while reviewers provide actionable feedback for repairing flawed artifacts (review gap), which bounded decisions alone cannot provide. We introduce Jev-LiteMAS, a multi-agent framework that bridges both gaps by combining a lightweight decision model (Jev) with generative execution and feedback. To address the planning gap, Jev selects experts dynamically from the task and prior expert outputs, using accumulated context to ground subsequent execution without a pre-generated trajectory. To address the review gap, Jev screens intermediate artifacts against task criteria: passing artifacts proceed directly, whereas flagged ones undergo LLM re-review for acceptance or corrective feedback. Across four benchmarks and two backbones, Jev-LiteMAS reduces generative-LLM tokens by 12.7-54.6% over LAS, maintaining accuracy within 1.34 percentage points and lowering latency in seven of eight settings. Grounding routing in substantive expert outputs prevents up to 18.0 points in accuracy loss.

</details>


### [40] [JevAgentHarness: Benchmarking, Deploying and Training Decision Models That Share Control with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21412)
*Kunlun Zhu,Tao Feng,Xu Han,Heng Wang,Ling Yang,Xiangru Tang,Jiaxuan You,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code: https://github.com/JevAgentHarness/JevAgentHarness

Selection reason: 系统研究agent harness中决策模型与LLM共享控制，含基准、轨迹阈值拟合与4B决策模型训练，可直接迁移到coding/优化agent。

TL;DR: 该工作研究LLM智能体harness中的决策委托：提出AgentDecisionBench评估决策模型，发现直接委托会降低成功率，进而设计JevAgentHarness阈值拟合，并训练AgentDecision-4B，在多样任务上以更低成本、更高速度保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体每一步都调用大型LLM，导致长任务缓慢且昂贵；多数调用是多项选择决策，可由Jev等廉价决策模型直接回答。但现有决策模型在智能体任务中能否低损失地替代LLM、以及如何安全委托，尚缺乏评估与方法。

Method: 构建AgentDecisionBench，覆盖从孤立问题到控制运行中智能体，使用公共基准和DecisionGym；提出JevAgentHarness，在智能体自身轨迹上为每个选择拟合置信度阈值，低于阈值时交由LLM决策；并利用智能体犯错后状态，以Jev答案为标签训练开源4B模型AgentDecision-4B。

Result: 孤立问题上最准确的决策模型仅解决69.5%智能体任务；无拟合阈值时委托工具选择使成功率下降19.0个百分点。AgentDecision-4B在分布外任务上解决91.5%，Jev为82.0%；在分布内任务上，此类4B模型使成本降低81.1-89.0%，速度最高提升2.5倍，且成功率未下降3个百分点或更多。

Conclusion: 通过阈值化委托与在智能体自生成状态上训练决策模型，可在智能体任务中以显著更低成本和更高速度保持成功率；模型、数据和harness将开源发布。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体如今可以修复软件、操作计算机并服务客户，但它们的harness（运行它们的程序）在每一步都会调用大型LLM，这使得长任务缓慢且昂贵。这些调用中的大多数最终都归结为多项选择决策，例如下一步调用哪个工具。诸如Jev之类的决策模型能够直接回答这些问题，其时间和价格仅为一次LLM调用的一小部分。我们研究harness如何将这些决策委托给决策模型，同时几乎不损失成功率。为了在智能体任务中评估决策模型，我们构建了AgentDecisionBench，它在公共基准和我们的DecisionGym环境上，从孤立问题一直到控制一个正在运行的智能体对决策模型进行测试。在孤立问题上最准确的决策模型仅解决了69.5%的智能体任务，并且在未使用拟合置信度阈值的情况下，委托工具选择使成功率降低了19.0个百分点。基于这些发现，我们设计了JevAgentHarness，它针对智能体自身轨迹上的每个选择拟合该阈值，并在低于阈值时让LLM进行决策。由于现有决策模型从未见过智能体在自身犯错后到达的状态，我们在这些状态上训练了开放4B模型AgentDecision-4B，并以Jev的答案作为标签。在与其训练数据不同的任务上，智能体使用AgentDecision-4B解决了91.5%的任务，使用Jev解决了82.0%。在与它们训练数据类似的任务上，以这种方式训练的4B模型使智能体成本降低81.1-89.0%，速度最高提升2.5倍，同时任务成功率没有下降3个百分点或更多。模型、数据和harness将在https://github.com/JevAgentHarness/JevAgentHarness发布。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now fix software, operate computers and serve customers, but their harnesses, the programs that run them, call a large LLM at every step, which makes long tasks slow and costly. Most of these calls end in a multiple-choice decision, such as which tool to call next. Decision models such as Jev answer these questions directly, at a fraction of an LLM call's time and price. We ask how a harness can delegate these decisions to a decision model with little loss of success. To evaluate decision models in agentic tasks, we build AgentDecisionBench, which tests them from isolated questions up to control of a running agent, on public benchmarks and our DecisionGym environment. The decision model most accurate on isolated questions solved only 69.5% of agent tasks, and without a fitted confidence threshold, delegating tool choices lowered success by 19.0 points. Building on these findings, we then design JevAgentHarness, which fits this threshold for each choice on the agent's own trajectories and lets the LLM decide below it. Because existing decision models never see the states an agent reaches after its own mistakes, we train AgentDecision-4B, an open 4B model, on such states with Jev's answers as labels. On tasks unlike its training data, the agent solved 91.5% of them with AgentDecision-4B and 82.0% with Jev. On tasks like their training data, 4B models trained this way made agents 81.1-89.0% cheaper and up to 2.5 times faster, without losing 3 or more points of task success. The models, data, and harness will be released at https://github.com/JevAgentHarness/JevAgentHarness.

</details>


### [41] [Do Agents Need a New Kind of Paper? SPRA: Structured Paper Representation for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21424)
*Yutong Leng,Haojiong Chen,Fukai Shang,Xiaoyang Wang,Mindong Liu,Runyuan Ma,Jiantao Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, including references and appendices; resource website: https://sciverse.opendatalab.com/

Selection reason: 面向科研agent的结构化论文表示与Schema-RAG，可迁移到agent知识检索与经验复用。

TL;DR: SPRA 用论文级结构化研究对象与类型化关系表示百万级 AI 论文，并通过 Schema-RAG 提升科学问答与跨论文推理，同时显著减少 token，资源已上线 SciVerse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体需要连接分散在叙事文章中的研究问题、方法与发现，因而需要一种可查询、可遍历、可复用且可验证的论文级表示。

Method: 提出 SPRA，以 empirical-AI profile 组织 Problems、Contributions、Components、ExperimentSetups、Measures 和 Findings，并记录论文级引用依赖、组件使用及与先前工作的比较，来源位置支持验证；在超过 100 万篇 AI 论文上实例化，包含 1995 万研究单元和 2407 万内部关系；评估通过 Schema-RAG 访问 SPRA，并在推理前固定任务证据。

Result: 七个子领域中，源条件科学 QA 比段落检索高 0.381/5，平均比 Full Compact 低 0.078/5；有向跨论文推理中 qualification 比段落检索高 0.184/5，Top-5 综合质量效应混合；所有配对 RQ2 任务中，输入+输出 token 比段落检索少 28.5%，比 Full Compact 少 95.3%；想法生成中 Overall 低 0.067 分，但 token 少 93.7%。

Conclusion: SPRA 可作为科学内容的可用表示，并作为连接、检查和复用论文级研究对象的研究工作流基础，资源可通过 SciVerse 学术数据平台访问。

中文摘要：科学智能体必须连接分散在叙事文章中的研究问题、方法和发现。我们提出 SPRA（Structured Paper Representation for Agents，面向智能体的结构化论文表示），一种对持久性研究对象及类型化关系的论文级表示。其经验型 AI 配置（empirical-AI profile）组织问题（Problems）、贡献（Contributions）、组件（Components）、实验设置（ExperimentSetups）、度量（Measures）与发现（Findings），以支持查询、遍历和复用。选定的论文级引用关系记录依赖、组件使用以及与先前工作的比较；来源位置支持验证。我们在超过一百万篇 AI 论文上实例化 SPRA，包含 1995 万个研究单元和 2407 万条内部关系。评估通过 Schema-RAG 访问 SPRA，任务证据在推理前固定。在七个子领域中，SPRA 相比段落检索将源条件科学问答提升 0.381/5，同时与 Full Compact 保持接近（平均低 0.078/5）。在有向跨论文推理中，qualification 相比段落检索提升 0.184/5；Top-5 综合显示出混合的质量效应。在所有配对的 RQ2 任务中（包括 Top-5），输入+输出 token 相比段落检索减少 28.5%，相比 Full Compact 减少 95.3%。在想法生成中，Overall 得分比 Full Compact 低 0.067 分，同时输入+输出 token 减少 93.7%。总之，这些结果支持 SPRA 作为科学内容的可用表示，并作为连接、检查和复用论文级研究对象的研究工作流的基础。SPRA 资源可通过 SciVerse 学术数据平台访问。

Original abstract: Scientific agents must connect research problems, methods, and findings dispersed through narrative articles. We introduce SPRA (Structured Paper Representation for Agents), a paper-level representation of persistent research objects and typed relations. Its empirical-AI profile organizes Problems, Contributions, Components, ExperimentSetups, Measures, and Findings for querying, traversal, and reuse. Selected paper-level citation relations record dependencies, component use, and comparisons with prior work; source locations support verification. We instantiate SPRA across more than one million AI papers, with 19.95 million research units and 24.07 million internal relations. Evaluations access SPRA through Schema-RAG with task evidence fixed before inference. Across seven subfields, SPRA improves source-conditioned scientific QA by 0.381/5 over passage retrieval while remaining close to Full Compact (0.078/5 lower on average). In directed cross-paper reasoning, qualification improves by 0.184/5 over passage retrieval; Top-5 synthesis shows mixed quality effects. Across all paired RQ2 tasks, including Top-5, input+output tokens decrease by 28.5% relative to passage retrieval and 95.3% relative to Full Compact. In idea generation, a 0.067-point lower Overall score than Full Compact accompanies 93.7% fewer input+output tokens. Together, these results support SPRA as a usable representation of scientific content and a foundation for research workflows that connect, inspect, and reuse paper-level research objects. The SPRA resource is accessible through the SciVerse scholarly-data platform.

</details>


### [42] [Learning from Interaction Experience via Localized Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21427)
*Zhezheng Hao,Yongkang Yang,Xilin Xia,Jiarui Yu,Yi Liu,Fanjunduo Wei,Peijie Dong,Tianfu Wang,Qiang Lin,Can Wang,Hande Dong,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 交互经验学习与局部上下文蒸馏，覆盖agent与代码生成任务，对self-improve和经验复用有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出局部化上下文蒸馏 LoCD，仅让教师重新生成学生轨迹中需修正的关键片段，从而兼顾信息量与低暴露偏差，并在多类任务上稳定超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏存在两难：离策略蒸馏使用教师生成轨迹，带来暴露偏差；在线策略蒸馏使用学生自身轨迹，但状态信息量不足，难以传达学生尚不能产生的行为。

Method: LoCD 先定位学生轨迹中需要修订的关键片段，再由教师基于学生自身前缀仅重新生成这些片段，最后用重新生成的片段训练学生。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上的大量实验表明，LoCD 持续优于强基线，训练更稳定，且参数更新更小、更稀疏。

Conclusion: LoCD 通过局部化教师重生成，将学习集中于最有信息量的状态并保持低暴露偏差，有效解决了现有上下文蒸馏的权衡问题。

中文摘要：从交互经验中学习对于使大语言模型在部署期间持续改进至关重要。上下文蒸馏提供了一条有前景的路径：从经验中提取的特权信息被提供给教师，教师的行为随后被蒸馏到一个无法访问该信息的学生模型中。然而，现有两种形式的上下文蒸馏都存在局限。离策略上下文蒸馏在教师生成的轨迹上训练学生，因而存在暴露偏差；而在线策略上下文蒸馏在学生的自身 rollout 上训练，其状态往往信息量不足，无法传达学生尚不能产生的行为。为解决这一困境，我们提出了局部化上下文蒸馏（Localized Context Distillation, LoCD）。LoCD 首先定位学生轨迹中需要修订的关键片段。教师随后仅基于学生自身前缀重新生成这些片段，学生则在重新生成的片段上训练。LoCD 由此将学习集中在信息量最大的状态上，同时保持较低的暴露偏差。跨数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务的广泛实验表明，LoCD 持续优于强基线，并以更小、更稀疏的参数更新实现更稳定的训练。

Original abstract: Learning from interaction experience is essential for enabling large language models to continually improve during deployment. Context distillation offers a promising path: privileged information extracted from experience is provided to a teacher, whose behavior is then distilled into a student model that has no access to it. However, both existing forms of context distillation have limitations. Off-policy context distillation trains the student on teacher-generated trajectories and thus suffers from exposure bias; whereas on-policy context distillation trains on the student's own rollouts, whose states are often uninformative and fail to convey behaviors the student cannot yet produce. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Localized Context Distillation (LoCD). LoCD first localizes the critical spans of a student trajectory that require revision. The teacher then regenerates only these spans from the student's own prefix, and the student is trained on the regenerated segments. LoCD thereby concentrates learning on the most informative states while keeping exposure bias low. Extensive experiments across math reasoning, code generation, and agent tasks demonstrate that LoCD consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves more stable training with smaller and sparser parameter updates.

</details>


### [43] [Incentivizing Tool-Use Reinforcement Learning for Post-Arrival Emergency Care Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21449)
*Pengfei Hu,Chenwei Wu,Qiao Jin,Jun Deng,Zhiyong Lu,Liyue Shen,Yue Ning*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 工具调用强化学习配合教师奖励与反向课程，方法可迁移到需要工具选择与技能蒸馏的agent。

TL;DR: 提出TIR-EC，通过工具集成强化学习、梯度提升树教师奖励和逆向课程蒸馏，提升LLM在EHR临床预测中的特征选择与数值风险判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在EHR预测任务上常不如树模型等传统ML方法，受控分析归因于两个弱点：不会选择有信息量的特征，以及即使给定正确特征也会误判数值对风险的含义。

Method: TIR-EC将EHR特征组作为LLM可调用工具；用梯度提升树教师提供有信息量工具参考和分级风险目标作为奖励进行强化学习；并通过逆向课程在RL过程中逐步从提示移除任务特定临床技能，将其蒸馏到模型权重。

Result: 在三个临床预测任务上，4B智能体取得所有对比模型中最高的macro F1，优于传统ML基线，并与更大的前沿LLM具有竞争力；进一步分析显示其以更少工具调用选择更有用证据并更有效组合测量值。

Conclusion: TIR-EC针对LLM在EHR预测中的特征选择与数值风险判断弱点，结合工具调用、RL与技能蒸馏，实现了优于传统基线且可与更大模型竞争的临床预测性能。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）能够对结构化测量值和自由文本记录进行推理，这使其在急诊科（ED）预测中颇具吸引力。然而，在基于EHR的预测任务上，大多数现有LLMs仍不及树模型等传统机器学习方法。我们的受控分析将这一差距追溯到两个弱点：LLMs不会选择有信息量的特征，并且即使给定了正确的特征，它们也会误判数值对风险的含义。我们提出TIR-EC，一个工具集成的强化学习框架，可同时解决这两个问题。TIR-EC将EHR特征组暴露为LLM可调用的工具。它利用来自梯度提升树教师的奖励来训练LLM，该教师提供一组有信息量的工具参考以及分级风险目标。TIR-EC还通过逆向课程将任务特定的临床技能蒸馏到模型权重中，该课程在RL过程中逐步从提示中移除这些技能。在三个临床预测任务上的评估表明，我们的4B智能体在所有对比模型中取得最高macro F1，优于传统机器学习基线，并与大得多的前沿LLMs具有竞争力。进一步分析表明，TIR-EC以更少的工具调用选择更有用的证据，并更有效地组合测量值。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reason over both structured measurements and free-text records, which makes them attractive for emergency department (ED) prediction. However, most existing LLMs still fall short of conventional ML methods like trees on EHR-based prediction tasks. Our controlled analysis traces this gap to two weaknesses: LLMs do not select informative features, and they misjudge what numerical values imply for risk even when given the right features. We propose TIR-EC, a tool-integrated reinforcement learning framework that addresses both. TIR-EC exposes EHR feature groups as tools the LLM can call. It trains the LLM with rewards from a gradient-boosted tree teacher, which provides a reference set of informative tools and a graded risk target. TIR-EC also distills task-specific clinical skills into the model weights through a reverse curriculum that gradually removes the skills from the prompt during RL process. Evaluation on three clinical prediction tasks shows that our 4B agent achieves the highest macro F1 of all compared models, outperforms conventional ML baselines, and is competitive with much larger frontier LLMs. Further analysis shows that TIR-EC selects more useful evidence with fewer tool calls and combines measurements more effectively.

</details>


### [44] [Tool-Augmented Large Language Models for Reliable Medical Calculation from Patient Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21477)
*Yang Zhong,Ling Luo,Xuanlyu Zhu,Yanhua Wang,Yuanyuan Sun,Jian Wang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted as a regular paper at BIBM2026

Selection reason: 工具增强LLM生成可执行计算工具并分离规则执行与语言理解，对可靠工具合成agent有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出工具增强LLM框架，将医学计算器规范检索、可执行工具生成和受约束变量提取结合，实现从患者笔记到确定性计算的可靠医学计算；在MedCalc-Bench上达到86.73%最终答案准确率，显著优于基线并减少算术幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接提示LLM从自由文本患者笔记执行医学计算时，规则回忆、变量提取、算术推理与格式输出纠缠于单一生成步骤，导致不可靠；因此需分离语言理解与规则及算术执行。

Method: 框架检索并结构化计算器规范，生成可复用可执行工具；用受约束提取器将患者笔记映射为计算器可用输入；由工具执行确定性计算，分离临床语言理解与规则执行和算术。

Result: 在MedCalc-Bench上最终答案准确率86.73%，较单次提示和仅监督微调基线分别高24.0和17.27个百分点；消融与错误分析显示增益主要来自基于规则的工具合成和模式约束提取，剩余失败集中于临床语义提取和基准特定输出契约。

Conclusion: 将计算器逻辑落地为可执行工具可缓解算术幻觉，提高最终临床输出的计算可靠性。

中文摘要：医学计算器将原始患者观察结果转化为临床可操作的值和评分。然而，将这些计算器直接应用于自由形式的患者笔记，需要精确的变量提取，并结合对复杂公式、评分规则和输出约定的准确执行。在此情境下，仅依赖直接的大语言模型（LLM）提示并不可靠，因为规则回忆、参数提取、算术推理和格式化会在单一生成步骤中相互纠缠。为解决这些局限，我们提出了一种工具增强的LLM框架，用于从患者笔记中进行可靠的医学计算。该框架检索并结构化计算器规范，生成可复用的可执行工具，并使用受约束的提取器将每条患者笔记映射为可供计算器使用的输入。随后，这些工具执行确定性计算，将临床语言理解与规则执行和算术分离。在MedCalc-Bench数据集上评估，我们的方法达到了86.73%的最终答案准确率，分别超过单次提示和仅监督微调基线24.0和17.27个百分点。消融和错误分析表明，这些性能提升主要由基于规则的工具合成和模式约束提取驱动，而剩余失败则集中在临床语义提取和基准特定的输出契约上。通过将计算器逻辑建立在可执行工具中，所提出的方法减轻了算术幻觉，并提高了最终临床输出的计算可靠性。

Original abstract: Medical calculators translate raw patient observations into clinically actionable values and scores. However, applying these calculators directly to free-form patient notes requires precise variable extraction combined with the exact execution of complex formulas, scoring rules, and output conventions. Relying solely on direct large language model (LLM) prompting is unreliable in this context, as rule recall, parameter extraction, arithmetic reasoning, and formatting become entangled within a single generative step. To address these limitations, we propose a tool-augmented LLM framework for reliable medical calculation from patient notes. The framework retrieves and structures calculator specifications, generates reusable executable tools, and uses a constrained extractor to map each patient note to calculator-ready inputs. The tools then perform deterministic calculation, separating clinical language understanding from rule execution and arithmetic. Evaluated on the MedCalc-Bench dataset, our method achieves 86.73% final-answer accuracy, outperforming the one-shot prompting and supervised fine tuning-only baselines by 24.0 and 17.27 percentage points. Ablation and error analyses show that these performance gains are primarily driven by rule-grounded tool synthesis and schema-constrained extraction, whereas remaining failures are concentrated in clinical-semantic extraction and benchmark-specific output contracts. By grounding calculator logic in executable tools, the proposed approach alleviates arithmetic hallucinations and improves the computational reliability of the final clinical outputs.

</details>


### [45] [MetaExp: Learning to Self-Improve through Experience via Verifiable Cross-Task Environment Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21492)
*Renhao Li,Xinjie Shen,Yang Su,Jianhong Tu,Derek F. Wong,Dayiheng Liu,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过可验证跨任务环境训练agent从交互经验中自我改进，将经验学习作为可迁移能力，高度契合长程agent优化。

TL;DR: MetaExp 通过可验证任务族训练语言模型智能体的可迁移经验学习能力，并在参数冻结条件下提升多个基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验学习方法依赖外部经验脚手架或在特定任务分布上训练，难以判断经验学习本身能否成为可迁移的模型能力；孤立任务监督不足以揭示跨回合仍然有用的知识。

Method: 构建保留可复用因子、变化任务特定实现的可验证任务族；设计 Shared-Tool 与 Shared-Workflow 两类任务族；扩展环境并收集过滤顺序交互轨迹；在来自 1,000 个任务族的 4,956 条轨迹上微调 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B；下游演化时冻结参数。

Result: SpreadsheetBench 提升 +8.5，APEX-Agents 峰值提升 +2.3，AutomationBench 提升 +14.8；在排除领域训练下增益仍保持。

Conclusion: 经验学习可作为一种可迁移能力，支持部署后持续的非参数改进。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体能够积累交互反馈，但可靠地将这些反馈转化为可复用经验仍然具有挑战性。现有方法要么优化外部经验脚手架，要么在特定任务分布上训练与经验相关的行为，这使得经验学习本身能否成为一种可迁移的模型能力仍未有定论。训练这样一种能力需要超越孤立任务的监督，而孤立任务主要支持回合内问题求解，而非揭示哪些知识在跨回合中仍然有用。在这项工作中，我们提出 MetaExp，一个可扩展框架，通过具有受控跨任务结构的可验证任务族来训练这种能力。每个任务族保留一个可复用因子，同时变化任务特定的实现，从而在不直接复用解的情况下实现迁移。我们提出两种互补设计：Shared-Tool 系列在任务间保持黑盒工具固定，以激发操作性经验；Shared-Workflow 系列在不断变化的实现中保留潜在流程，以激发程序性经验。我们在两种设计下扩展环境，收集并过滤顺序交互轨迹，并在来自 1,000 个任务族的 4,956 条轨迹上微调 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B。在下游演化期间参数冻结的情况下，MetaExp 在 SpreadsheetBench 上提升 +8.5 个点，在 APEX-Agents 上最高提升 +2.3，在 AutomationBench 上提升 +14.8。这些增益在排除领域训练下依然持续，支持经验学习作为一种可迁移能力，用于部署后持续的非参数改进。

Original abstract: Language-model agents can accumulate interaction feedback, but reliably turning that feedback into reusable experience remains challenging. Existing methods either optimize external experience scaffolds or train experience-related behavior on specific task distributions, leaving open whether experience learning itself can become a transferable model capability. Training such a capability calls for supervision beyond isolated tasks, which mainly support in-episode problem solving rather than revealing what knowledge remains useful across episodes. In this work, we introduce MetaExp, a scalable framework that trains this capability through verifiable task families with controlled cross-task structure. Each family preserves a reusable factor while varying task-specific realizations, enabling transfer without direct solution reuse. We propose two complementary designs: Shared-Tool families keep black-box tools fixed across tasks to elicit operational experience, while Shared-Workflow families preserve latent procedures across changing realizations to elicit procedural experience. We scale environments under both designs, collect and filter sequential interaction trajectories, and fine-tune Qwen3.5-122B-A10B on 4,956 trajectories from 1,000 task families. With parameters frozen during downstream evolution, MetaExp improves by +8.5 points on SpreadsheetBench, peaks at +2.3 on APEX-Agents, and improves by +14.8 on AutomationBench. The gains persist under domain-excluded training, supporting experience learning as a transferable capability for continued non-parametric improvement after deployment.

</details>


### [46] [QREF: Improving DeepSearch Agents by Aligning Search with Reasoning via Query Refinement and Evidence Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21512)
*Yujia Chen,Yuhui Wang,Junzhe Wang,Shihan Dou,Di Liang,Yujiong Shen,Yuhao Zhou,Zhiheng Xi,Ming Zhang,Senjie Jin,Jiayi Chen,Dingwei Zhu,Yajie Yang,Yuan Hua,Chenyao Lu,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: DeepSearch agent的查询精炼与证据过滤，RL对齐检索与推理，可迁移到检索/工具反馈型agent。

TL;DR: QREF是一种即插即用的查询精炼与证据过滤方法，通过SFT初始化并用RL优化，提升DeepSearch智能体在复杂检索任务中的性能，同时减少无效查询和检索噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSearch智能体常生成意图错位、约束不足或冗余的查询，造成证据覆盖不足与检索噪声累积，限制其复杂信息检索性能。

Method: 提出QREF，包含查询精炼器和证据过滤器。查询精炼器按当前推理意图重写查询并保证批次内互补覆盖；证据过滤器保留与当前搜索批次及推理目标相关的证据。查询精炼器先经监督微调初始化，再在真实网络搜索环境中通过强化学习优化。

Result: 在四个挑战性基准上提升DeepSearch智能体性能，WebSailor平均相对Pass@1提升33.55%，TongyiDeepResearch提升11.04%，且无需修改架构或重训练推理策略；相比无QREF的原始骨干，RL训练的精炼器将缺乏判别性检索锚点的查询批次比例降低95%；相比SFT训练版本，也降低了基于过滤器的无效查询比例。

Conclusion: QREF作为即插即用模块，可有效改善DeepSearch智能体的查询质量与证据利用效率，在不改变架构和推理策略的前提下显著提升检索推理性能。

中文摘要：DeepSearch智能体通过迭代搜索与推理，在复杂信息检索任务上取得强劲性能。然而，它们经常发出意图错位、约束不足或冗余的搜索查询，导致证据覆盖率差并累积检索噪声。为缓解这些局限，我们提出QREF，一种即插即用的查询精炼器与证据过滤器。具体而言，查询精炼器根据当前推理意图重写搜索查询，同时确保每个搜索批次之间的互补覆盖。证据过滤器保留与当前搜索批次和推理目标都相关的证据，减少干扰性检索结果。我们通过监督微调初始化查询精炼器，并基于与真实世界网络搜索环境的交互，通过强化学习对其进行优化，以减少无效查询。大量实验表明，我们的方法在四个具有挑战性的基准上提升了DeepSearch智能体的性能，在WebSailor上实现平均相对Pass@1提升33.55%，在TongyiDeepResearch上提升11.04%，且无需修改架构或重新训练其推理策略。与没有QREF的原始DeepSearch骨干相比，经RL训练的查询精炼器将缺乏判别性检索锚点的查询批次比例降低了95%。与经SFT训练的查询精炼器相比，它还降低了基于过滤器的无效查询比例。

Original abstract: DeepSearch agents achieve strong performance on complex information-seeking tasks through iterative search and reasoning. However, they often issue intent-misaligned, under-constrained, or redundant search queries, resulting in poor evidence coverage and accumulating retrieval noise. To mitigate these limitations, we propose QREF, a plug-and-play Query Refiner and Evidence Filter. Specifically, the Query Refiner rewrites search queries according to the current reasoning intent while ensuring complementary coverage across each search batch. The Evidence Filter retains evidence relevant to both the current search batch and the reasoning objective, reducing distracting retrieval results. We initialize the Query Refiner with supervised fine-tuning and optimize it through reinforcement learning based on interactions with a real-world web search environment, reducing ineffective queries. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our approach improves the performance of DeepSearch agents across four challenging benchmarks, achieving average relative Pass@1 improvements of 33.55% on WebSailor and 11.04% on TongyiDeepResearch without modifying architectures or retraining their reasoning policies. Compared with the original DeepSearch backbone without QREF, the RL-trained refiner reduces the fraction of query batches that lack discriminative retrieval anchors by 95%. Compared with the SFT-trained refiner, it also lowers the filter-based ineffective query ratio.

</details>


### [47] [Agent Status Protocol (ASP): A Lightweight Specification for State Reporting in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21538)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl,Emmett Coin,Csaba Bolyós,Andreas Zettl*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 30 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 多agent系统的状态上报与存活/进展检测协议，对agent运行可靠性与安全监控有可迁移的工程参考。

TL;DR: ASP 0.1 是轻量级异构AI智能体状态报告协议：智能体通过 HTTP POST 推送 JSON 状态事件，服务器通过 SSE 向仪表盘等观察者广播聚合状态；针对崩溃、挂起或被入侵智能体自报告不可信的问题，利用心跳缺失、进展检测及监督者/护栏/操作员/对等方观察进行补充；状态按生命周期、健康、任务、floor、治理等独立维度建模，并复用 A2A、OFP 和 OpenTelemetry GenAI 词汇；本文将其作为开放讨论的提案而非标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI智能体需要向仪表盘等观察者实时报告运行状态，但崩溃、挂起或被入侵的智能体无法可靠地自报告自身故障，因此需要一种不单纯依赖自报告、能够融合多来源观察并表达多维运行状态的状态报告机制。

Method: 定义 ASP v0.1：智能体通过 HTTP POST 推送小型 JSON 事件；服务器通过 SSE 向观察者暴露聚合状态；服务器根据错失心跳推断存活，检测存活但无进展的智能体，并接受来自监督者、护栏、操作员和对等方的观察，部分观察优先于智能体自述，部分与其并列；状态沿生命周期、健康、任务、floor、治理等独立维度建模；复用 A2A 任务状态、OFP floor 状态和 OpenTelemetry GenAI 语义约定中的属性约定。

Result: 本文提出 ASP 0.1 作为提案和工作草案，而非已批准标准；协议支持多维状态、多来源观察和已有词汇复用，但摘要未报告实验评估或标准化状态。

Conclusion: ASP 0.1 旨在开启社区讨论，作者邀请评论、替代提案和独立实现，以推动该协议后续发展。

中文摘要：Agent状态协议（ASP）定义了一种轻量级方式，使异构AI智能体能够向状态服务器报告其运行状态，并使仪表盘等观察者能够在状态变化时立即接收该状态。智能体通过HTTP POST推送小型JSON事件；服务器通过Server-Sent Events（SSE）向观察者暴露聚合状态。由于已崩溃、挂起或被入侵的智能体无法被信任来报告自身故障，ASP并不只依赖自报告：服务器通过错失的心跳推断存活状态，能够检测仍然存活但不再取得进展的智能体，并接受来自监督者、护栏、操作员和对等方的观察，其中一些观察优先于智能体关于自身的说法，而另一些则与其并列显示。ASP沿独立维度（生命周期、健康、任务、floor、治理）对状态建模，因此智能体可以同时处于例如在线、降级、正在执行任务并等待floor的状态。它复用现有词汇表：任务状态来自Agent2Agent（A2A）协议，floor状态来自Open Floor Protocol（OFP），属性约定来自OpenTelemetry GenAI语义约定。本文提出ASP 0.1版作为提案，而非已批准标准。它是一份旨在开启讨论的工作草案，作者邀请社区提出评论、替代提案和独立实现。

Original abstract: The Agent Status Protocol (ASP) defines a lightweight way for heterogeneous AI agents to report their operational state to a status server, and for observers such as dashboards to receive that state as soon as it changes. Agents push small JSON events over HTTP POST; the server exposes the aggregated state to observers over Server-Sent Events (SSE). Because an agent that has crashed, hung or been compromised cannot be trusted to report its own failure, ASP does not rely on self-reports alone: the server infers liveness from missed heartbeats, can detect agents that are alive but no longer making progress, and accepts observations from supervisors, guardrails, operators and peers, some of which take precedence over what the agent says about itself while others are shown alongside it. ASP models state along independent dimensions (lifecycle, health, task, floor, governance), so that an agent can be, for example, online, degraded, working on a task and waiting for the floor at the same time. It reuses existing vocabularies: task states from the Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, floor states from the Open Floor Protocol (OFP), and attribute conventions from the OpenTelemetry GenAI semantic conventions. This paper presents version 0.1 of ASP as a proposal, not as an approved standard. It is a working draft intended to open a discussion, and the authors invite comments, alternative proposals and independent implementations from the community.

</details>


### [48] [ClaimScope: Label-Blind Witness Verification for Mathematical Claim Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21581)
*Mushir Akhtar*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Workshop (Mathematical Reasoning and AI)

Selection reason: 基于witness的自动验证与反馈修正机制，其'只校验提交产物、反馈可定位结构错误'思路可迁移到agent验证环节。

TL;DR: ClaimScope 基准以判定、范围、反例、修复审计数学声明；在 Qwen3-8B 上验证器反馈提升反例接受与安全完成，但严格完成和判定准确率未改善，增益源于见证结构校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学智能体常用答案准确率或证明接受度评估，缺少对提出声明进行可执行证据审计的基准；需将声明分类与可执行证据分开。

Method: 构建 ClaimScope，含 72 项任务（优化器状态传输、有限时域仿射递推、精确 2×2 线性代数）。评估 Qwen3-8B：直接生成与四种匹配两遍条件，均从同一草稿开始；见证检查器只看模型提交状态，不参考参考判定、不提供替代见证。

Result: 验证器反馈在无效声明上获 23/39 接受反例、25/72 安全完成，优于无检查器通用反馈的 15/39 和 20/72；严格完成仍 17/72，判定准确率 47/72 降至 46/72；按声明分组安全完成差异 .094，bootstrap 95% 区间 [.000,.198]；七项任务级增益均符合时域/输入长度诊断；事后仅模式校正直接输出也达 25 个安全任务。

Conclusion: 结果区分声明分类与可执行证据，增益来自见证结构校正，而非更广泛推理能力提升。

中文摘要：数学智能体通常通过答案准确率或证明接受度来评估。我们引入 ClaimScope，一个包含 72 项任务的基准，用于通过判定、范围、反例和修复来审计所提出的声明。任务涵盖优化器状态传输、有限时域仿射递推以及精确的 2×2 线性代数。我们评估 Qwen3-8B，采用直接生成和四种匹配的两遍条件，每个条件都从同一草稿开始。一个见证检查器仅评估模型提交的状态，不参考参考答案的判定，也不提供替代见证。其反馈仍然传达有关有效性的信息：经核实的反驳确立该声明为假，而拒绝原因可以提示结构性修复。在此评估中，验证器反馈在无效声明上产生 23/39 个被接受的反例和 25/72 个安全完成，相比之下，无检查器的通用反馈为 15/39 和 20/72。严格完成仍为 17/72；判定准确率从 47/72 变为 46/72。按声明分组的安全完成差异为 .094，bootstrap 95% 区间为 [.000,.198]。所有七项任务级增益都遵循时域/输入长度诊断。对直接输出进行事后仅模式校正，可在不进行另一次模型调用的情况下达到相同的 25 个安全任务。这些结果将声明分类与可执行证据分开，同时将观察到的增益定位在见证结构校正上，而不是证明更广泛的推理改进。

Original abstract: Mathematical agents are commonly evaluated by answer accuracy or proof acceptance. We introduce ClaimScope, a 72-task benchmark for auditing proposed claims through a verdict, scope, counterexample, and repair. Tasks cover optimizer-state transport, finite-horizon affine recurrences, and exact $2\times2$ linear algebra. We evaluate Qwen3-8B with direct generation and four matched two-pass conditions, each starting from the same draft. A witness checker evaluates only the model's submitted state, without consulting reference verdicts or supplying a replacement witness. Its feedback still conveys information about validity: a verified refutation establishes that the claim is false, and rejection reasons can suggest structural fixes. In this evaluation, verifier feedback yields $23/39$ accepted counterexamples on invalid claims and $25/72$ safe completions, compared with $15/39$ and $20/72$ for checker-free generic feedback. Strict completion remains $17/72$; verdict accuracy changes from $47/72$ to $46/72$. The claim-grouped safe-completion difference is $.094$, with a bootstrap 95\% interval of $[.000,.198]$. All seven task-level gains follow horizon/input-length diagnostics. A post-hoc schema-only correction of the direct outputs reaches the same 25 safe tasks without another model call. These results separate claim classification from executable evidence, while locating the observed gains in witness-structure correction rather than demonstrating broader reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [49] [Evaluating Inquiry Learning in LLM-Based Agents: Hypothesizing, Reasoning, and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21604)
*Marta Knežević,Seyed Parsa Neshaei,Jibril Frej,Vinitra Swamy,Tanja Käser*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: EMNLP 2026 (Findings)

Selection reason: 在交互环境中评测LLM agent的假设生成、探索与迭代修正，提出通用agent探究能力评测范式。

TL;DR: 该研究提出统一交互式框架评估LLMs的科学探究能力，将Countdown任务扩展为可操控变量、收集观察并推断关系的数学环境；七个LLMs整体探究能力有限，但教育支架式结构化引导可显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学探究依赖探索、假设生成与观察推理的迭代整合，但既有研究多孤立评估LLMs的单项能力，未检验其在完整探究循环中的整合表现。

Method: 提出统一、交互式的探究能力评估框架，将Countdown任务扩展为数学环境，让智能体通过操纵变量、收集观察并推断关系来探测系统；在七个LLMs和多种提示策略下分析探索策略、因果发现与假设精炼，并测试受人类探究学习启发的结构化引导。

Result: 实验表明LLMs的固有探究能力有限，发现底层表达式的成功率低；但结构化引导能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在策略性探索和迭代假设精炼方面仍有明显局限；面向人类学习者的简单引导策略可迁移用于支持LLMs更有效的探究行为。

中文摘要：科学探究涉及探索、假设生成以及基于观察进行推理的迭代循环，以揭示复杂系统中的关系。大型语言模型（LLMs）在该过程的若干组成部分中已展现出强大能力，包括归纳推理、假设生成和数据分析。然而，先前工作通常孤立地评估这些能力，因而未能回答模型如何在完整探究循环中整合它们。在这项工作中，我们引入一个框架，用于在统一、交互式的设定中评估LLMs的探究能力。我们将Countdown任务扩展到一个数学环境，其中智能体通过操纵变量、收集观察并推断它们之间的关系来探测系统。该设置使得能够分析探究过程中的探索策略、因果发现和假设精炼。在七个LLMs和多种提示策略上，我们的实验揭示了有限的固有探究能力以及发现底层表达式时的低成功率。然而，受教育支架启发提供的结构化引导显著提升了表现。我们的发现凸显了LLMs在策略性探索和迭代假设精炼方面的当前局限，并表明为人类学习者在探究学习中开发的简单引导策略如何能够支持更有效的探究行为。

Original abstract: Scientific inquiry involves iterative cycles of exploration, hypothesis generation, and reasoning over observations to uncover relationships in complex systems. Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in several components of this process, including inductive reasoning, hypothesis generation, and data analysis. However, prior work typically evaluates these abilities in isolation, leaving open how models integrate them across a full inquiry cycle. In this work, we introduce a framework for evaluating LLMs' inquiry capabilities in a unified, interactive setting. We extend the Countdown task to a mathematical environment where an agent probes the system by manipulating variables, collecting observations, and inferring relationships between them. This setup enables analysis of exploration strategies, causal discovery, and hypothesis refinement during inquiry. Across seven LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, our experiments reveal limited inherent inquiry capabilities and low success rates in discovering the underlying expressions. However, structured guidance inspired by educational scaffolding significantly improves performance. Our findings highlight current limitations of LLMs in strategic exploration and iterative hypothesis refinement, and show how simple guidance strategies developed for human learners in inquiry learning can support more effective inquiry behavior.

</details>


### [50] [AgentPerturb: Evaluating LLM Agent Robustness under Controlled Interaction Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21624)
*Yuanjian Xu,Jinyi Han,Jianing Hao,Yuxuan Sun,Fanyang Lu,Richeng Xuan,Guang Zhang,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 10 figures. Project repository: https://github.com/xuyj233/AgentPerturb

Selection reason: 系统刻画LLM agent在交互扰动下的鲁棒性，发现agent不做验证即传播错误信息，对agent可靠性设计与验证机制高度可迁移。

TL;DR: 本文以扰动目标与扰动类型构成的二维视角，在 APEX-Agents 和 Toolathlon 上统一评估 LLM 智能体鲁棒性；发现扰动普遍降低性能、智能体难以识别输入变化且常传播污染信息，成功扰动轨迹成本更高，并提出面向鲁棒性的训练方向与基准构建流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务复杂且含噪，给 LLM 智能体带来显著挑战；但现有评估缺少同时刻画潜在扰动目标与扰动类型的系统框架，因此需要对前沿模型鲁棒性进行统一研究并揭示扰动致败机制。

Method: 采用扰动目标与扰动类型的二维视角，在 APEX-Agents 和 Toolathlon 上进行受控评估，结合轨迹级分析与成本分析，量化当前前沿模型的鲁棒性、追踪扰动引发失败的机制，并提供严谨的基准构建流程。

Result: 扰动持续降低智能体性能，主要脆弱性因任务环境而异；轨迹级分析显示智能体很少识别被改变的输入，常未经验证传播污染信息；聚焦成本分析表明成功的受扰动轨迹可能需要显著更多计算。

Conclusion: 研究启示面向鲁棒性的训练应覆盖多样噪声形式，并奖励验证、不确定性意识与恢复；作者提供严谨的基准构建流程，代码与数据将在 https://github.com/xuyj233/AgentPerturb 发布。

中文摘要：现实世界任务通常复杂且充满噪声，这给LLM智能体带来了巨大挑战。然而，现有评估缺乏一个系统性的框架，无法联合刻画潜在的扰动目标与扰动类型。我们利用这一二维视角开展了一项统一研究，量化当前前沿模型的鲁棒性，追踪扰动引发失败的机制，并为面向鲁棒性的训练得出启示。通过在 APEX-Agents 和 Toolathlon 上进行受控评估，我们发现扰动会持续降低性能，而主要脆弱性因任务环境而异。轨迹级分析显示，智能体很少能识别被改变的输入，并且常常在未经验证的情况下传播被污染的信息。一项聚焦成本的分析进一步表明，成功的受扰动轨迹可能需要显著更多的计算。这些发现启发了涵盖多种噪声形式并奖励验证、不确定性意识与恢复的训练目标。我们提供了一个严谨的基准构建流程。代码与数据将在 https://github.com/xuyj233/AgentPerturb 发布。

Original abstract: Real-world tasks are often complex and noisy, posing substantial challenges for LLM agents. Yet existing evaluations lack a systematic framework for jointly characterizing potential perturbation targets and types. We use this two-dimensional view to conduct a unified study that quantifies the robustness of current frontier models, traces the mechanisms behind perturbation-induced failures, and draws implications for robustness-oriented training. Through controlled evaluations on APEX-Agents and Toolathlon, we find that perturbations consistently degrade performance, while the dominant vulnerabilities vary across task environments. Trajectory-level analysis reveals that agents rarely recognize altered inputs and often propagate corrupted information without verification. A focused cost analysis further shows that successful perturbed trajectories can require substantially more computation. These findings motivate training objectives that cover diverse forms of noise and reward verification, uncertainty awareness, and recovery. We provide a rigorous benchmark-construction pipeline. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/xuyj233/AgentPerturb.

</details>


### [51] [The Self-Improvement Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21646)
*Brian B. Moser,Stanislav Frolov,Tobias C. Nauen,Federico Raue,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 形式化自我改进中自动门控（奖励模型/评审）的可靠性-非保守性不可兼得问题，并给出有界评估可恢复保证的条件，直接启发自我优化agent的接受判据设计。

TL;DR: 论文形式化智能体自我改进中自动门控的可靠性问题：在无界通用设定下，任何终止门控或搜索器都无法同时做到可靠且非保守；但在有界评估与有限提案预算下，穷举模拟可恢复保证，且仍存在有界分数上升而真实分数下降的接受更新链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益自主，会修改自身组件；安全自我改进需要自动门控（如奖励模型或自动评审器）可靠判断更新是否真正带来语义改进。核心矛盾在于门控必须可终止、可计算，而语义改进依赖可能无界的程序行为。

Method: 将智能体建模为程序，用不一定可计算的语义分数定义改进；研究两类评估抽象：认证器（批准或拒绝更新）和搜索过程（在存在改进候选时输出一个）。分析完全一般设定、随机化有界错误门控，以及有界评估加有限提案预算的可判定情形。

Result: 在完全一般设定下，不存在终止过程能同时保证可靠性（不接受非改进）与非保守性（不遗漏改进）；自指构造可迫使任何门控或搜索器失败，随机化有界错误门控同样受限。在有界评估和有限提案预算下，穷举模拟给出具有所需保证的终止搜索过程。但恢复有限：存在被接受更新链，使每轮有界分数上升而真实分数下降。

Conclusion: 结果划清了边界：无界普遍设定中正确性保证会崩溃，而有界设定中可通过设计恢复保证；自动门控的安全自我改进只能在受限、可判定的条件下获得严格保证。

中文摘要：随着大语言模型的兴起，智能体系统正变得越来越自主：它们编写代码、调用工具、修改提示词，并提出对自身组件的修改。这种自主性对安全自我改进提出了一个根本性问题：像奖励模型或自动评审器这样的自动化门控，能否可靠地决定一个被提议的更新是否应被接受？困难是结构性的。门控必须是一个可终止、可计算的过程，而我们最终关心的属性，即一个更新是否是真正的语义改进，取决于可能无界的程序行为。我们通过将智能体建模为程序，并以无须可计算的语义分数来定义改进，从而形式化这种错配。我们研究两种评估抽象。第一种是认证器，它批准或拒绝一个更新。第二种是搜索过程，给定一个提案生成器，它尝试在存在某个改进候选时输出该候选。我们表明，在完全一般的设定下，不存在终止过程能够同时保证可靠性（绝不接受非改进）和非保守性（绝不遗漏可用的改进）：自指构造能够迫使任何这样的门控或搜索器失败。具有有界错误的随机化门控面临同样的限制。随后，我们识别出实践中使用的一种标准可判定情形（有界评估和有限提案预算），在该情形下，穷举模拟产生一个具有所需保证的终止搜索过程。我们还展示了这种恢复的极限：一串被接受的更新可以在每一轮都提高有界分数，而真实分数却下降。我们的结果在无界、普遍的设定（其中正确性保证崩溃）与有界设定（其中正确性保证可通过设计恢复）之间划出了边界。

Original abstract: With the rise of large language models, agentic systems are becoming increasingly autonomous, as they write code, call tools, revise prompts, and propose modifications to their own components. This autonomy raises a fundamental question for safe self-improvement: can an automated gate like a reward model or an automated reviewer reliably decide whether a proposed update should be accepted? The difficulty is structural. The gate must be a terminating, computable procedure, while the property we ultimately care about, namely whether an update is a genuine semantic improvement, depends on potentially unbounded program behavior. We formalize this mismatch by modeling agents as programs and defining improvement in terms of a semantic score that need not be computable. We study two evaluation abstractions. The first is a certifier that approves or rejects an update. The second is a search procedure that, given access to a proposal generator, attempts to output some improving candidate if one exists. We show that, in the fully general setting, no terminating procedure can simultaneously guarantee reliability (never accepting a non-improvement) and non-conservatism (never missing an available improvement): self-referential constructions can force any such gate or searcher to fail. Randomized gates with bounded error face the same limit. We then identify a standard decidable regime used in practice (bounded evaluation and a finite proposal budget) under which exhaustive simulation yields a terminating search procedure with the desired guarantees. We also show the limit of this recovery: a chain of accepted updates can raise the bounded score in every round while the true score falls. Our results draw a boundary between unbounded, universal settings where correctness guarantees collapse and bounded settings where they can be recovered by design.

</details>


### [52] [Data World: Scaling Data Environments from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21656)
*Zhanli Li,Zeyuan Xu,Chaoxu Pang,Zeyuan Liu,Anyang Ji,Te Sun,Kongcheng Zhang,Wenbo Xu,Xubin Yue,Shipeng Luo,Junjie Zhao,Yingfan Hua,Jingqing Ruan,Zhongda Su,Feng Hong,Zhizhao Zeng,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Hanjia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Code: https://github.com/Zhanli-Li/Data-World/ Data: https://huggingface.co/collections/Zhanli-Li/dataworld Project page: https://zhanli-li.github.io/Data-World/

Selection reason: 多agent引擎自底向上合成数据环境、任务与评分规则并支持RL训练，属可迁移的agent训练/评测环境构建方法。

TL;DR: 提出 Data World 多智能体引擎，先指定文件背后的世界机制，再合成环境、任务与评分标准，构建 DataWorld-Bench 与训练任务；十个前沿模型仍面临挑战，且 RL-zero 训练显著提升数据智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体训练受限于环境策展成本高、隐私与知识产权约束，以及缺乏已知生成真值时开放式发现难以验证。

Method: 先指定文件背后的世界，构建 Data World 多智能体引擎，从显式机制合成环境、任务和评分标准；通过独立审计确保目标可恢复性，并用人类偏好筛选问题真实性，构建 DataWorld-Bench 与训练任务；在此基础上进行 RL-zero 训练。

Result: DataWorld-Bench 覆盖 50 个保留世界和 16 个领域共 100 个任务，训练集包含来自 1,037 个不相交世界的 2,135 个任务；十个前沿模型表现仍具挑战，尤其在洞见发现上存在计算-洞见差距；DataWorld-35B-A3B 在 DataWorld-Bench 提升 7.06 分、DAComp 提升 14.28 分、DABstep-Hard 提升 20.90 分、JobBench 提升 12.13 分。

Conclusion: 先指定世界再生成文件的合成范式可低成本、可验证地扩展数据智能体训练，并显著提升模型在数据分析与更广泛职业基准上的表现。

中文摘要：数据智能体使用代码处理异构文件并得出分析洞见。扩展其训练面临昂贵环境策展、隐私和知识产权限制，以及在没有已知生成真值的情况下验证开放式发现的困难。我们的核心洞见是：在生成文件本身之前，先指定文件背后的世界。我们提出 Data World，一个多智能体引擎，从显式机制合成环境、任务和评分标准。独立审计检查从观察文件中恢复目标的可恢复性；评分标准支持评估和 RL 奖励。基于人类偏好的问题真实性策展选取了 100 个任务，覆盖 50 个保留世界和 16 个领域，用于 DataWorld-Bench。该引擎还从 1,037 个不相交世界生成 2,135 个训练任务。对十个前沿模型的评估表明，该基准仍然具有挑战性，尤其在洞见发现方面，并存在一致的计算-洞见差距。在合成任务上进行 RL-zero 训练，从 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 得到 DataWorld-35B-A3B，且无需轨迹蒸馏。在匹配评估下，它相较其基座模型在 DataWorld-Bench 上提升 7.06 分，在 DAComp 上提升 14.28 分，在 DABstep-Hard 上提升 20.90 分，在 JobBench（一个更广泛的职业基准）上提升 12.13 分。

Original abstract: Data agents use code to process heterogeneous files and derive analytical insights. Scaling their training faces costly environment curation, privacy and intellectual-property restrictions, and difficulty verifying open-ended findings without known generative truth. Our core insight is to specify the world behind the files before generating the files themselves. We introduce Data World, a multi-agent engine that synthesizes environments, tasks, and rubrics from explicit mechanisms. Independent audits check target recoverability from observed files; rubrics support evaluation and RL rewards. Human preference-based curation for question realism selects 100 tasks across 50 held-out worlds and 16 domains for DataWorld-Bench. The engine also yields 2,135 training tasks from 1,037 disjoint worlds. Evaluation of ten frontier models shows that the benchmark remains challenging, particularly for insight discovery, with a consistent computation-insight gap. RL-zero training on the synthesized tasks produces DataWorld-35B-A3B from Qwen3.6-35B-A3B without trajectory distillation. Under matched evaluation, it improves over its base by 7.06 points on DataWorld-Bench, 14.28 on DAComp, 20.90 on DABstep-Hard, and 12.13 on JobBench, a broader occupational benchmark.

</details>


### [53] [DeciCAD: Harness-Grounded Learning of Action Policies for CAD Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21755)
*Yufeng Peng,Yixin Zhang,Ziyi Liu,Zhikun Xu,Jiarong Yang,Sijie Wang,Wenjie Feng,Qiyu Kang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出harness决定agent动作空间与决策观测，并从轨迹中提取因果决策监督结合SFT/在线RL，对SemOpt式harness与策略学习有直接启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出领域原生CAD智能体框架DeciCAD，通过语义动作、决策观察、反馈和持久状态定义决策空间，并结合轨迹因果监督、SFT与几何接地在线RL训练策略。在四个CAD基准上取得最优F1/IoU，输出token减少45--88%，SFT将F1从0.6883提升至0.7437，RL后几何可用性从66.5%升至73.5%、F1达0.7880。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体行为受agent harness所定义的决策空间影响，而CAD具有持久状态、几何约束和长时程流程等专业特性，通用编码框架未必适配。因此，需要研究操作空间如何影响CAD中的智能体行为与策略学习。

Method: 提出DeciCAD领域原生harness，暴露语义CAD动作、决策导向观察、环境反馈和持久状态转移；基于交互轨迹提取因果决策监督，使用SFT和几何接地的在线RL改进策略；在四个CAD基准上与通用编码harness等进行对比评估。

Result: 在四个CAD基准上，DeciCAD取得最佳F1和IoU，并将模型输出token相对通用编码harness减少45--88%。在匹配监督下，其SFT策略将F1从0.6883提升至0.7437并优于编码策略；后续在线RL将几何可用性从66.5%提升至73.5%，F1提升至0.7880。

Conclusion: 由harness诱导的决策空间会实质性地影响CAD中的智能体执行与策略学习；领域原生框架结合因果监督和在线RL能同时提升性能与效率。

中文摘要：大型语言模型智能体通过闭环动作策略运作，其决策空间由智能体框架（agent harness）定义。我们研究这种操作空间如何塑造计算机辅助设计（CAD）中的智能体行为与策略学习；CAD是一个以持续状态、几何约束和长时程流程为特征的专业领域。我们在DeciCAD中实例化了这一视角，DeciCAD是一个领域原生框架，暴露语义CAD动作、面向决策的观察、环境反馈和持续状态转移。基于该框架，我们开发了一条学习流水线，从交互轨迹中提取因果决策监督，并通过有监督微调（SFT）和几何接地的在线强化学习（RL）改进策略。在四个CAD基准上，DeciCAD取得了最佳的F1和交并比（IoU），同时相对于通用编码框架将模型输出token减少了45--88%。在匹配监督下，其SFT策略将F1从0.6883提升至0.7437，并优于编码策略；随后的在线RL进一步将几何可用性从66.5%提升至73.5%，并将F1提升至0.7880。这些结果表明，由框架诱导的决策空间会实质性地影响CAD中的智能体执行和策略学习。

Original abstract: Large Language Model agents operate through closed-loop action policies whose decision space is defined by an agent harness. We investigate how this operating space shapes agent behavior and policy learning in computer-aided design (CAD), a professional domain characterized by persistent state, geometric constraints, and long-horizon procedures. We instantiate this perspective in DeciCAD, a domain-native harness that exposes semantic CAD actions, decision-oriented observations, environment feedback, and persistent state transitions. Building on this harness, we develop a learning pipeline that extracts causal decision supervision from interaction trajectories and improves the policy through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and geometry-grounded online reinforcement learning (RL). Across four CAD benchmarks, DeciCAD achieves the best F1 and intersection over union (IoU) while reducing model-output tokens by 45--88% relative to a general-purpose coding harness. Under matched supervision, its SFT policy improves F1 from 0.6883 to 0.7437 and outperforms the coding policy; subsequent online RL further raises geometry availability from 66.5% to 73.5% and F1 to 0.7880. These results demonstrate that the decision space induced by a harness materially affects both agent execution and policy learning in CAD.

</details>


### [54] [From Research Goals to Scientific Closure: Evaluating Agents Through Evidence-Supported Physics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21767)
*Yijia Mao,Song Dai,Tao Chen,Xiangyu Lu,Linqian Zeng,Chenxi Hu,Sitong Zhou,Dongyang He,Xiaodan Mao,Linxuan Zhang,Tong Zhu,Yafu Li,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Feng He,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以证据支撑的科研闭环为准则评测agent长程研究能力，其'局部得分高但闭环不足'的评测设计可迁移到长程agent评测。

TL;DR: 提出PhysClosureBench基准，用原子分数和RC0–RC4评估AI智能体能否完成研究闭合；七个前沿模型局部得分高但完整闭合率低，显示持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI智能体能否组织科学研究，并得出有充分支持的结论。

Method: 构建包含50个经专家验证、目标明确的研究任务的PhysClosureBench，涵盖强场、阿秒及邻近物理领域；任务给定研究目标与基本科学约束，由智能体自行组织研究并生成、验证证据；采用20项标准的原子分数衡量局部科学成就，并用RC0–RC4评估研究闭合度；测试七个前沿LLM。

Result: Claude-Opus-5取得最高平均原子分数6.29/10，但仅32.7%的尝试达到RC4；36.2%的得分至少为8/10的尝试仍未达到RC4；所有七个模型在三次运行中均达到RC4的任务数，显著少于至少一次运行中达到RC4的任务数；案例分析显示结果误读和未解决的物理适用性限制会导致闭合不完整。

Conclusion: AI智能体的局部科学成就与实现完整研究闭合的能力之间存在持续差距。

中文摘要：AI智能体能否组织科学研究，从而得出有充分支持的结论？为评估这一能力，我们提出PhysClosureBench，这是一个包含50个经专家验证、目标明确的研究任务的基准，涵盖强场、阿秒及邻近物理领域。每项任务指定研究目标与基本科学约束，同时让智能体自行组织研究，并决定如何生成和验证回答问题所需的证据。一个包含20项标准的原子分数（Atomic Score）衡量局部科学成就，而研究闭合度（Research Closure，RC0–RC4）则根据不可或缺的证据要求与适用范围条件是否得到满足，评估研究在多大程度上解决了指定研究问题。我们评估了七个前沿LLM，其中Claude-Opus-5取得最高平均原子分数（6.29/10），但仅在32.7%的尝试中达到RC4。在整个基准上，36.2%的得分至少为8/10的尝试仍未达到RC4，而所有七个模型在三次运行中均达到RC4的任务数量，都显著少于至少一次运行中达到RC4的任务数量。案例层面分析进一步说明，对生成结果的误读以及未解决的物理适用性限制，如何导致不完整的闭合。总之，这些结果揭示了AI智能体在局部科学成就与实现完整研究闭合能力之间存在持续差距。

Original abstract: Can AI agents organize scientific investigations that lead to well-supported conclusions? To evaluate this capability, we introduce PhysClosureBench, a benchmark of 50 expert-verified, goal-specified research tasks spanning strong-field, attosecond, and neighboring physics areas. Each task specifies the research objective and essential scientific constraints, while leaving the agent to organize the investigation and determine how to generate and validate the evidence needed to answer the question. A 20-criterion Atomic Score measures local scientific accomplishment, while Research Closure (RC0--RC4) assesses how fully the investigation resolves the specified research question based on whether its indispensable evidence requirements and applicable scope conditions are satisfied. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs, where Claude-Opus-5 achieves the highest mean Atomic Score (6.29/10) but reaches RC4 in only 32.7% of attempts. Across the benchmark, 36.2% of attempts scoring at least 8/10 still fall short of RC4, while all seven models reach RC4 on substantially fewer tasks in all three runs than in at least one. Case-level analysis further illustrates how misinterpretation of generated results and unresolved physical applicability limits can lead to incomplete closure. Together, these results reveal a persistent gap between AI agents' local scientific accomplishments and their ability to achieve complete research closure.

</details>
