### [55] [RSIFlow: Adaptive Self-Improvement Workflows with an Evolving Meta-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21791)
*Huan Zhang,Mingju Chen,Dongxu Zhou,Can Lv,Sen Cui,Faguo Wu,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 递归自我改进框架，从轨迹诊断瓶颈自适应选择改进工作流并进化meta-harness，与自动代码优化agent的harness进化高度同构。

TL;DR: RSIFlow 是一个递归自我改进框架，通过自适应工作流编排与元智能体能力演化，动态诊断瓶颈并改进工作流，在六个基准和两个骨干模型上取得 SOTA 平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进方法虽能提升 LLM 智能体性能，但难以让自我改进工作流适应不断变化的性能瓶颈，也难以利用执行反馈持续改进工作流编排。

Method: RSIFlow 从任务-智能体轨迹和环境反馈中诊断性能瓶颈，动态选择组件特定的改进工作流；元智能体将工作流结果蒸馏为组件特定技能和跨组件原则，并演化自身执行框架以优化后续工作流编排。

Result: 在六个基准和两个骨干模型上，RSIFlow 在所有基准上达到 SOTA 平均性能；消融研究验证了自适应工作流编排和元执行框架演化的贡献。

Conclusion: RSIFlow 将自适应工作流编排与元智能体能力演化结合，有效实现递归自我改进，并持续提升 LLM 智能体在复杂任务中的表现。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在复杂任务中展现出强大能力，包括代码生成、工具使用和多跳问答。现有的自我改进方法通过模型优化、执行框架（harness）演化和可执行产物精炼来提升智能体性能，而递归自我改进（RSI）进一步将优化扩展到改进机制本身。然而，如何使自我改进工作流适应不断演化的性能瓶颈，同时通过执行反馈改进工作流编排，仍是一个关键挑战。我们提出 RSIFlow，一个递归自我改进框架，将自适应工作流编排与元智能体能力演化相结合。RSIFlow 从任务-智能体轨迹和环境反馈中诊断性能瓶颈，以动态选择组件特定的改进工作流。元智能体随后将工作流结果蒸馏为组件特定技能和跨组件原则，演化其自身的执行框架，以改进后续工作流编排。在六个基准和两个骨干模型上的实验表明，RSIFlow 在所有基准上达到 SOTA 平均性能。消融研究进一步证明了自适应工作流编排和元执行框架演化的贡献。代码见 https://github.com/1HUAN1/RSI-Flow。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex tasks, including code generation, tool use, and multi-hop question answering. Existing self-improvement methods enhance agent performance through model optimization, harness evolution, and executable artifact refinement, while recursive self-improvement (RSI) further extends optimization to the improvement mechanisms themselves. However, adapting self-improvement workflows to evolving performance bottlenecks while improving workflow orchestration through execution feedback remains a key challenge. We propose \textbf{RSIFlow}, a \textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{I}mprovement framework that integrates adaptive work\textbf{flow} orchestration with meta-agent capability evolution. RSIFlow diagnoses performance bottlenecks from task-agent trajectories and environmental feedback to dynamically select component-specific improvement workflows. The meta-agent then distills workflow outcomes into component-specific skills and cross-component principles, evolving its own harness to improve subsequent workflow orchestration. Experiments across six benchmarks and two backbone models show that RSIFlow achieves the SOTA average performance on all benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of adaptive workflow orchestration and meta-harness evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/1HUAN1/RSI-Flow.

</details>


### [56] [CoWorld Reasoner: Agentic Multimodal Reasoning with the Evolving Video World Model-Driven Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21910)
*Dongjie Cheng,Ruifeng Yuan,Ran Wei,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以工具/技能形式暴露世界模型操作并做harness与执行代码的共同进化，工具选择与harnes自演化机制可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: CoWorld Reasoner 是一个以视频世界模型为统一视觉推理引擎的智能体框架，通过工具与技能灵活调用视觉想象，并经协同演化改进工具、技能和执行代码，在七个推理基准上超过直接推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型需要超越静态图像内容进行运动、动作和视角变化的时空推理；视频世界模型可生成额外视觉证据，但现有方法受限于围绕预定义视觉想象形式的固定流程，难以适应多样化推理任务。

Method: 提出 CoWorld Reasoner 智能体框架，将 VWM 作为统一视觉推理引擎；其视频世界模型驱动 Harness 通过工具暴露多种视觉操作，并用技能引导 VLM 决定是否想象、选择操作、构建生成请求和解释视觉证据；同时提出 VLM-VWM Harness 协同演化，在固定模型权重下根据执行反馈联合修正工具、技能和执行代码。

Result: 在涵盖空间智能、时间与因果推理和感知推理的七个基准上，CoWorld Reasoner 优于同一 VLM 的直接推理；最强变体在全部七个基准上提升，MMSI 相对准确率提升 11.8%，ERQA 提升 13.0%，并在五个基准上优于所有五个使用相同骨干网络的演化基线。

Conclusion: 以视频世界模型为统一引擎的智能体式视觉想象与协同演化机制，能够提升 VLM 在多样推理任务中的表现，并优于固定流程和演化基线。

中文摘要：随着视觉语言模型（VLM）的进步，人们日益期望它们能够对运动、动作和视角变化执行超出输入图像所示内容的时空推理。视频世界模型（VWM）通过生成任务相关的视觉内容作为额外证据，为视觉想象提供了一种自然的统一引擎。然而，现有方法通常通过以预定义形式的视觉想象（例如新视角合成）为中心的固定流程来调用 VWM。为支持面向多样化推理任务的灵活视觉想象，我们提出 CoWorld Reasoner，这是一种将 VWM 用作统一视觉推理引擎的智能体框架。其视频世界模型驱动测试框架（Harness）通过工具暴露多种视觉操作，并使用技能引导 VLM 决定是否进行想象、选择操作、构建生成请求以及解释生成的视觉证据。我们进一步提出 VLM-VWM 测试框架协同演化（VLM-VWM Harness Co-Evolution），它基于执行反馈联合修正工具、技能和执行代码，同时保持两个模型的权重固定。在涵盖空间智能、时间与因果推理以及感知推理的七个基准上，CoWorld Reasoner 优于使用同一 VLM 的直接推理。最强变体在全部七个基准上均有提升，包括在 MMSI 上 11.8% 和在 ERQA 上 13.0% 的相对准确率提升，并在五个基准上优于所有五个使用相同骨干网络的演化基线。

Original abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) advance, they are increasingly expected to perform spatiotemporal reasoning over motion, actions, and view changes beyond what the input images show. Video World Models (VWMs) provide a natural unified engine for visual imagination by generating task-relevant visual content as additional evidence. However, existing approaches typically invoke a VWM through fixed pipelines centered on a predefined form of visual imagination, such as novel-view synthesis. To support flexible visual imagination for diverse reasoning tasks, we introduce CoWorld Reasoner, an agentic framework that uses a VWM as a unified visual reasoning engine. Its Video World Model-Driven Harness exposes multiple visual operations through tools and uses skills to guide the VLM in deciding whether to imagine, selecting an operation, constructing a generation request, and interpreting the resulting visual evidence. We further introduce VLM-VWM Harness Co-Evolution, which jointly revises tools, skills, and execution code based on execution feedback while keeping both model weights fixed. Across seven benchmarks spanning spatial intelligence, temporal and causal reasoning, and perceptual reasoning, CoWorld Reasoner outperforms direct reasoning with the same VLM. The strongest variant improves on all seven benchmarks, including relative accuracy gains of 11.8% on MMSI and 13.0% on ERQA, and outperforms all five evolving baselines using the same backbone across five benchmarks.

</details>


### [57] [OntoAct: Hypothesis-Based Neuro-Symbolic Agents through Ontological Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21983)
*Donghyeok Lee,Bong Gyun Kang,Junyong Ahn,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 49 pages, 13 figures, 33 tables. Preprint, under review

Selection reason: 神经符号agent将经验抽象为本体公理，由LLM提假设、符号验证器查证缺失证据并驱动后续动作，属可迁移的规划-验证-经验复用架构。

TL;DR: OntoAct 是一个基于本体抽象的神经符号框架，将个体经验转化为可复用公理；LLM 提出假设与行动，符号验证器依据观测评估条件并迭代更新。另提出 Tacit-FLE 基准，用于交互式诊断与修复工厂隐藏故障。在 Tacit-FLE、Mars 和 ALFWorld 上，OntoAct 以高效交互和推理取得领先任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经验中学习需要恢复可复用的知识，并确定其何时适用于新情境。

Method: 提出 OntoAct：基于本体抽象的假设驱动神经符号框架。它将个体经验转化为关于实体类型、关系和条件的公理，使潜在模式跨情境复用；LLM 利用该世界知识提出假设与行动，符号验证器根据当前观测评估支持条件，验证识别缺失证据，新观测与干预结果更新假设评估并指导后续行动。同时提出 Tacit-FLE 基准，基于 Factorio 学习环境（FLE），利用继承的运营记录诊断和修复现有工厂中的隐藏故障；其合成维护语料库将证据分散于记录中，已执行修复可能未解决故障，需智能体通过交互重新评估诊断。

Result: 在 Tacit-FLE、Mars 和 ALFWorld 上，OntoAct 以高效的交互和推理实现了领先的任务性能。

Conclusion: OntoAct 通过本体抽象与神经符号验证，将经验转化为可复用公理并动态更新假设，从而在复杂任务中实现高效且领先的表现；Tacit-FLE 为隐藏故障诊断与修复提供了交互式评估基准。

中文摘要：从经验中学习需要恢复可复用的知识，并确定其何时适用于新情境。我们提出 OntoAct，一种基于本体抽象、以假设为基础的神经符号框架。OntoAct 将个体经验转化为关于实体类型、关系和条件的公理，使其潜在模式可跨情境复用。大型语言模型（LLM）利用这些世界知识提出假设和行动，而符号验证器则根据当前观测评估其支持条件。验证可识别缺失证据，新的观测和干预结果会更新假设评估并指导后续行动。我们还提出 Tacit-FLE，一个基于 Factorio 学习环境（FLE）构建的基准，用于利用继承的运营记录诊断和修复现有工厂中的隐藏故障。其合成的维护语料库将证据分散在记录中，而已执行的修复可能未能解决故障，要求智能体通过交互重新评估其诊断。在 Tacit-FLE、Mars 和 ALFWorld 上，OntoAct 以高效的交互和推理实现了领先的任务性能。

Original abstract: Learning from experience requires recovering reusable knowledge and determining when it applies to a new situation. We introduce OntoAct, a hypothesis-based neuro-symbolic framework built on ontological abstraction. OntoAct turns individual experiences into axioms over entity types, relations, and conditions, making their underlying patterns reusable across situations. A large language model (LLM) uses this world knowledge to propose hypotheses and actions, while a symbolic verifier assesses their supporting conditions against current observations. Verification identifies missing evidence, and new observations and intervention outcomes update hypothesis assessments and guide subsequent actions. We also introduce Tacit-FLE, a benchmark built on the Factorio Learning Environment (FLE) for diagnosing and repairing hidden faults in existing factories using inherited operational records. Its synthetic maintenance corpus distributes evidence across records, and an executed repair may leave the fault unresolved, requiring agents to reassess their diagnoses through interaction. Across Tacit-FLE, Mars, and ALFWorld, OntoAct achieves leading task performance with efficient interaction and inference.

</details>


### [58] [CARA: Causally Guided Retrieval Agent for Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22017)
*Weicheng Liu,Xiangcheng Wu,Depeng Xu,Haopeng Zhang,Xi Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 带因果相关性判据（后果性/必要性）的检索 agent，通过证据增删评估指导图探索与多 agent 结果融合，其证据效用评估思路可迁移到 agent 的中间结果验证与剪枝。

TL;DR: CARA 是一种免训练、因果引导的检索智能体，通过后果性和必要性评估证据因果相关性，并结合路径一致性与倒数排名融合来改进知识图谱检索和下游问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体检索器虽能平衡全局搜索与多跳探索，但主要依赖文本相似度，难以识别真正有助于回答问题的证据。

Method: 提出免训练的因果引导检索智能体 CARA，用后果性（加入证据是否提升可回答性）和必要性（移除证据是否削弱可回答性）评估证据，并结合路径一致性指导图探索，使用倒数排名融合聚合多智能体证据。

Result: 在三个 STaRK 数据集及增强真实答案的 STaRK 上，CARA 持续优于现有检索器，同时提升答案质量与忠实度，并检查更少图节点、需要更少检索操作。

Conclusion: 因果相关性评估可使智能体图检索更聚焦、更有效，从而提升检索性能与下游问答表现。

中文摘要：在文本丰富的知识图谱上进行检索增强生成，需要识别有助于回答问题的证据，而不仅仅是匹配其文本内容。现有的智能体检索器能够自适应地平衡全局搜索与多跳探索，但主要依赖文本相似度来指导检索。我们提出 CARA，一种免训练、因果引导的检索智能体，它通过两个互补标准显式评估所检索证据的因果相关性：后果性（consequentiality），衡量加入证据是否提升可回答性；必要性（essentiality），评估移除证据是否削弱可回答性。CARA 将这些评估与路径一致性相结合来指导图探索，并采用倒数排名融合来聚合来自多个智能体的证据。我们在三个 STaRK 数据集上评估 CARA，并用真实答案增强 STaRK，以支持下游问答评估。实验结果表明，CARA 持续优于现有检索器。CARA 还提升了答案质量和忠实度，同时检查更少的图节点并需要更少的检索操作。这些发现表明，因果相关性评估能够实现更聚焦且更有效的智能体图检索，从而同时提升检索性能和下游问答表现。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over text-rich knowledge graphs requires identifying evidence that contributes to answering a question, rather than merely matching its textual content. Existing agentic retrievers adaptively balance global search and multi-hop exploration but primarily rely on textual similarity to guide retrieval. We propose CARA, a training-free, CAusally guided Retrieval Agent that explicitly evaluates the causal relevance of retrieved evidence through two complementary criteria: consequentiality, measuring whether adding evidence improves answerability, and essentiality, assessing whether removing evidence weakens answerability. CARA integrates these assessments with path consistency to guide graph exploration and employs reciprocal rank fusion to aggregate evidence from multiple agents. We evaluate CARA on three STaRK datasets and augment STaRK with ground-truth answers to enable downstream question answering evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that CARA consistently outperforms existing retrievers. CARA also improves answer quality and faithfulness while examining fewer graph nodes and requiring fewer retrieval operations. These findings demonstrate that causal relevance assessment enables more focused and effective agentic graph retrieval, improving both retrieval performance and downstream question answering.

</details>


### [59] [GraphACM: Graph-based Action Consistency Measurement for Uncertainty Quantification in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22031)
*Yaxin Ma,Wenbo Zhang,Satya Krishna Pothapragada,Alina Zare*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用多次 rollout 的状态-动作图度量行为一致性并预测任务成败，属于 agent 可靠性与不确定性量化方法，可直接用于评估代码优化 agent 的稳定性与失败预警。

TL;DR: 提出基于图的行为一致性框架，合并等价状态并在共享决策状态比较动作，以节点级/全局一致性量化不确定性；在三个LLM与三个环境上平均AUROC 0.811，优于最强一致性基线和言语化置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只给出单一任务级一致性分数，无法定位行为差异发生的位置，也未确保动作是在等价决策情境下比较，因此需要细粒度且状态条件化的行为一致性度量与不确定性量化。

Method: 对同一任务进行多次执行，将等价状态合并为有向、带动作标签的图，在共享决策状态上比较动作；定义节点级和全局动作一致性度量，并将全局一致性分数作为不确定性代理，用于预测任务成功或失败。

Result: 在ALFWorld、AppWorld和WebShop上对三个LLM的实验表明，该度量与任务成功正相关，任务成功预测平均AUROC为0.811，优于最强的行为一致性基线（0.729）和言语化置信度（0.769）。

Conclusion: 状态条件化的行为一致性度量有效，并能精确定位智能体动作在何处一致。

中文摘要：在重复执行中表现出的行为一致性为评估大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的可靠性提供了有信息量的信号。然而，现有方法通常用单一的任务级分数来概括一致性，既未识别行为差异发生在何处，也未判断动作是否在等价的决策情境下进行比较。我们提出一个基于图的框架，用于细粒度行为一致性测量与不确定性量化。给定同一任务的多条执行轨迹（rollouts），我们将等价状态合并为一个有向的、带动作标签的图，从而能够在共享决策状态下进行动作比较。我们引入节点级和全局的动作一致性度量，并将全局一致性分数作为不确定性的代理，用于预测任务成功或失败。在ALFWorld、AppWorld和WebShop上对三个LLM进行的实验表明，我们的度量与任务成功正相关，并在任务成功预测上取得平均AUROC 0.811，优于最强的行为一致性基线（0.729）和言语化置信度（0.769）。这些结果凸显了状态条件化行为一致性的有效性，并精确定位了智能体动作在何处一致。

Original abstract: Behavioral consistency across repeated executions provides an informative signal for assessing the reliability of large language model (LLM) agents. However, existing approaches typically summarize consistency with a single task-level score, without identifying where behavioral differences occur or whether actions are compared under equivalent decision contexts. We propose a graph-based framework for fine-grained behavioral consistency measurement and uncertainty quantification. Given multiple rollouts of the same task, we merge equivalent states into a directed, action-labeled graph, enabling action comparisons at shared decision states. We introduce node-level and global measures of action consistency, using the global consistency score as a proxy for uncertainty to predict task success or failure. Experiments across three LLMs on ALFWorld, AppWorld, and WebShop demonstrate that our measure is positively associated with task success and achieves an average AUROC of 0.811 for task-success prediction, outperforming the strongest behavioral-consistency baseline (0.729) and verbalized confidence (0.769). These results highlight the effectiveness of state-conditioned behavioral consistency and pinpoint where agent actions agree.

</details>


### [60] [FAOPs: Normal Forms and an Algebra for AI Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22061)
*Ali Mohammadi Shanghooshabad*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 28 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 把 agent 工作流形式化为类型化函数并给出四层范式、可证明等价代数与编译期优化/终止/代价约束，直接对应 agent workflow 规范化与自动优化，与代码优化 agent 高度同构。

TL;DR: 提出 FAOP 形式化理论，用四种渐进式正规形式将 AOP 规范化为可独立检查、受作用域约束的原子步骤，并在 SkillBase 中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AOP 需要可追踪、可审计、可复现、受访问控制且有时间和成本边界，但现有粗粒度文件导致步骤捆绑、副本漂移、工具访问过宽、重构无检查。

Method: 将工作流建模为作用域状态上的类型化函数，定义 FNF-1 至 FNF-4 渐进式正规形式、FAOP 等式代数、+V/+O/+B 正交扩展和 0-3 架构级分类器，并在 SkillBase 与独立执行器中实现。

Result: 每个原子步骤成为有契约的独立实体，可独立进行类型检查、作用域限制、边界约束、测试、追踪和审计；重构与并行化变为编译器重写，完整性、隔离、类型、终止、成本和优化成为编译期关注点。

Conclusion: FAOP 为 AI 智能体工作流带来数据库级纪律，使 AOP 的治理与运行时有保证；金融工作流示例展示了端到端可行性。

中文摘要：组织越来越多地将操作流程编码为多步骤的智能体技能，这里称为智能体操作流程（Agent Operating Procedures, AOPs），这些流程必须可追踪、可审计、可复现、受访问控制，并在时间和成本上有界，而这些是受监管行业强制要求。然而 AOP 通常被保存为粗粒度文件，其中步骤捆绑了多个动作，复制得到的流程会逐渐偏离，任何步骤都可能访问任何工具，重构则不受检查。我们提出函数式智能体操作流程（Functional Agent Operating Procedures, FAOP），一种将数据库级纪律引入 AI 智能体工作流的形式化理论。FAOP 将每个工作流重铸为作用域状态上的类型化函数，并使整个库接受四种渐进式正规形式：分解（FNF-1）将工作流拆分为原子步骤，类型化（FNF-2）使其数据流显式且经过类型检查，因子化（FNF-3）为每个共享过程提供单一规范定义，从而使变更传播而非漂移，作用域化（FNF-4）将每一步限制为最小权限的工具访问。一旦规范化，每个原子步骤都是具有声明的契约的独立实体，并可独立进行类型检查、作用域限制、边界约束、测试、追踪和审计。FAOP 的一个可证明可靠的等式代数将重构和并行化转化为编译器重写；正交扩展添加经验证的终止性（+V）、优化（+O）以及时间和成本边界（+B）；以及一个架构级分类器（0-3）判断工作流是否根本需要 AI。这些共同使完整性、隔离性、类型、终止性、成本和优化成为整个库的编译期关注点，而不是运行时的期望。我们在 SkillBase 中实现了该理论，这是一个 AOP 管理系统（正如 PostgreSQL 之于数据，SkillBase 之于 AOP），并配有一个独立执行器在运行时强制执行这些保证。一个经过完整演练的金融工作流示例端到端地展示了该方法。

Original abstract: Organizations increasingly encode operational procedures as multi-step agent skills, here called Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs), that must be traceable, auditable, reproducible, access-controlled, and bounded in time and cost, requirements that regulated sectors make mandatory. Yet AOPs are typically kept as coarse-grained files, in which steps bundle actions, copied procedures drift apart, any step may reach any tool, and refactoring goes unchecked. We present Functional Agent Operating Procedures (FAOP), a formal theory that brings database-grade discipline to AI agent workflows. FAOP recasts each workflow as a typed function over scoped state and subjects an entire library to four progressive normal forms: Decomposed (FNF-1) splits a workflow into atomic steps, Typed (FNF-2) makes its data flow explicit and type-checked, Factored (FNF-3) gives each shared procedure a single canonical definition so a change propagates instead of drifting, and Scoped (FNF-4) confines every step to least-privilege tool access. Once normalized, every atomic step is a separate entity with a declared contract and can be type-checked, scope-confined, bounded, tested, traced, and audited on its own. A FAOP algebra of provably sound equalities turns refactoring and parallelization into compiler rewrites; orthogonal extensions add verified termination (+V), optimization (+O), and time and cost bounds (+B); and an architectural-level classifier (0-3) determines whether a workflow needs AI at all. Together, these make integrity, isolation, typing, termination, cost, and optimization compile-time concerns across the whole library, not runtime hopes. We implement the theory in SkillBase, an AOP management system (what PostgreSQL is to data, SkillBase is to AOPs), together with a separate executor that enforces these guarantees at run time. A worked financial workflow illustrates the approach end to end.

</details>


### [61] [When Reasoning Is Not Enough: Proactive Inquiry for Cross-Modal Safety in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22068)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于风险条件不确定性决定何时提出澄清问题并做路径优化，其主动询问/过度拒绝权衡机制可迁移到 agent 的主动澄清与风险控制策略。

TL;DR: 论文识别多模态大模型在图文联合解释下的意图级风险，提出基于风险条件化不确定性的 SAFE-PRO，通过安全澄清提问与路径优化提升安全性，并构建 SSUI-Dialog 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独无害的图像与文本联合时，MLLM 可能不安全；除推理漂移的隐式风险外，还存在跨模态歧义导致模型悄然接受危险前提的意图级风险，路径级优化无法单独纠正。

Method: 提出 SAFE-PRO，利用 RCU 信号（预测熵与学习到的跨模态风险分数）决定何时提出安全澄清问题、何处进行路径优化；奖励优先安全并惩罚过度拒绝；构建带双意图监督的多模态多轮数据集 SSUI-Dialog，并理论分析 RCU 下的梯度方差与不安全补全风险。

Result: 在 7 个基准和 2 个基础 MLLM 上，SAFE-PRO 持续优于 SRPO 的安全性，过度拒绝约减半，平均单轮安全性超过 GPT-4o 与 Claude-3.5-Sonnet。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合风险条件化不确定性、安全澄清与路径优化可更有效应对多模态意图级安全风险，并在安全与过度拒绝间取得更好平衡。

中文摘要：多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在单独无害的图像和文本被联合解释时可能表现出不安全行为。除了隐式推理风险（即推理逐渐偏向有害内容）之外，我们识别出意图级风险，即跨模态歧义会悄然使模型接受一个危险前提，而仅靠路径级优化无法纠正。我们提出 SAFE-PRO，它使用风险条件化不确定性（Risk-Conditioned Uncertainty, RCU）信号，将预测熵与学习到的跨模态风险分数相结合，以决定何时提出以安全为基础的澄清性问题，以及在何处应用路径优化，其奖励优先考虑安全并惩罚过度拒绝。我们还构建了 SSUI-Dialog，一个具有双意图监督的多模态多轮数据集，并从理论上分析了 RCU 下的梯度方差和不安全补全风险。在七个基准和两个基础 MLLM 上，SAFE-PRO 在安全性上持续优于 SRPO，将其过度拒绝大致减半，并在平均单轮安全性上超过 GPT-4o 和 Claude-3.5-Sonnet。

Original abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can act unsafely when individually benign images and texts are interpreted jointly. Beyond implicit reasoning risk, where reasoning drifts toward harm, we identify intent-level risk, where cross-modal ambiguity silently commits the model to a hazardous premise that path-level optimization alone cannot correct. We propose SAFE-PRO, which uses a Risk-Conditioned Uncertainty (RCU) signal, combining predictive entropy with a learned cross-modal risk score, to decide when to ask safety-grounded clarifying questions and where to apply path optimization, under a reward that prioritizes safety and penalizes over-refusal. We also build SSUI-Dialog, a multimodal multi-turn dataset with dual-intent supervision, and theoretically analyze gradient variance and unsafe-completion risk under RCU. Across seven benchmarks and two base MLLMs, SAFE-PRO consistently improves safety over SRPO, roughly halves its over-refusal, and surpasses GPT-4o and Claude-3.5-Sonnet in average single-turn safety.

</details>


### [62] [Learning from Unverified Peers via Shared Residual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22080)
*Feiyang Chen,Runze Ma,Zichen Wang,Jiong Lou,Zihan Wang,Shuangjia Zheng,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 从自身未验证的同伴轨迹中做共享残差自蒸馏，无需正确性标注即可获得训练信号，其利用未验证轨迹的自改进机制对 agent 训练与经验复用有启发。

TL;DR: SRD 利用模型自身未验证的同伴轨迹进行同策略自蒸馏，通过相对对照比较预测分布变化，保留方向一致且幅度保守的调整；无需正确答案监督，推理时无需同伴，显著提升 AIME 成绩且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无参考答案、可验证反馈或外部教师时，模型自生成解的正确性难以可靠评估；但错误解也可能包含有用中间推理，因此监督价值不应只由最终答案决定。研究问题是：能否不先判定同伴解正确性，直接从模型自身未经验证的同伴解中学习改进。

Method: 提出 Shared Residual Distillation（SRD），一种同策略自蒸馏方法。它从模型自身未经验证的同伴轨迹中学习，比较同伴相对于对照对预测分布的改变，仅保留方向一致且幅度保守的调整，从而在不把任何完整解当作正确的前提下获得监督；训练后推理阶段不需要同伴。

Result: 从对应基础模型出发，SRD 将 AIME24--26 avg@12 从 11.02 提升至 16.20（Qwen3-1.7B），从 19.07 提升至 45.46（Qwen3-4B），从 21.39 提升至 54.44（Qwen3-8B）。受控消融支持共享残差构造的价值；在两个通用能力基准上相对基础模型未观察到退化。

Conclusion: 模型可以从自身未经验证的同伴解中学习并提升性能，而无需先判定哪些解正确；SRD 通过共享残差式、方向一致且幅度保守的蒸馏信号实现该目标，在推理时无需同伴，并在数学推理基准上大幅提升，同时不损害通用能力。

中文摘要：一个语言模型可以对同一问题生成多个解，其中一些导向正确答案，另一些则导向错误答案。许多后训练方法利用参考答案、可验证反馈或外部教师提供的监督，将这些轨迹转化为学习信号。在没有此类监督时，从模型自身生成内容中学习就不再那么直接，因为其正确性无法被可靠评估。一个错误解仍可能包含有用的中间推理，因此其作为监督的价值不应仅由其最终答案决定。模型能否在不先判定哪些同伴解正确的情况下，从自身未经验证的同伴解中学习并取得改进？我们提出共享残差蒸馏（Shared Residual Distillation, SRD），一种同策略自蒸馏方法，它从模型自身未经验证的同伴轨迹中学习。SRD 比较同伴相对于对照如何改变预测分布，保留方向一致且幅度保守的调整。这在不将任何完整解视为正确的情况下产生监督，并且训练后的模型在推理时不需要同伴。从相应基础模型出发，SRD 将平均 AIME24--26 avg@12 从 Qwen3-1.7B 上的 11.02 提升到 16.20，从 Qwen3-4B 上的 19.07 提升到 45.46，从 Qwen3-8B 上的 21.39 提升到 54.44。受控消融支持共享残差构造的价值，并且我们在两个通用能力基准上未观察到相对于基础模型的退化。

Original abstract: A language model can generate several solutions to the same problem, some leading to correct answers and others to incorrect ones. Many post-training methods turn these trajectories into learning signals using supervision from reference answers, verifiable feedback, or external teachers. Without such supervision, learning from the model's own generations becomes less straightforward, as their correctness cannot be reliably assessed. An incorrect solution may nevertheless contain useful intermediate reasoning, so its value as supervision need not be determined solely by its final answer. Can a model improve by learning from its unverified peer solutions without first determining which are correct?
  We propose Shared Residual Distillation (SRD), an on-policy self-distillation method that learns from the model's own unverified peer trajectories. SRD compares how peers change the prediction distribution relative to a control, retaining adjustments that agree in direction and are conservative in magnitude. This yields supervision without treating any complete solution as correct, and the trained model requires no peers at inference time. Starting from the corresponding base models, SRD raises mean AIME24--26 avg@12 from 11.02 to 16.20 on Qwen3-1.7B, from 19.07 to 45.46 on Qwen3-4B, and from 21.39 to 54.44 on Qwen3-8B. Controlled ablations support the value of the shared-residual construction, and we observe no degradation relative to the base models on two general-capability benchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [PROBE: Learning to Audit Policy Compliance in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22124)
*Kshitij Mishra,Abhijith Sharma,Nils Lukas,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: 将工具调用 agent 的策略合规审计形式化为双人马尔可夫博弈并训练多轮审计员（GRPO/DPO/GFlowNet 对比），属于 agent 评测、可靠性与安全控制的可迁移方法。

TL;DR: 论文提出 PROBE，通过多轮、工具中介对话训练 LLM 审计器，以发现已部署智能体的策略违规，并将其形式化为双人马尔可夫博弈，结合复合奖励与多种优化方法进行训练。实验表明，PROBE 审计器比同架构零样本对抗者发现更广、更高产出的违规，即使规模显著更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在关键空白：能力基准只测智能体能做什么，内容安全红队只测有害生成，二者都不检验智能体在原本有效的工具使用中是否遵守预定义策略；而此类违规可能延迟、跨工具调用或在下游系统才显现，难以审计。

Method: 提出 PROBE 算法，训练基于 LLM 的审计器像真实用户一样与已部署智能体进行多轮、工具中介对话，主动探查策略违规。将智能体审计形式化为双人马尔可夫博弈，设计平衡违规发现、行为合理性、对话进展和策略多样性的复合奖励，并在受控设置中比较 GRPO、DPO 与基于 GFlowNet 的优化。

Result: 大量实验显示，使用 PROBE 训练的审计器比同架构零样本对抗者发现的策略违规更广泛、产出更高，即使审计器显著小于目标智能体。

Conclusion: 多轮行为审计是智能体评估中一个独特且必要的维度，可补充能力与安全基准；PROBE 为现实世界智能体系统的自动策略审计提供了可扩展路径，并指向审计器暴露失败、智能体从审计器反馈中改进的共同演化循环。

中文摘要：现代智能体系统通过外部工具扩展语言模型，使其能够通过多轮交互完成复杂任务。随着这些智能体被部署在具有重要影响的场景中，它们常常受到管理定价、隐私、确认、安全及其他要求的策略约束。然而，现有评估方法留下了一个关键空白：能力基准衡量智能体能完成什么，内容安全红队测试衡量有害生成，但两者都没有测试智能体在原本有效的工具使用过程中是否遵守预定义策略。此类策略违规难以审计，因为其影响可能仅在若干轮之后才显现、跨越多个工具调用，或在 downstream systems 中出现。我们提出 PROBE（Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration，通过行为探索进行策略规则观察），一种用于训练基于 LLM 的审计器的算法，该审计器作为真实用户与已部署智能体交互，并通过多轮、工具中介的对话探查策略违规。我们将智能体审计形式化为一个双人马尔可夫博弈，并提出一种复合奖励，平衡违规发现、行为合理性、对话进展和策略多样性。为了研究训练目标和奖励设计，我们在受控设置中比较 GRPO、DPO 和基于 GFlowNet 的优化。在大量实验中，使用 PROBE 训练的审计器发现的策略违规比同架构零样本对抗者广泛得多且产出更高，即使审计器显著小于目标智能体。我们的结果表明，多轮行为审计是智能体评估的一个独特且必要的维度，补充了能力与安全基准。PROBE 为现实世界智能体系统的自动策略审计提供了一条可扩展路径，并暗示了一个共同演化循环，其中审计器暴露失败，智能体从审计器反馈中改进。

Original abstract: Modern agentic systems extend language models with external tools, enabling them to complete complex tasks through multi-turn interactions. As these agents are deployed in consequential settings, they are often constrained by policies governing pricing, privacy, confirmation, safety, and other requirements. However, existing evaluation methods leave a critical gap: capability benchmarks measure what agents can accomplish, while content-safety red-teaming measures harmful generations, but neither tests whether agents comply with predefined policies during otherwise valid tool use. Such policy violations are difficult to audit because their effects may emerge only after several turns, span multiple tool calls, or surface in downstream systems. We propose PROBE (Policy Rule Observation via Behavioral Exploration), an algorithm for training LLM-based auditors that interact with deployed agents as realistic users and probe them for policy violations through multi-turn, tool-mediated dialogue. We formalize agent auditing as a two-player Markov game and propose a composite reward that balances violation discovery, behavioral plausibility, conversation progress, and policy diversity. To study training objectives and reward design, we compare GRPO, DPO, and GFlowNet-based optimization in a controlled setting. Across extensive experiments, auditors trained with PROBE uncover substantially broader and higher-yield policy violations than same-architecture zero-shot adversaries, even when the auditor is significantly smaller than the target agent. Our results show that multi-turn behavioral auditing is a distinct and necessary axis of agent evaluation, complementing capability and safety benchmarks. PROBE offers a scalable path toward automated policy auditing for real-world agentic systems and suggests a co-evolutionary loop in which auditors expose failures and agents improve from auditor feedback.

</details>


### [64] [Believed but Not Said: Measuring Hidden Belief Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22138)
*Liangjie Huang,Henry Peng Zou,Wei-Chieh Huang,Wooseong Yang,Yuwei Han,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 量化多 agent 讨论中对抗意见导致的隐性信念崩塌与沉默顺从，直接揭示多 agent 协作/互检机制的脆弱性，对多 agent 验证架构设计有重要警示。

TL;DR: 在十智能体事实讨论中，少数对抗智能体可让良性智能体私下接受错误答案的比例从0.6%升至44.8%（1个对抗者）和60.5%（3个对抗者），且私下改变远多于公开支持；错误答案会传播，基线知识不预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统假设同伴互查能纠正个体错误，但该假设缺乏对最小对抗攻击的实证检验。

Method: 构建十智能体事实性讨论；设置1或3个对抗智能体反复主张貌似合理的错误答案；每轮用对同伴隐藏的单独调用探测良性智能体私下答案，并标注其公开立场；在6个模型、3个事实性QA数据集上评估。

Result: 无攻击者时仅0.6%良性智能体最终持错误答案；1个对抗者升至44.8%，3个升至60.5%。3个对抗者时，良性智能体在51.6%暴露轮次中私下持错误答案，仅19.7%公开支持。保留正确答案者常不辩护；错误答案一旦被采纳会在良性智能体间传播；基线知识不预测鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 同伴互查并不能可靠防止个体错误；对抗影响多为私下且不可见，可扩散并削弱群体稳健性。需重新评估多智能体讨论的安全性假设。

中文摘要：LLM 系统越来越依赖智能体群体，它们彼此讨论、批评和检查，并假设同伴会捕捉到个体错误。我们用一个最小对抗者来检验这一假设。在一场由十个智能体参与的事实性讨论中，一个或三个对抗性智能体反复断言一个看似合理的错误答案。每一轮之后，我们通过一次其回答对同伴隐藏的单独调用，探测每个良性智能体的私下答案，并单独标注其对该主张的公开立场。在六个模型和三个事实性问答数据集上，没有攻击者时群体保持稳定：只有 0.6% 的良性智能体最终持错误答案。一个对抗性智能体将该比例提高到 44.8%，三个提高到 60.5%。关于从众的研究通常报告的是公开同意而没有私下改变；我们发现了相反情况。当三个对抗性智能体在场时，良性智能体在 51.6% 的暴露轮次中持有错误答案，但仅在 19.7% 中公开支持它。因此，大量影响在讨论中不可见，并且当智能体被问及自己的观点时，错误答案仍会持续。保留正确答案的智能体常常未能为其辩护；一旦被采纳，错误答案就会在良性智能体之间传播，而基线知识并不能预测鲁棒性。

Original abstract: LLM systems increasingly rely on groups of agents that discuss, critique, and check one another, assuming that peers will catch individual errors. We test this assumption against a minimal adversary. In a ten-agent factual discussion, one or three adversarial agents repeatedly assert a plausible false answer. After each round we probe each benign agent's private answer through a separate call whose response is hidden from its peers, and we separately label its public stance toward the claim. Across six models and three factual-QA datasets, groups remain stable without an attacker: only 0.6% of benign agents finish with the false answer. A single adversarial agent increases this rate to 44.8% and three to 60.5%. Studies of conformity typically report public agreement without private change; we find the reverse. With three adversarial agents present, benign agents hold the false answer in 51.6% of exposed turns but publicly endorse it in only 19.7%. Much of the influence therefore remains invisible in the discussion, and the false answers persist when agents are asked for their own views. Agents that retain the true answer often fail to defend it; once adopted, the false answer spreads among benign agents, and baseline knowledge does not predict robustness.

</details>


### [65] [Are You Sure Your LLM Is Sure? Repairing and Auditing Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22139)
*Arijit Dey,Bhartendu Kumar,Ramya Hebbalaguppe,Khuushi Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: main paper 10 pages; supplementary 44 pages; 5 figures

Selection reason: 将置信度修复、采纳与残差审计分离，并给出有限样本控制的采纳流程，可迁移到 agent 的自我评估、接受/重生成决策与可靠性审计。

TL;DR: RADAR 将置信度修复、修复采纳与残差审计分离，在平方损失下分解风险，提出 PAIC 修复、配对采纳和提示聚类核检验；实验表明更丰富预测器收益可能因标量化丢失，且配对投注显著提升采纳低测试风险候选的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量重校准只能修正分数本身可见的错误，无法区分置信度修复能力、是否应部署修复以及残留系统错误；因此需要将修复、采纳和审计分离的可靠性框架。

Method: 在平方损失下将预测风险分解为结果噪声、分数隐藏信息和映射误差，并指出保序修复会带来池化间隙；修复用 PAIC 在未标注目标提示上基于正确性探针学习单调映射；采纳用有限样本控制假改进的配对程序；审计用提示聚类核检验检测特征对齐残差并处理同提示依赖答案。

Result: 在多个 LLM 检查点、基准和分布偏移上，更丰富预测器可改善置信度估计，但投影回标量分数可能丢失增益，且更低预测风险不一定带来更好复核决策；在 47,400 次重放原始分数分配中，配对投注以 42.95% 采纳更低留出测试风险候选，单侧经验 Bernstein 为 15.09%。

Conclusion: RADAR 提供原则性工作流：修复标量分数可表达的部分，仅在有证据支持时采纳修复，并诊断重校准后遗留的系统性错误；代码和数据将在接收后发布。

中文摘要：置信度分数决定了一个 LLM 答案是被接受、复核还是重新生成，然而标量重校准只能纠正通过分数本身可见的错误。我们提出了 RADAR（Repair, Adopt, and Diagnose After Recalibration，即重校准后的修复、采纳与诊断），一个将置信度修复与决定是否部署该修复以及对残留错误进行审计分离开来的可靠性框架。在平方损失下，我们将预测风险分解为结果噪声、被分数隐藏的信息以及映射误差；将修复限制为保序映射还会额外引入一个池化间隙（pooling gap）。对于修复，我们提出 Probe-Anchored Isotonic Calibration（PAIC，探针锚定等渗校准），它从未标注目标提示上的正确性探针预测中学习一个单调映射。PAIC 的超额风险取决于探针的分数条件偏差和拟合误差，而非其总体准确率。对于采纳，我们推导了一个配对程序，可对虚假改进进行有限样本控制。对于审计，我们开发了一个提示聚类的核检验，用于检测与特征对齐的残差错误，同时考虑来自同一提示的依赖答案。在多个 LLM 检查点、基准和分布偏移上，更丰富的预测器可以改进置信度估计，但将其信息投影回标量分数可能会损失这些增益，而且更低的预测风险未必产生更好的复核决策。在 47,400 次重放的原始分数分配中，配对投注在 42.95% 的分配中采纳了在留出测试风险上更低的候选，而单侧经验 Bernstein 为 15.09%。因此，RADAR 提供了一个原则性工作流：修复标量分数所能表达的内容，仅在有证据支持时采纳修复，并诊断重校准后遗留的系统性错误。代码和数据将在接收后发布。

Original abstract: Confidence scores determine whether an LLM answer is accepted, reviewed, or regenerated, yet scalar recalibration can only correct errors visible through the score itself. We introduce RADAR (Repair, Adopt, and Diagnose After Recalibration), a reliability framework that separates confidence repair from deciding whether to deploy the repair and auditing the errors that remain. Under squared loss, we decompose prediction risk into outcome noise, information hidden by the score, and mapping error; restricting repair to order-preserving maps additionally introduces a pooling gap. For repair, we propose Probe-Anchored Isotonic Calibration (PAIC), which learns a monotone map from correctness-probe predictions on unlabeled target prompts. PAIC's excess risk depends on the probe's score-conditional bias and fitting error rather than its overall accuracy. For adoption, we derive a paired procedure with finite-sample control of false improvements. For auditing, we develop a prompt-clustered kernel test that detects feature-aligned residual errors while accounting for dependent answers from the same prompt. Across multiple LLM checkpoints, benchmarks, and distribution shifts, richer predictors can improve confidence estimation, but projecting their information back to a scalar score can lose these gains, and lower prediction risk need not yield better review decisions. Across 47,400 replayed raw-score allocations, paired betting adopts candidates with lower held-out test risk in 42.95% of allocations, versus 15.09% for one-sided empirical Bernstein. RADAR thus provides a principled workflow to repair what a scalar score can express, adopt a repair only when supported by evidence, and diagnose systematic errors that recalibration leaves behind. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [Realtime-Bench: Can Frontier Models Solve Real-time Interactive Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22155)
*Jingyu Tang,Xu Yang,Zhehao Zhang,Zheng Wang,Qi Cheng,Haozhe Zhao,Manling Li,Wenxuan Li,Xide Xia,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向实时交互的 agent 基准（Wall Clock/Paused Clock/Token Clock 分离），揭示时延与推理开销对策略成功的影响，可为 agent 评测与预算约束研究提供方法参考。

TL;DR: 提出 Realtime-Bench 评测实时交互，Wall Clock 最佳成功率仅 44%、Pass^3 仅 36%；通过 Paused Clock 和 Token Clock 分离服务延迟与流式能力，发现时间成本制约成功，推理努力存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时流式模型评估主要关注对话质量和流式感知，未检验严格时间约束下的有效行动，而实时交互要求智能体感知动态环境并及时行动。

Method: 构建 Realtime-Bench 动态任务集；设置 Wall Clock（按实际推理延迟推进）、Paused Clock（消除推理延迟的理想参考）和 Token Clock（按生成 token 数推进模拟时间），以分离服务延迟、流式能力和时间意识。

Result: Wall Clock 下最佳成功率仅 44%，最佳 Pass^3 仅 36%；时间成本限制动态环境中的成功；时间压力下更高推理努力效果混合，体现决策质量与及时执行的权衡。

Conclusion: 应改进实时多模态智能体，方向包括具有时间意识的自适应推理，以及连续的感知与行动。

中文摘要：实时交互要求智能体感知不断演变的环境并在时间约束下行动，然而现有对实时流式模型的评估聚焦于对话质量和流式感知，未能检验严格时间约束下的有效行动。我们提出 Realtime-Bench，一套精心策划的动态任务，其中延迟行动直接影响成功与否。在 Wall Clock 下，环境根据实际推理延迟推进，最佳成功率仅达到 44%，最佳 Pass^3 仅为 36%，表明实时交互仍然具有挑战性。为了将服务延迟与模型的实时流式能力区分开，我们将 Wall Clock 与 Paused Clock 进行比较，后者消除推理延迟作为理想化参考，并引入 Token Clock，其根据生成的 token 数量推进模拟时间，独立于物理服务速度来测试时间意识。我们发现，时间成本限制了在不断演变环境中的成功，并且在时间压力下，更高的推理努力具有混合效应，反映了决策质量与及时执行之间的权衡。这些发现为改进实时多模态智能体指明了方向，包括具有时间意识的自适应推理以及连续的感知与行动。

Original abstract: Real-time interaction requires agents to perceive evolving environments and act under temporal constraints, yet existing evaluations of real-time streaming models focus on conversational quality and streaming perception, leaving effective action under strict temporal constraints untested. We introduce Realtime-Bench, a suite of carefully curated dynamic tasks where delayed actions directly affect success. Under Wall Clock, where environments advance according to actual inference latency, the best success rate reaches only 44% and the best Pass^3 is only 36%, demonstrating that real-time interaction remains challenging. To separate serving latency from models' real-time streaming capability, we compare Wall Clock with Paused Clock, which removes inference delays as an idealized reference, and introduce Token Clock, which advances simulated time according to generated token counts, testing temporal awareness independently of physical serving speed. We find that temporal costs limit success in evolving environments and that higher reasoning effort has mixed effects under time pressure, reflecting a trade-off between decision quality and timely execution. These findings suggest directions for improving real-time multimodal agents, including adaptive reasoning with temporal awareness and continuous perception and action.

</details>


### [67] [Terminal-RSI: Recursive Self-Improving Terminal Agents via Recursive Self-Rewrite and Policy-Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22192)
*Junyao Yang,Zongxia Li,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Ruiwen Zhou,Haitao Mi,Shuicheng Yan,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 28 pages, 16 figures, 5 tables

Selection reason: 终端/代码 agent 的递归自改写与 critic 继承策略精炼，含 TB-GAE、族稠密奖励与成功优先奖励，直接对应 coding agent 的自动优化、轨迹复用与验证驱动训练。

TL;DR: Terminal-RSI 通过交替进行已验证轨迹递归自重写与继承评论家的策略精炼，结合 TB-GAE、FDR、SPR 等设计，在多个 Terminal-Bench 基准和 LHTB/SWR100 上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体需要把验证器反馈有效转化为策略更新，并将局部部分进展推进为完整任务解决。

Method: 提出 Terminal-RSI，交替 RSR 与继承评论家的 RPR；设计 TB-GAE、FDR、SPR；使用预热评论家模型、递归评论家交接和阶段特定学习率协调两阶段精炼；采用五阶段训练序列。

Result: 得到 Terminal-RSI-27B：Terminal-Bench 4.0 19.84%、2.1 86.52%、3.0 16.95%、Hard 79.00%；LHTB 过程奖励 0.21→0.37，严格完成率 0/46→5/46；SWR100 成功率 3.00%→6.00%。

Conclusion: 递归耦合已验证轨迹重写与继承评论家的策略精炼，可使每个保留阶段巩固已发现行为，并推动智能体从部分局部修复逐步走向严格终端验证。

中文摘要：将验证器结果转化为有用的策略更新，并将部分进展转化为完整任务解决，对于终端智能体至关重要。我们提出 Terminal-RSI，它在已验证轨迹的递归自重写（Recursive Self-Rewrite, RSR）与继承评论家的递归策略精炼（Recursive Policy-Refine, RPR）之间交替进行。我们提出轮次边界跳过观测的广义优势估计（Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE, TB-GAE），以保留 token 特定的基线，同时在交互轮次间施加衰减。家族密集奖励（Family Dense Reward, FDR）校准跨任务家族的语义进展；成功优先奖励（Success-Prioritized Reward, SPR）将严格成功与有界部分得分区分开。预热评论家模型、递归评论家交接以及阶段特定学习率协调这两个精炼阶段。从 Qwen3.8-27B 基础模型开始，经过 RSR-1、RPR-1、RSR-2 和 RPR-2 的五阶段序列产生了 Terminal-RSI-27B：在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上为 19.84%，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上为 86.52%，在 Terminal-Bench 3.0 上为 16.95%，在 Terminal-Bench Hard 上为 79.00%。在 LHTB 上，过程奖励从 0.21 升至 0.37，严格完成率从 0/46 升至 5/46；SWR100 成功率从 3.00% 翻倍至 6.00%。通过递归地将已验证轨迹重写与继承评论家的策略精炼耦合起来，每个保留阶段都巩固已发现的行为，并逐步推动智能体从部分局部修复走向严格终端验证。

Original abstract: Converting verifier outcomes into useful policy updates and partial progress into complete task resolution is crucial for terminal agents. We present Terminal-RSI, which alternates Recursive Self-Rewrite (RSR) of verified trajectories with critic-inheriting Recursive Policy-Refine (RPR). We formulate Turn-Boundary Skip-Observation GAE (TB-GAE) to retain token-specific baselines while applying decay across interaction turns. Family Dense Reward (FDR) calibrates semantic progress across task families; Success-Prioritized Reward (SPR) separates strict success from bounded partial credit. A Warm-up Critic Model, recursive critic handoff, and stage-specific learning rates coordinate the two refinement phases. The five-stage sequence from Qwen3.8-27B base model through RSR-1, RPR-1, RSR-2, and RPR-2 yields Terminal-RSI-27B: 19.84% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, 86.52% on Terminal-Bench 2.1, 16.95% on Terminal-Bench 3.0, and 79.00% on Terminal-Bench Hard. On LHTB, process reward rises from 0.21 to 0.37 and strict completion from 0/46 to 5/46; SWR100 success doubles from 3.00% to 6.00%. By recursively coupling verified trajectory rewriting with critic-inheriting policy refinement, each retained stage consolidates discovered behaviors and progressively advances the agent from partial local repairs to strict terminal verification.

</details>


### [68] [AutoSFT: An Agentic System for Automated Data Research in Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22194)
*Bingxiang He,Pengqi Sun,Shihao Peng,Shaoxuan Li,Huiyu Xiao,Hongjian Shi,Zixuan Fu,Ziqing Qiao,Yuxin Zuo,Cheng Qian,Xinqiang Yu,Jiayi Li,Xiusi Chen,Yizhen Song,Xiangyang Wang,Iofai Lei,Yudong Wang,Ning Ding,Zhiyuan Liu,Xiaozhi Wang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, 16 figures

Selection reason: 多 agent 自动做 SFT 数据研究，提出数据动作空间与元 agent 分离执行/分析/合成，其上下文管理与角色分工设计可迁移到自动化 agent 工作流。

TL;DR: AutoSFT 通过多智能体结构分离执行与分析、多角度提议并引入合成子智能体，覆盖多样 SFT 数据动作；相比仅调整比例的单智能体，在 IFEval、IFBench、MBPP+ 上均有提升，说明自动化 SFT 数据研究关键在于覆盖多样动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化后训练系统在完整训练栈下仅收敛到 SFT 并主要修改数据，且只提供选行或生成等单一控制；而人类数据工程师会调整比例、修剪、修复、替换、合成数据。需探究自动系统能否覆盖完整数据动作范围以及所需结构。

Method: 设计数据动作空间；先以单智能体运行该循环；提出 AutoSFT 多智能体系统：元智能体分离执行与分析，从独立角度收集提议，并调用合成子智能体覆盖数据池外能力；每次比较固定模型、训练器与预算，且每轮从基座模型重新训练以单独归因组件效果。

Result: 单智能体仅达到调整类别比例，上下文被执行输出填满并在搜索中途丢弃。AutoSFT 中执行器使上下文损失减半；分析子智能体提升最佳 IFEval +6.5；合成增加 +3.4 IFEval 与 +2.9 IFBench。迁移到代码时，分析相比无提升的单智能体增加 +3.7 MBPP+。

Conclusion: 自动化 SFT 数据研究更依赖覆盖多样数据动作，而非增强单一动作；AutoSFT 通过多智能体结构实现更全面的数据动作覆盖。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体日益自动化后训练，但当给定完整训练栈时，它们会收敛到监督微调，并将大部分精力用于修改训练数据。现有系统只赋予智能体一种控制方式，即从固定池中选择行或调用生成器，而人类数据工程师会调整比例、修剪、修复、替换和合成数据。我们为这些变化设计了一个数据动作空间，并探究自动化系统能否执行完整范围的动作而非仅其中一种，以及这需要何种结构。我们首先用单个智能体运行这一循环，它只达到一种动作，即调整类别比例，同时其上下文被执行输出填满并在搜索中途被丢弃。因此，我们引入 \textbf{AutoSFT}，一个多智能体系统，其中元智能体将执行与分析分离，从独立角度收集提议，并调用合成子智能体来处理数据池无法覆盖的能力，同时在每次比较中保持模型、训练器和预算固定。依次加入每个组件均有帮助：执行器将上下文损失减半，分析子智能体将最佳 IFEval 提升 $+6.5$，合成则增加 $+3.4$ IFEval 和 $+2.9$ IFBench。同一循环可迁移到代码，其中分析相比未提升的单智能体增加了 $+3.7$ MBPP+。每个结果都来自每轮从基座模型重新训练的多次运行，因此每个组件的效果都可单独归因。我们的发现表明，自动化 SFT 数据研究更多依赖于覆盖多样的数据动作，而非依赖于更强的单一动作。

Original abstract: Language model agents increasingly automate post-training, but when given the full training stack they converge on supervised fine-tuning and spend most effort revising training data. Existing systems give the agent one kind of control, selecting rows from a fixed pool or calling a generator, whereas a human data engineer adjusts proportions, prunes, repairs, replaces and synthesizes the data. We design a data action space for these changes and ask whether an automated system can make the full range rather than one of them, and what structure that requires. We first run this loop with a single agent, and it reaches only one action, adjusting category proportions, while its context fills with execution output and is discarded mid-search. We therefore introduce \textbf{AutoSFT}, a multi-agent system where a meta agent separates execution from analysis, gathers proposals from independent angles, and calls a synthesis subagent for capabilities the pool cannot cover, with the model, the trainer, and the budget fixed within each comparison. Adding each component in turn helps: the executor halves context loss, the analysis subagents lift the best IFEval by $+6.5$, and synthesis adds $+3.4$ IFEval and $+2.9$ IFBench. The same loop transfers to code, where analysis adds $+3.7$ MBPP+ over a single agent that does not improve. Every result comes from runs that retrain from the base model each round, so each component's effect is individually attributable. Our findings suggest that automating SFT data research depends more on covering diverse data actions than on a stronger single action.

</details>


### [69] [HTN-Orchestrate: A Hierarchical Task Network Framework for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22195)
*Yanchen Deng,Jianguo Huang,Yao Long Teng,Yixuan Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Liangcai Su,Xinyu Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于层次任务网络的两层多 agent 编排，延迟分解并支持失败后重建任务网，对 agent 规划、依赖建模与动态重规划有直接借鉴价值。

TL;DR: 提出 HTN-Orchestrate 两层框架，用分层任务网络分离全局规划与局部执行，在五个基准上以相同基础模型超过 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态调度灵活但缺少显式子任务层级与依赖建模，预定义工作流有结构但过早固定分解，二者难以兼顾局部执行与全局规划。

Method: 采用 HTN 两层架构：Planner 维护子任务层级与依赖并延迟分解；局部 Orchestrators 调度 worker 执行叶子子任务；结果按 AND 语义上传，必要时依据累积发现重建任务网络。

Result: 在覆盖网页研究、科学推理和研究工作流的五个基准上，HTN-Orchestrate 优于 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness；BrowseComp 准确率 89.73%，SUPERChem 准确率 73.44%，分别高出 Agent Team 2.45 和 4.69 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将全局任务规划与局部子任务执行通过 HTN 显式分离，可在保持执行灵活性的同时维护层级与依赖，并提升复杂多智能体任务的性能。

中文摘要：在复杂任务上编排多个智能体，需要协调局部子任务执行与全局任务规划。动态调度方法提供了执行灵活性，但没有显式建模子任务层级或依赖关系；而预定义工作流施加了这种结构，却在执行开始前就固定了任务分解。因此，我们引入 HTN-Orchestrate，一个通过分层任务网络（HTN）分离这些职责的两层框架。规划器（Planner）维护子任务层级和依赖关系，将分解推迟到前置条件结果可用之后；而局部编排器（Orchestrators）则调度工作者执行每个叶子子任务。结果在 \textbf{AND} 语义下向上传播，当当前分解无法解决任务时，累积的发现会为重建的任务网络提供信息。在涵盖网页研究、科学推理和研究工作流的五个基准上，HTN-Orchestrate 在相同基础模型下优于 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness，在 BrowseComp 上达到 89.73% 的准确率，在 SUPERChem 上达到 73.44%，分别超过 Agent Team 2.45 和 4.69 个百分点。

Original abstract: Orchestrating multiple agents on a complex task requires reconciling local subtask execution with global task planning. Dynamic dispatch approaches provide execution flexibility but do not explicitly model subtask hierarchies or dependencies, while predefined workflows impose this structure but fix the decomposition before execution begins. Therefore, we introduce HTN-Orchestrate, a two-layer framework that separates these responsibilities through a hierarchical task network (HTN). The Planner maintains subtask hierarchies and dependencies, deferring decomposition until prerequisite results become available, while local Orchestrators schedule workers to execute each leaf subtask. Results propagate upward under \textbf{AND} semantics, and accumulated findings inform a rebuilt task network when the current decomposition cannot resolve the task. On five benchmarks spanning web research, scientific reasoning, and research workflows, HTN-Orchestrate outperforms ReAct, Agent Team, and DeepSeek Harness using the same base model, achieving 89.73% accuracy on BrowseComp and 73.44% on SUPERChem, exceeding Agent Team by 2.45 and 4.69 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [70] [Shortcut Sensitivity in Agent-Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22243)
*Hasan Demirkiran,Jens Ernstberger*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 workshops TAE (Can We Trust AI Evaluation?) and AIWILD (Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond)

Selection reason: 提出三级捷径审计框架，揭示 agent 安全评测数据中的可预测线索会改变 guard 判定，对 agent 评测可靠性与 benchmark 设计有直接方法论价值。

TL;DR: 该文提出三级捷径审计框架，审计 LinuxArena 与两个 TS-Bench 步骤数据集，发现基准数据中的线索可预测标签并影响防护机制决策；在 AgentDojo-Traj 的配对干预中，改写 <INFORMATION> 使阻断率降低 8.33 个百分点，不透明工具别名使其提高 11.46 个百分点，且效应在扣除安慰剂后仍非零，但此类 Haiku 规模效应在 Luna 或确定性 Q8 TS-Guard 中未复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督 AI 智能体敏感工具调用的防护机制必须被可靠评估，但评估数据可能含有捷径线索，使标签在未测量目标安全构念时即可预测，并可能影响防护机制自身的决策，从而污染评估结论。

Method: 提出三级捷径审计框架，区分标签可预测性、观测性防护关联与配对决策敏感性；审计已完成的 LinuxArena 轨迹和两个 TS-Bench 衍生步骤数据集；并在冻结的 64 步 AgentDojo-Traj 队列上，以七配置筛选和重复、交错的 Haiku 调用测试最强的安全行标记关联。

Result: LinuxArena 中较短平均 shell 命令强烈预测轨迹标签；AgentDojo-Traj 中精确工具查找在域留出下 F_1 从 .901 降至 .495；AgentHarm-Traj 中 TS-Guard F_1 在行加权下为 .902，在相等交互加权下降至 .719，同时平衡准确率上升。配对干预中，重写 <INFORMATION> 使阻断率降低 8.33 个百分点，不透明工具别名使其提高 11.46 个百分点；所有不安全调用仍被阻断，变化涉及针对继承标签的假阳性；扣除格式安慰剂效应后两效应仍非零，探索性 95% 范围不含零；同样的编辑也改变测量准确率。这些 Haiku 规模效应在 Luna 或确定性 Q8 TS-Guard 中未复现。

Conclusion: 基准表示可以实质性改变防护机制的决策和测量评估性能；建议采用域组留出、评估单元指标以及安慰剂对照的配对干预来减少捷径线索对防护评估的干扰。

中文摘要：AI 智能体通过工具采取行动。有些行动是敏感的，因此监督这些行动的防护机制本身必须被可靠地评估。然而，用于评估防护机制的数据可能包含线索，这些线索使标签在没有测量预期安全构念的情况下变得可预测，而且这些线索也可能影响防护机制的决策。我们引入一个三级捷径审计框架，并审计已完成的 LinuxArena 轨迹以及两个源自 TS-Bench 的步骤数据集。我们区分标签可预测性、观测性防护关联和配对决策敏感性。在 LinuxArena 中，较短的平均 shell 命令能强烈预测轨迹标签。在 AgentDojo-Traj 中，精确工具查找在混合域折下从 F_1=.901 下降到域留出下的 .495。在 AgentHarm-Traj 中，发布的 TS-Guard F_1 在行加权下从 .902 下降到相等交互加权下的 .719，同时平衡准确率上升。然后，我们在一个冻结的 64 步 AgentDojo-Traj 队列上，使用重复、交错的 Haiku 调用，在七配置筛选中测试最强的安全行标记关联。重写 <INFORMATION> 标记使阻断率降低 8.33 个百分点，而不透明工具别名使其提高 11.46 个百分点。所有不安全调用仍被阻断；变化涉及针对继承标签的假阳性。在减去格式安慰剂效应后，两种效应仍非零，探索性 95% 范围不包含零。同样的编辑也改变了针对继承标签的测量准确率。这提供了直接证据，表明基准表示可以实质性改变防护机制的决策和测量评估性能。Haiku 规模大小的效应在 Luna 或确定性 Q8 TS-Guard 中未复现。我们建议使用域组留出、评估单元指标和安慰剂对照的配对干预。

Original abstract: AI agents take actions through tools. Some actions are sensitive, so guards that supervise them must themselves be evaluated reliably. Yet the data used to evaluate a guard can include cues that make labels predictable without measuring the intended safety construct, and those cues can also influence guard decisions. We introduce a three-level shortcut-auditing framework and audit completed LinuxArena trajectories and two TS-Bench-derived step datasets. We distinguish label predictability, observational guard association, and paired decision sensitivity. In LinuxArena, shorter average shell commands strongly predict trajectory labels. In AgentDojo-Traj, exact-tool lookup falls from F_1=.901 with mixed-domain folds to .495 under domain holdout. In AgentHarm-Traj, released TS-Guard F_1 falls from .902 under row weighting to .719 with equal interaction weighting, while balanced accuracy rises. We then test the strongest safe-row marker association in a seven-configuration screen on a frozen 64-step AgentDojo-Traj cohort using repeated, interleaved Haiku calls. Rewriting the <INFORMATION> marker lowers blocking by 8.33 percentage points, while opaque tool aliases raise it by 11.46 points. All unsafe calls remain blocked; the changes concern false positives against inherited labels. Both effects remain nonzero after subtracting the formatting-placebo effect, with exploratory 95% ranges excluding zero. The same edits also change measured accuracy against inherited labels. This provides direct evidence that benchmark representation can materially change a guard's decisions and measured evaluation performance. The Haiku-sized effects do not reproduce in Luna or deterministic Q8 TS-Guard. We recommend domain group holdouts, evaluation-unit metrics, and placebo-controlled paired interventions.

</details>


### [71] [Disagreement-Guided Weak-to-Strong Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22245)
*Sima Noorani,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出无需标注的弱到强路由信号（模型间分歧度），可用于 agent 中按不确定性决定是否升级到更强模型，与 agent 成本/可靠性控制相关。

TL;DR: 提出一种无需真值监督的弱到强路由方法，利用模型分歧近似最优路由策略，在智能体和单轮任务中权衡路由成本与响应准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备和机器人系统部署小模型受成本与延迟约束，但其能力有限；强模型可提供辅助，但何时路由通常依赖难以大规模获取的真值正确性信号，尤其在开放式和智能体任务中。

Method: 先刻画允许本地多次生成弱模型响应的最优弱到强路由策略；基于模型间比较推导有原则的近似，引入可直接从模型生成响应测量的模型分歧；强模型越接近真值分布，近似越准确。

Result: 在智能体和单轮任务中，无需真值监督即可有效权衡路由成本与响应准确性；即使有真值正确性信号用于训练，基于分歧的路由也可优于直接预测正确性的方法。

Conclusion: 模型分歧可作为弱到强路由的有效无监督信号，为资源受限场景提供更可靠的路由决策。

中文摘要：小型语言模型正越来越多地部署在边缘设备和机器人系统上，以满足成本与延迟约束，但其能力有限，可能使可靠部署面临挑战。更强的模型可以在需要时提供辅助，但决定何时将弱模型响应路由到更强模型，往往依赖响应正确性信号。然而，在开放式和智能体任务中，获取此类信号可能需要昂贵的真值监督，而这类监督难以大规模获得。我们重新审视弱到强路由的基础，并询问路由决策是否可以转而由不需要真值监督的信号来引导。我们首先刻画最优弱到强路由策略，同时允许通过本地推理生成多个弱模型响应。在此刻画的基础上，我们基于模型间比较推导出对最优路由的一种有原则的近似。特别地，我们引入一种模型分歧的概念，它可以直接从模型生成的响应中测量，而无需真值监督。重要的是，当强模型成为真值分布的更好代理时，该近似会变得越来越准确。在智能体和单轮任务中，我们的方法无需真值监督，就能有效权衡路由成本与响应准确性之间的取舍。此外，即使真值正确性信号可用于训练，基于分歧的路由也能优于直接训练以预测正确性的方法。

Original abstract: Small language models are increasingly deployed on edge devices and robotic systems to meet cost and latency constraints, yet their limited capabilities can make reliable deployment challenging. Stronger models can provide assistance when needed, but deciding when to route a weak-model response to a stronger model often relies on signals of response correctness. In open-ended and agentic tasks, however, obtaining such signals can require costly ground-truth supervision that is difficult to obtain at scale. We revisit the foundations of weak-to-strong routing and ask whether routing decisions can instead be guided by signals that do not require ground-truth supervision. We first characterize the optimal weak-to-strong routing policy, while allowing for multiple weak-model responses generated through local inference. Building on this characterization, we derive a principled approximation to the optimal routing based on model-to-model comparison. In particular, we introduce a notion of model disagreement that can be measured directly from model-generated responses, without ground-truth supervision. Importantly, the approximation becomes increasingly accurate as the strong model becomes a better proxy for the ground-truth distribution. Across agentic and single-turn tasks, our approach effectively navigates the trade-off between routing cost and response accuracy without ground-truth supervision. Moreover, even when ground-truth correctness signals are available for training, disagreement-based routing can outperform methods trained directly to predict correctness

</details>


### [72] [LEASH: Uncertainty-Aware Weak-to-Strong Escalation and Oversight for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22280)
*Qingni Wang,Changdae Oh,Yuzhe Yang,Sharon Li,Jiachen Yang,Ang Li,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向 computer-use agent 的弱到强升级与黑盒强模型持续监督，利用历史成功/失败轨迹的隐状态不确定性做步级升级，直接涉及 agent 可靠性与成本控制。

TL;DR: LEASH是一种免训练框架，利用弱智能体的潜在不确定性信号决定何时升级到强智能体，并在交接后通过影子观察器持续监督；在OSWorld上优于随机升级和单独弱智能体，并在匹配Claude Opus 5成功率时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，始终使用前沿模型成本高，小型开放权重模型单独运行易失败；弱到强升级需要廉价可靠的升级信号，且强智能体在黑盒API下难以监控。

Method: 提出LEASH：三层机制——初始任务不确定性用于早期路由、步级不确定性用于运行时升级、交接后影子观察器用于持续监督；核心是比较弱模型隐藏状态与历史成功/失败轨迹隐藏状态，以潜在不确定性驱动三层，并复用动作生成时的激活，无需额外前向传播或监控器训练。

Result: OSWorld跨四个模型骨干，LEASH持续优于单独弱智能体，匹配升级率下比随机升级高6.55–11.94个百分点；Qwen3.5-35B-A3B达到73.66%任务成功率、每任务0.4117美元，匹配始终开启Claude Opus 5的72.90%，推理成本降低19.2%。

Conclusion: 潜在不确定性可作为廉价有效的升级与监督信号；LEASH在保持黑盒强模型即插即用的同时，实现成本与成功率的良好折中。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体日益处理长时程任务，而在每一步都运行前沿模型代价高昂，仅靠小型开放权重模型又常常失败。弱到强升级提供了一种折中方案：默认由弱智能体行动，仅在需要时将控制权交给强智能体。核心挑战在于决定何时升级，这要求一种足够廉价、可在每一步检查的不确定性信号。然而，即便在交接之后，强智能体仍可能迷失方向，而强智能体通常通过的黑盒API并不暴露可供监控的内部信息。为此，我们提出LEASH，这是一个免训练框架，只有当弱智能体的潜在不确定性表明需要时，才释放强智能体，并在之后对其加以约束。LEASH包含三层：(1) 用于早期路由的初始任务不确定性，(2) 用于运行时升级的步级不确定性，以及交接后 (3) 用于对强黑盒智能体持续监督的影子观察器。在此，我们将潜在不确定性作为所有三层的燃料。具体而言，我们比较弱模型的隐藏状态与其过去成功和失败轨迹中的隐藏状态，若某状态看起来更像失败，则表明更高不确定性。在弱智能体执行期间，我们的不确定性估计器复用动作生成过程中已产生的激活，因此不需要额外前向传播或专门的监控器训练。在OSWorld上跨四个当代模型骨干的实验表明，LEASH持续优于单独弱智能体，并在匹配升级率下比随机升级高出6.55--11.94个百分点。使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B时，LEASH以每任务0.4117美元达到73.66%的任务成功率，匹配始终开启的Claude Opus 5（72.90%），同时将推理成本降低19.2%。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks, where running a frontier model at every step is expensive and a small open-weight model alone often fails. Weak-to-strong escalation offers a middle ground where a weak agent acts by default and hands control to a strong one only when needed. The core challenge is deciding when to escalate, which requires an uncertainty signal cheap enough to check at every step. However, even after handoff, the strong agent can still get lost, but a black-box API that the strong agent is usually served exposes no internals to monitor. To this end, we propose LEASH, a training-free framework that unleashes the strong agent only when the weak agent's latent uncertainty signals it is needed, and keeps it on a leash afterward. LEASH has three layers: (1) initial task uncertainty for early routing, (2) step-level uncertainty for runtime escalation, and, after handoff, (3) a shadow observer for continued oversight of the strong black-box agent. Here, we use latent uncertainty as a fuel for all three layers. Specifically, we compare the weak model's hidden states with those from its past successful and failed trajectories, and a state that looks more like the failures signals higher uncertainty. During weak-agent execution, our uncertainty estimator reuses activations already produced during action generation, so it needs no extra forward pass or dedicated monitor training. Experiments on OSWorld across four contemporary model backbones show that LEASH consistently improves over standalone weak agents and outperforms random escalation by 6.55--11.94 points at matched escalation rates. With Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, LEASH achieves 73.66% task success at $0.4117 per task, matching always-on Claude Opus 5 (72.90%) while reducing inference cost by 19.2%.

</details>


### [73] [Improving Environment-Agnostic Coding Agents: The Role of Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22289)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 37 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 直接研究环境无关 coding agent 的 scaffolding/harness 设计对 ARC、Terminal-Bench 上表现的影响，与 SemOpt 关注的 agent 脚手架与自动延续控制高度相关。

TL;DR: 研究通用智能体为何仍依赖开发者脚手架，以及仅靠环境无关改动能否提升性能；通过提示微调、专用参考对比和脚本化重置控制等实验，发现部分改进有效但伴随更高 token 成本，且提供的程序仍重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更强模型可能自行构建有用程序，但开发者提供的脚手架优势为何持续存在尚不清楚；同时需要检验环境无关智能体能否不依赖特定环境工程而改进。

Method: 在 ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze 和 Terminal-Bench 上，以两个几乎相同的提示变体评估基于 Codex 的智能体，保持代码、提示和通用工具不变；改变一条鼓励报告困难并交还控制权的指令，并与 ARC-AGI-3 专用参考比较；进一步将重置决策交给遵循既有约定的脚本化控制器，并开展补充的 AGI Maze 教程经验实验；同时提供 EnvTraverse 框架和评估基线。

Result: 指令改动提升了 ARC-AGI-3 和 AGI Maze 的平均分数，Terminal-Bench 变化小且混合；通用智能体仍低于 ARC-AGI-3 专用参考，即使使用 GPT-5.6-sol；脚本化重置恢复了部分专用优势；额外教程经验可能降低 AGI Maze 性能；性能提升伴随更高 token 成本，且无法区分更好利用信息/程序与额外或不同分配的推理。

Conclusion: 关键在于区分信息和工具是否可用与能否有效使用；对测试模型而言，提供的程序仍然重要。EnvTraverse 框架和基线智能体可用于检验进一步的环境无关改动是否能在某些环境中提升性能且不损害其他环境。

中文摘要：更强的语言模型可能日益为自身构建有用的程序，从而削弱开发者提供的脚手架的优势。我们研究这一优势为何持续存在，以及一个与环境无关的智能体能否在没有特定环境工程的情况下得到改进。我们在 ARC-AGI-3、AGI Maze 和 Terminal-Bench 上，以两个几乎相同的提示变体评估一个基于 Codex 的智能体。每个变体在不同环境中部署时保持代码、提示和通用工具不变，仅将环境特定信息作为输入接收。仅改变一条指令以鼓励报告困难并返回控制权以进行自动继续，就提高了 ARC-AGI-3 和 AGI Maze 上的平均分数，而 Terminal-Bench 则显示小幅、混合的变化。然而，该通用智能体仍低于 ARC-AGI-3 专用参考，即使使用 GPT-5.6-sol 也是如此。该参考规定的是组织与控制，而不是提供新的游戏规则或解决方案。为研究这一差距，我们将重置决策转移给一个脚本化控制器，该控制器遵循已给予智能体的约定。这一小改动恢复了专用智能体的一部分优势。一项补充的 AGI Maze 实验表明，额外的教程经验可能降低性能。总之，这些发现凸显了拥有可用信息和工具与有效使用它们之间的区别。对于此处测试的模型，提供的程序仍然重要。性能提升还伴随着更高的基于 token 的成本。这些实验没有将更好地利用信息和程序与额外或不同分配的推理区分开来。我们提供 EnvTraverse 框架和一个经过评估的基线智能体，用于测试进一步的与环境无关的改动是否能在某些环境中提高性能，同时不降低其他环境中的性能。

Original abstract: Stronger language models may increasingly construct useful procedures for themselves, reducing the advantage of developer-supplied scaffolding. We investigate why this advantage persists and whether an environment-agnostic agent can be improved without environment-specific engineering. We evaluate a Codex-based agent in two nearly identical prompt variants on ARC-AGI-3, AGI Maze, and Terminal-Bench. Each variant is deployed with unchanged code, prompts, and general-purpose tools across environments, receiving environment-specific information as input. Changing only an instruction to encourage reporting difficulties and returning control for automatic continuation improves mean scores on both ARC-AGI-3 and AGI Maze, while Terminal-Bench shows small, mixed changes. Yet the general agent remains below an ARC-AGI-3-specific reference, including with GPT-5.6-sol. The reference prescribes organization and control rather than supplying new game rules or solutions. To investigate this gap, we transfer reset decisions to a scripted controller that follows a convention already given to the agent. This small change recovers part of the specialized agent's advantage. A complementary AGI Maze experiment shows that additional tutorial experience can reduce performance. Together, these findings highlight the distinction between having information and tools available and using them effectively. Supplied procedures still matter for the models tested here. The performance gains also come with higher token-based cost. The experiments do not separate better use of information and procedures from additional or differently allocated inference. We provide the EnvTraverse framework and an evaluated baseline agent for testing whether further environment-independent changes improve performance in some environments without degrading it in others.

</details>


### [74] [EliteReplay: Preserving Diverse Solutions in RL Post-Training via Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22290)
*Hanane Nour Moussa,Doeun Lee,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过 quality-diversity 精英回放缓解 RL 后训练导致的解多样性收窄，可迁移到代码优化 agent 保持多样候选优化策略的训练思路。

TL;DR: EliteReplay将GRPO与质量-多样性搜索构建的精英解档案结合，通过回放损失进行后训练，在提升准确率的同时保留更多解多样性，并在文本到SQL任务和歧义问题上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能提升LLM在可验证任务上的准确率，但会导致行为锐化、解集收窄；已有方法常优化不能反映正确解多样性的指标。理想目标是让模型产生多个好解，而非仅一个可靠解。

Method: 提出EliteReplay后训练配方：训练前用冻结基础模型收集覆盖不同方法的解，构建基于质量-多样性搜索的精英档案；训练中将GRPO与该档案上的回放损失结合。

Result: 在多个文本到SQL任务上，EliteReplay的pass@128高于所有基线，不同正确策略比最强基线最多多77%；保留基础模型大部分正确策略，策略保留优于现有方法；在AmbiQT歧义问题上覆盖两种解释的频率约为最强基线的两倍。

Conclusion: EliteReplay能够在提高准确率的同时更好保留基础模型的解多样性，并在歧义问题上带来实际收益，说明兼顾质量与多样性的后训练具有应用价值。

中文摘要：强化学习已成为一种常见的后训练技术，用于提高LLM在可验证任务上的准确率。然而，此类训练可能会收窄模型所能产生的解集。已有若干方法试图应对这种行为锐化效应，但它们通常优化的指标并不能反映正确解的多样性。理想情况下，我们希望模型能够产生多个好的解，而不仅仅是一个可靠的解。我们提出EliteReplay，一种后训练配方，将GRPO与通过质量-多样性搜索构建的“精英”档案配对。训练前，我们使用冻结的基础模型收集覆盖不同方法的解；训练中，GRPO与该档案上的回放损失相结合。所得后训练模型在提高准确率的同时，保留了更多基础模型的解多样性。在多个文本到SQL任务上，EliteReplay取得了比所有基线更高的pass@128，产生的不同正确策略比最强基线最多多77%。它保留了基础模型的大部分正确策略，在策略保留方面优于现有方法。这种多样性在歧义问题上转化为实际收益：在AmbiQT上，EliteReplay覆盖两种解释的频率几乎是最高基线的两倍。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a common post-training technique to improve the accuracy of LLMs on verifiable tasks. However, such training can narrow the set of solutions the model can produce. Several approaches have attempted to address this behavior-sharpening effect, but they often optimize metrics that do not reflect the diversity of correct solutions. Ideally, we want a model that can produce multiple good solutions, not only a single reliable one. We propose EliteReplay, a post-training recipe that pairs GRPO with an "elite" archive built by quality-diversity search. Before training, we use the frozen base model to collect solutions that span distinct approaches; during training, GRPO is combined with a replay loss on this archive. The result is a post-trained model that improves accuracy while preserving more of the base model's solution diversity. On multiple text-to-SQL tasks, EliteReplay achieves a higher pass@128 than all baselines, producing up to 77% more distinct correct strategies than the strongest baselines. It preserves most of the base model's correct strategies, outperforming existing methods in strategy retention. This diversity translates into practical benefits on ambiguous questions, where EliteReplay covers both interpretations nearly twice as often as the strongest baseline on AmbiQT.

</details>


### [75] [A team of Gemini 4 Argon agents beats Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22319)
*Maxence Faldor,Satinder Singh,Roberta Raileanu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 四个 LLM agent 自主编写/调试数千个程序并跨日长程协作通关 Minecraft，展示多 agent 分工、共享记忆与长程程序化执行，对长程 coding agent 极具参考价值。

TL;DR: 四个 Gemini 4 Argon 智能体在无人类分配角色或编写游戏逻辑的情况下，通过 Mineflayer 在随机 Easy 生存世界中自主规划、编码与协作，历时 27 小时 14 分钟、编写 2,783 个程序、死亡 605 次后击败末影龙，首次仅用标准库实现 AI 端到端自主通关 Minecraft。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以端到端自主通关 Minecraft 为测试场景，检验 AI 智能体在长时程中保持连贯计划、进行长时程编码以及多智能体协调的能力；这些能力对复杂现实软件工程与企业任务同样关键。

Method: 将四个 Gemini 4 Argon 智能体置于沙盒化 Minecraft 生存世界。每个智能体用开源库 Mineflayer 控制一名玩家，通过游戏内聊天与共享内存协作。未分配角色、未编写游戏逻辑，所有程序由智能体自行规划、编写和调试。研究人员仅通过实时仪表盘观察并可在必要时暂停或停止，但未进行干预。运行从 Easy 难度随机生成世界开始，仅使用标准 Mineflayer 库，不使用预侦察种子或人类编写的游戏专用工具。

Result: 团队共编写 2,783 个程序，在 27 小时 14 分钟内击败末影龙，过程中死亡 605 次。据作者所知，这是 AI 智能体首次端到端自主完成 Minecraft，并满足仅用标准 Mineflayer 库、无预侦察种子或人类游戏专用工具的条件。获胜要求智能体在超过一天、数千个相互依赖步骤中保持连贯计划。

Conclusion: 该结果展示了 AI 智能体具备长时程编码与多智能体协调能力，可类比复杂现实软件工程和企业任务的需求。论文描述了实验设置、运行过程、团队策略（分工、分享所学、回收彼此物品）以及结果的局限。

中文摘要：我们将四个 Gemini 4 Argon 智能体放入一个沙盒化的 Minecraft 生存世界，并给它们一个目标：击败末影龙。每个智能体通过一个名为 Mineflayer 的开源库控制一个玩家，并通过游戏内聊天和共享内存与队友协调。没有人分配角色，也没有人编写游戏逻辑。智能体自行规划、编写和调试每一个程序。研究人员可以通过实时仪表盘观看本次运行，并随时暂停或停止它，但他们没有理由进行干预。团队从一个 Easy 难度的随机生成世界开始，编写了 2,783 个程序，并在 27 小时 14 分钟内击败了末影龙，沿途死亡 605 次。据我们所知，这是 AI 智能体首次端到端自主完成 Minecraft，且仅使用标准 Mineflayer 库，没有预先侦察的种子或由人类编写的其他游戏专用工具。获胜要求智能体在超过一天的时间里，在数千个相互依赖的步骤中保持连贯计划。复杂的现实世界软件工程和企业任务同样需要这种长时程编码和多智能体协调。我们描述了设置、运行如何展开、团队策略（分工、分享所学、回收彼此物品）以及该结果的局限。

Original abstract: We placed four Gemini 4 Argon agents in a sandboxed Minecraft survival world and gave them one goal: defeat the Ender Dragon. Each agent controlled a player via an open-source library called Mineflayer and coordinated with its teammates through in-game chat and shared memory. No one assigned roles or wrote game logic. The agents planned, wrote, and debugged every program themselves. Researchers could watch the run on a live dashboard and pause or stop it at any time, but they had no cause to intervene. Starting from a randomly generated world on Easy difficulty, the team wrote 2,783 programs and defeated the dragon in 27 hours and 14 minutes, dying 605 times along the way. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end autonomous completion of Minecraft by AI agents, achieved using only the standard Mineflayer library without pre-scouted seeds or other game-specific tools written by humans. Winning required the agents to keep a coherent plan across thousands of dependent steps for more than a day. Complex real-world software engineering and enterprise tasks demand the same long-horizon coding and multi-agent coordination. We describe the setup, how the run unfolded, the team's strategies (dividing the work, sharing what they learned, recovering each other's items), and the limits of the result.

</details>


### [76] [KnowGPO: Knowledge-Grouped Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22331)
*Siqi Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yuchen Yan,Xingyu Wu,Qipeng Chen,Xin Zhang,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/KnowGPO

Selection reason: 针对搜索 agent 提出以知识状态对齐轨迹的分组策略优化与过程奖励，属于通用 agent 训练/信用分配方法，可迁移到长程优化 agent 的中间奖励设计。

TL;DR: 提出 KnowGPO：以原子知识条件状态对齐轨迹，并用知识增量作为过程奖励；在 Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH、GAIA 上超越 GRPO、GiGPO、IGPO 3.8–4.2 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索中信用分配困难：结果奖励稀疏，现有过程监督依赖表层轨迹状态或模型内部信号，在异构观测和长而嘈杂的检索上下文下不可靠。

Method: 将每个查询所需信息分解为原子知识条件，以已满足条件子集表示每步轨迹状态；同一知识状态的轨迹分组比较，连续步间的增量知识增益作为环境基础的过程奖励。

Result: 在 Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH、GAIA 上，KnowGPO 比仅结果监督（GRPO）和过程监督基线（GiGPO、IGPO）高 3.8–4.2 分；8B 模型媲美或超过多数 32B 开源系统。

Conclusion: 基于任务相关知识状态的轨迹对齐与过程奖励可为深度搜索智能体提供更可靠的信用分配，并显著提升性能。

中文摘要：深度搜索智能体通过长时程、多轮交互解决知识密集型任务，其中将信用分配给单个搜索决策从根本上说是困难的。基于结果的强化学习仅提供在整个轨迹中共享的单一终端奖励。近期的过程监督方法旨在以更细粒度分配信用，但它们通常将信用锚定于表层轨迹状态或模型内部信号，而在深度搜索中，观测是异构的且检索到的上下文冗长且噪声大，这两者都变得不可靠。我们提出 KnowGPO，一种强化学习框架，它根据轨迹所获得的任务相关知识而非其表层观测来对齐轨迹。对于每个查询，KnowGPO 将推导答案所需的信息分解为原子知识条件，并通过轨迹在每一步已满足的条件子集来表示该轨迹的状态。共享相同知识状态的轨迹被分组以进行组内比较，而连续步骤之间的增量知识增益作为环境基础的过程奖励。在 Xbench、BrowseComp-ZH 和 GAIA 上的实验表明，KnowGPO 持续优于仅结果监督（GRPO）和过程监督基线（GiGPO、IGPO）3.8 到 4.2 个点，我们的 8B 模型可与大多数 32B 开源系统匹敌或超越。

Original abstract: Deep search agents solve knowledge-intensive tasks through long-horizon, multi-turn interactions, where assigning credit to individual search decisions is fundamentally difficult. Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides only a single terminal reward shared across an entire trajectory. Recent process-supervision methods aim to assign credit at finer granularity, but they typically anchor credit to either surface-level trajectory states or model-internal signals, both of which become unreliable in deep search where observations are heterogeneous and retrieved contexts are long and noisy. We propose KnowGPO, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns trajectories by the task-relevant knowledge they have acquired rather than by their surface observations. For each query, KnowGPO decomposes the information required to derive the answer into atomic knowledge conditions, and represents the state of a trajectory at each step by the subset of conditions it has satisfied. Trajectories sharing the same knowledge state are grouped for within-group comparison, and the incremental knowledge gain between consecutive steps serves as an environment-grounded process reward. Experiments on Xbench, BrowseComp-ZH, and GAIA show KnowGPO consistently outperforms outcome-only (GRPO) and process-supervision baselines (GiGPO, IGPO) by 3.8 to 4.2 points, with our 8B model rivaling or exceeding most 32B open-source systems.

</details>


### [77] [MLExpert: Towards a Generalist Agentic Model for Autonomous Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22344)
*Shangheng Du,Jinxin Shi,Shuaiyu Zhang,Yiming Wu,Junpeng Zhao,Shiyang Feng,Xin Li,Liang He,Xiangchao Yan,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Code: https://github.com/InternScience/MLExpert Model: https://huggingface.co/InternScience/MLExpert

Selection reason: 训练 35B 通用 agentic 模型自主进行长程机器学习工程，含交互 harness、窗口式交互 RL 与异步策略修正，并在 agent scaffold 优化上验证泛化，与自动代码/性能优化 agent 高度契合。

TL;DR: 提出面向长时程MLE的后训练方法及WIRL，训练35B MoE智能体MLExpert，在MLE-Bench Lite上将奖牌率从37.9%提升至72.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM向AI4AI/RSI发展；长时程MLE实验反馈耦合多个前置决策且成本高，训练需从长交互轨迹中选取有用监督并利用中间结果学习。

Method: 构建Kaggle-MLE Factory与交互执行框架；分层智能体SFT迁移通用工具使用和编码能力；WIRL将相邻实验结果间的多轮决策作为执行窗口并赋予共享结果奖励；异步收集与策略校正支持轨迹结束前更新策略。

Result: MLExpert（35B MoE）在MLE-Bench Lite上将基础模型平均奖牌率从37.9%提升至72.7%，与Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3等更大前沿模型相当；在NatureBench Lite、智能体脚手架优化和算法优化上也提升。

Conclusion: 学习到的优化能力可泛化到其他任务，表明该后训练方法推进自主长时程MLE优化与AI4AI。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）正从辅助科学研究与工程，扩展到自主开发和改进AI系统，推动“AI for AI”（AI4AI）作为通向递归自我改进（RSI）的路径。工作流驱动的机器学习工程（MLE）系统通过管理长时程实验的复杂编排，为这一追求提供了切实可行的路径；而近期的MLE系统采用模型主导的交互，使模型能够自主组织实验，并基于执行反馈调整优化策略。然而，每次实验的反馈都反映了若干先前决策，而一次长时程MLE运行包含许多此类高成本实验。因此，训练必须从长交互轨迹中选择有用的监督信号，并从中间结果中学习。我们提出一种面向自主长时程MLE优化的后训练方法，并将其用于训练MLExpert，一个35B专家混合（Mixture-of-Experts）智能体模型。我们设计了Kaggle-MLE Factory来构建可执行任务，以及用于模型主导实验的交互执行框架。分层智能体SFT通过结果引导的监督，将通用工具使用和编码能力迁移到MLE。窗口化交互强化学习（WIRL）将相邻实验结果之间的多轮决策视为一个执行窗口，并为其分配共享的基于结果的奖励。异步收集与策略校正支持在轨迹结束前进行策略更新。在MLE-Bench Lite上，MLExpert将基础模型的平均奖牌率从37.9%提升到72.7%，达到与Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3等更大前沿模型相当的性能。进一步实验表明，MLExpert在NatureBench Lite、智能体脚手架优化和算法优化上均提升了性能，表明所学到的优化能力可泛化到其他任务。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are expanding from assisting scientific research and engineering to autonomously developing and improving AI systems, advancing AI for AI (AI4AI) as a pathway toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Workflow-driven machine learning engineering (MLE) systems provide a practical path toward this pursuit through complex orchestration that manages long-horizon experimentation, while recent MLE systems adopt model-directed interaction, allowing models to autonomously organize experiments and adapt optimization strategies based on execution feedback. However, feedback from each experiment reflects several preceding decisions, and a long MLE run contains many such costly experiments. Training must therefore select useful supervision from long interaction trajectories and learn from intermediate outcomes. We propose a post-training method for autonomous long-horizon MLE optimization and apply it to train MLExpert, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts agentic model. We design the Kaggle-MLE Factory to construct executable tasks and an interaction harness for model-directed experimentation. Hierarchical agentic SFT transfers general tool-use and coding capabilities to MLE through outcome-guided supervision. Windowed Interaction RL (WIRL) treats the multi-turn decisions between successive experiment results as an execution window and assigns it a shared outcome-based reward. Asynchronous collection and policy correction support policy updates before the trajectory ends. On MLE-Bench Lite, MLExpert improves the average medal rate of the base model from 37.9% to 72.7%, achieving performance comparable to larger frontier models such as Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3. Further experiments show that MLExpert improves performance on NatureBench Lite, agent scaffold optimization, and algorithm optimization, suggesting that the learned optimization capability generalizes to other tasks.

</details>
